查我的 MineSim 项目总体路线为“MineSim 复现 → 蒙特卡洛/MCTS 接入 → 单车跑通 → 寻找双车冲突场景 → 接入新地图 → 神经网络接入”。处理任务时必须先完整阅读我提供的全部文档和现有证据，所有判断严格基于已验证事实，不猜测、不重复审计已确认的地图、代码链路、参数和实验结果；先按优先级识别当前阻塞、下一步最小行动和当天可完成的交付物，并明确每一步 PASS 标准。执行上优先省钱：通常由 ChatGPT 负责分析、决策和给出代码，我在云端终端执行后粘贴结果；只有复杂算法设计、顽固 Bug、关键结论复核或明显适合云端自主执行的麻烦任务才交给 Codex，Codex 默认使用 gpt-5.6-luna medium，必要时才临时升级 Terra/Sol，每次只完成当前一个最小步骤，只返回 PASS/FAIL 和必要字段，不写长报告、不重复已知事实。能用 Shell/Python 免费确认的文件、进程、哈希、Git 状态等不要调用模型；长时间仿真由普通终端运行并写进度文件，不让 Codex 长时间等待。工作流程坚持“只读 preflight → 单步 smoke → 短闭环 → 完整实验”，失败只定位当前故障，不做无意义全仓扫描；大文件、日志、临时脚本和结果统一放 /root/autodl-tmp，正式仓库只保留必要源码修改。高资源卡按量计费，未开卡时只有约 2G CPU、无 GPU，因此必须先把能免费完成的前置工作全部做完，确实需要计算资源时再明确告诉我开卡；每次对话都要告诉我当前任务需要高资源卡还是低资源卡。开卡配置为 PyTorch 2.1.0、Python 3.10/Ubuntu 22.04、CUDA 12.1、RTX 4090D 24GB×1、15 vCPU、80GB 内存、30GB 系统盘和 50GB 免费数据盘，费用约 1.88–1.98 元/小时。以后给我的每段可执行代码都必须先进入 /root/MineSim-Dynamic、激活 minesim 环境并设置必要环境变量；交互终端顶层绝不使用裸 exit，需要失败终止时放进子 shell (...) 或只打印 FAIL，避免 SSH 被退出；除非我主动要求详细解释，否则回答以简短说明 + 当前一步代码为主，同时始终优先保证项目安全、可复现和结果质量。为尽量降低复制粘贴、长代码和长日志带来的对话负担，MineSim 后续统一采用“**文件优先、聊天只传任务和结论**”的交互方式：几百行以上的源码、Shell、JSON、CSV、日志和实验结果原则上不再整段粘贴到聊天中，直接上传原文件即可；每次任务都审查代码，短代码直接生成，长代码再采用文件。一次任务只提供当前步骤真正相关的少量关键文件，通常控制在 3–6 个，我按需读取具体函数、行段和结果，不要求重复发送已经确认的上下文。需要我生成较长的新脚本或修改核心旧文件时，优先提供可直接使用的 .py、.sh 或 .patch 文件，聊天中只保留文件用途、执行命令和 PASS 标准；小修改仍优先 unified diff，避免重写整个旧文件。AutoDL 产生的大量日志、JSON、CSV、图片、视频、实验目录或多个关联结果，可以统一保存在 /root/autodl-tmp，完成当前步骤后直接打成 ZIP/7z 压缩包上传给我，我负责在压缩包中核对文件、SHA、manifest、日志和结果，不需要你逐项复制终端内容；聊天里通常只需告诉我“这是哪一步的结果包、执行是否结束、有没有明显 PASS/FAIL”。终端即时反馈也尽量只粘贴短小的关键字段、错误末端或我明确要求的输出，完整 traceback/长日志则保存成文件上传。所有判断仍以真实云端源码、Git、SHA、运行结果和上传 artifact 为准，已经验证和冻结的证据不重复审计；整个流程保持“我给最小步骤或文件 → 你在云端执行 → 结果直接保存/压缩 → 上传给我 → 我只分析当前 gate → 再给下一步”，以最大程度减少手工操作、对话长度和重复劳动，同时保持安全、可复现和可追溯。

我们刚刚准备的下一门静态检查，是把 C03 的 12 个 feature 精确绑定到嵌套 JSON path，同时全 /root/autodl-tmp 搜索历史 C13 真正使用过的 inference harness。脚本已经设计出来，但从目前上传记录看，还没有看到这段脚本的真实执行输出；因此不能把它写成已 PASS。

所以当前唯一下一步是：

→ synthetic checkpoint-compatible forward

当前资源：低资源，不开高卡。 这一步只审计真正的历史 C13 forward harness，不执行模型。

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic
export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""

(
set -eu

ROOT=/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1
HARNESS=/root/autodl-tmp/RUN_C13_ONE_TIME_INDEPENDENT_VALUE_V2_EVAL_V1.sh
FAIL_LOCK="$ROOT/c13_value_v2_full_collection_retry1/C13_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_TECHNICAL_FAILURE_LOCK_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_HARNESS_SHA=f8112e1e6bf77805b3c5141a03a26ae273051cade96762899dc21194503cfab4
EXPECTED_FAIL_LOCK_SHA=50c072076805e983e23dc14150bd70574dfb5b1c964f718d783a53fea806e538

echo "=== PREFLIGHT ==="

HEAD="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
echo "HEAD=$HEAD"

[ "$HEAD" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"
[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

[ -f "$HARNESS" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HARNESS_MISSING=$HARNESS"
    false
}

[ -f "$FAIL_LOCK" ] || {
    echo "FAIL FAILURE_LOCK_MISSING=$FAIL_LOCK"
    false
}

HARNESS_SHA="$(sha256sum "$HARNESS" | awk '{print $1}')"
FAIL_LOCK_SHA="$(sha256sum "$FAIL_LOCK" | awk '{print $1}')"

echo "HARNESS_SHA256=$HARNESS_SHA"
echo "FAIL_LOCK_SHA256=$FAIL_LOCK_SHA"

[ "$HARNESS_SHA" = "$EXPECTED_HARNESS_SHA" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HARNESS_SHA"
    false
}

[ "$FAIL_LOCK_SHA" = "$EXPECTED_FAIL_LOCK_SHA" ] || {
    echo "FAIL FAILURE_LOCK_SHA"
    false
}

echo "IMMUTABLE_EVIDENCE_GATE=PASS"

python - "$HARNESS" "$FAIL_LOCK" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import re

harness_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
fail_p = Path(sys.argv[2])

text = harness_p.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8",
    errors="ignore",
)
lines = text.splitlines()

fail = json.loads(
    fail_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)

HARNESS_SHA = hashlib.sha256(
    harness_p.read_bytes()
).hexdigest()

print()
print("=== HISTORICAL C13 HARNESS STATIC AUDIT ===")
print("HARNESS =", harness_p)
print("HARNESS_SHA256 =", HARNESS_SHA)

checks = {
    "checkpoint_name":
        "paper1_value_v2_final_dev_pre_c13.pt",

    "checkpoint_sha":
        "ce32cb7943a05d62ea16aee95a86f26bab7521af1d345e39f5c8a410a02ffb13",

    "normalization_name":
        "paper1_value_v2_final_dev_normalization.npz",

    "model_class":
        "ActionConditionedValueV2",

    "torch_load":
        "torch.load",

    "load_state_dict":
        "load_state_dict",

    "model_eval":
        ".eval()",

    "no_grad":
        "no_grad",

    "ranking_metrics":
        "ranking_metrics",

    "prediction_shape_115_16":
        "(115, 16)",
}

for name, token in checks.items():
    print(
        f"{name.upper()}_PRESENT="
        + ("PASS" if token in text else "FAIL")
    )


print()
print("=== CRITICAL SOURCE LOCATIONS ===")

patterns = [
    "paper1_value_v2_final_dev_pre_c13.pt",
    "paper1_value_v2_final_dev_normalization.npz",
    "ActionConditionedValueV2",
    "torch.load",
    "load_state_dict",
    ".eval()",
    "no_grad",
    "ranking_metrics",
    "pred",
    "prediction",
]

hits = []

for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
    if any(p in line for p in patterns):
        hits.append(i)

merged = []

for line_no in hits:
    start = max(1, line_no - 4)
    end = min(len(lines), line_no + 7)

    if merged and start <= merged[-1][1] + 1:
        merged[-1] = (
            merged[-1][0],
            max(merged[-1][1], end),
        )
    else:
        merged.append((start, end))

for start, end in merged:
    print()
    print(f"--- LINES {start}-{end} ---")
    for i in range(start, end + 1):
        print(f"{i}: {lines[i - 1]}")


print()
print("=== FAILURE LOCK BINDING ===")

def walk(obj, path=""):
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        for k, v in obj.items():
            p = f"{path}.{k}" if path else k
            yield from walk(v, p)

    elif isinstance(obj, list):
        for i, v in enumerate(obj):
            yield from walk(
                v,
                f"{path}[{i}]",
            )

    else:
        yield path, obj


bindings = []

for path, value in walk(fail):
    rendered = str(value)

    if (
        HARNESS_SHA in rendered
        or harness_p.name in rendered
        or "forward" in path.lower()
        or "prediction" in path.lower()
        or "metric" in path.lower()
        or "harness" in path.lower()
        or "runner" in path.lower()
    ):
        bindings.append(
            (path, value)
        )

for path, value in bindings:
    print(
        f"{path} = {value}"
    )

print(
    "FAILURE_LOCK_REFERENCES_HARNESS_SHA="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if any(
            HARNESS_SHA in str(v)
            for _, v in walk(fail)
        )
        else "FAIL"
    )
)

print(
    "FAILURE_LOCK_REFERENCES_HARNESS_NAME="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if any(
            harness_p.name in str(v)
            for _, v in walk(fail)
        )
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


print()
print("=== METRIC-BINDING FAILURE SEARCH ===")

failure_tokens = [
    "takes 0 positional arguments",
    "3 were given",
    "ranking_metrics",
    "main()",
    "prediction_shape",
]

for token in failure_tokens:
    found = (
        token in text
        or token in json.dumps(
            fail,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
    )

    print(
        f"{token}="
        + ("FOUND" if found else "ABSENT")
    )


print()
print("=== STATIC CONTRACT CLASSIFICATION ===")

required = [
    "paper1_value_v2_final_dev_pre_c13.pt",
    "ActionConditionedValueV2",
    "torch.load",
    "load_state_dict",
    ".eval()",
    "no_grad",
]

contract_pass = all(
    token in text
    for token in required
)

failure_bound = any(
    HARNESS_SHA in str(v)
    for _, v in walk(fail)
)

if contract_pass and failure_bound:
    status = "PROVEN_HISTORICAL_C13_FORWARD_HARNESS"
else:
    status = "HOLD_REQUIRES_MORE_EVIDENCE"

print(
    "INFERENCE_CHAIN_STATIC_CONTRACT="
    + ("PASS" if contract_pass else "FAIL")
)

print(
    "HISTORICAL_EXECUTION_BINDING="
    + ("PASS" if failure_bound else "FAIL")
)

print(
    "C13_HARNESS_CLASSIFICATION="
    + status
)

print("CHECKPOINT_LOADED_THIS_STEP=False")
print("MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED_THIS_STEP=False")
print("C03_PREDICTION_COMPUTED=False")
print("C03_METRICS_COMPUTED=False")
print("VALUE_V2_EVALUATION_AUTHORIZED=False")
PY

echo
echo "=== FINAL ==="
echo "AUDIT_ONLY=PASS"
echo "NEXT=LOCK_EXACT_CHECKPOINT_LOAD_FORWARD_CHAIN"
)

这一步结果是：inference chain 静态合同已经 PASS，但“这个 shell 就是当时实际执行 forward 的那个 harness”的历史绑定还没闭合。Failure lock 已证明 model_forward_call_count=1、预测形状 [115,16]，但没有直接记录 harness 的 SHA/文件名。

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic
export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""

(
set -eu

BASE=/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1
HARNESS=/root/autodl-tmp/RUN_C13_ONE_TIME_INDEPENDENT_VALUE_V2_EVAL_V1.sh
FAIL_LOCK="$BASE/c13_value_v2_full_collection_retry1/C13_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_TECHNICAL_FAILURE_LOCK_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_HARNESS_SHA=f8112e1e6bf77805b3c5141a03a26ae273051cade96762899dc21194503cfab4
EXPECTED_FAIL_SHA=50c072076805e983e23dc14150bd70574dfb5b1c964f718d783a53fea806e538

echo "=== PREFLIGHT ==="

HEAD="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
echo "HEAD=$HEAD"

[ "$HEAD" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

HARNESS_SHA="$(sha256sum "$HARNESS" | awk '{print $1}')"
FAIL_SHA="$(sha256sum "$FAIL_LOCK" | awk '{print $1}')"

echo "HARNESS_SHA=$HARNESS_SHA"
echo "FAIL_LOCK_SHA=$FAIL_SHA"

[ "$HARNESS_SHA" = "$EXPECTED_HARNESS_SHA" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HARNESS_SHA"
    false
}

[ "$FAIL_SHA" = "$EXPECTED_FAIL_SHA" ] || {
    echo "FAIL FAILURE_LOCK_SHA"
    false
}

echo "PREFLIGHT=PASS"

python - "$BASE" "$HARNESS" "$FAIL_LOCK" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib

base = Path(sys.argv[1])
harness = Path(sys.argv[2])
failure_path = Path(sys.argv[3])

HARNESS_SHA = hashlib.sha256(
    harness.read_bytes()
).hexdigest()

failure = json.loads(
    failure_path.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def walk(obj, path=""):
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        for k, v in obj.items():
            p = f"{path}.{k}" if path else k
            yield from walk(v, p)

    elif isinstance(obj, list):
        for i, v in enumerate(obj):
            yield from walk(
                v,
                f"{path}[{i}]"
            )

    else:
        yield path, obj


# ==========================================================
# 1. Locate historical C13 START locks
# ==========================================================

start_hits = []

for p in base.rglob("*.json"):
    n = p.name.upper()

    if (
        "C13" in n
        and (
            "START" in n
            or "STARTED" in n
        )
    ):
        start_hits.append(p)

start_hits = sorted(
    set(start_hits)
)

print()
print("=== C13 START LOCKS ===")
print("START_LOCK_COUNT=", len(start_hits))

for p in start_hits:
    print()
    print("START_PATH=", p)
    print("START_SHA256=", sha(p))

    try:
        obj = json.loads(
            p.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )
        print(
            json.dumps(
                obj,
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
                ensure_ascii=False,
            )
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print(
            "START_PARSE_ERROR=",
            repr(e),
        )


# ==========================================================
# 2. Locate C13 preauthorization / execution locks
# ==========================================================

preauth_hits = []

for p in base.rglob("*.json"):
    n = p.name.upper()

    if (
        "C13" in n
        and (
            "PREAUTH" in n
            or "AUTHORIZATION" in n
        )
    ):
        preauth_hits.append(p)

preauth_hits = sorted(
    set(preauth_hits)
)

print()
print("=== C13 PREAUTH LOCKS ===")
print("PREAUTH_LOCK_COUNT=", len(preauth_hits))

keywords = (
    "sha",
    "runner",
    "harness",
    "script",
    "command",
    "checkpoint",
    "normalization",
    "start",
    "evaluation",
    "forward",
    "prediction",
)

for p in preauth_hits:
    print()
    print("PREAUTH_PATH=", p)
    print("PREAUTH_SHA256=", sha(p))

    try:
        obj = json.loads(
            p.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )
    except Exception:
        continue

    for k, v in walk(obj):
        if any(
            word in k.lower()
            for word in keywords
        ):
            print(
                f"{k} = {v}"
            )


# ==========================================================
# 3. Inspect failure lock binding fields
# ==========================================================

print()
print("=== FAILURE LOCK BINDINGS ===")

failure_fields = []

for k, v in walk(failure):
    if any(
        word in k.lower()
        for word in keywords
    ):
        failure_fields.append(
            (k, v)
        )
        print(
            f"{k} = {v}"
        )


# ==========================================================
# 4. Does failure lock bind a START lock?
# ==========================================================

print()
print("=== START -> FAILURE CHAIN ===")

start_binding_count = 0

failure_text = json.dumps(
    failure,
    sort_keys=True,
    ensure_ascii=False,
)

for p in start_hits:
    s = sha(p)

    sha_bound = (
        s in failure_text
    )

    path_bound = (
        str(p) in failure_text
        or p.name in failure_text
    )

    print(
        f"START={p.name}"
    )
    print(
        "FAILURE_REFERENCES_START_SHA="
        + ("PASS" if sha_bound else "FAIL")
    )
    print(
        "FAILURE_REFERENCES_START_PATH="
        + ("PASS" if path_bound else "FAIL")
    )

    if sha_bound or path_bound:
        start_binding_count += 1

print(
    "START_FAILURE_BINDING_COUNT=",
    start_binding_count,
)


# ==========================================================
# 5. Does START lock bind this exact harness?
# ==========================================================

print()
print("=== HARNESS -> START CHAIN ===")

harness_start_binding_count = 0

for p in start_hits:
    text = p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="ignore",
    )

    sha_bound = (
        HARNESS_SHA in text
    )

    name_bound = (
        harness.name in text
    )

    print(
        f"START={p.name}"
    )
    print(
        "START_REFERENCES_HARNESS_SHA="
        + ("PASS" if sha_bound else "FAIL")
    )
    print(
        "START_REFERENCES_HARNESS_NAME="
        + ("PASS" if name_bound else "FAIL")
    )

    if sha_bound or name_bound:
        harness_start_binding_count += 1

print(
    "HARNESS_START_BINDING_COUNT=",
    harness_start_binding_count,
)


# ==========================================================
# 6. Inspect harness source around START creation
# ==========================================================

print()
print("=== HARNESS START-WRITE SOURCE ===")

lines = harness.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8",
    errors="ignore",
).splitlines()

tokens = (
    "START=",
    "start_path",
    "STARTED",
    "PREAUTH",
    "preauth",
    "write_text",
    "json.dump",
    "json.dumps",
    "sha(start",
    "sha256",
)

line_hits = []

for i, line in enumerate(
    lines,
    1,
):
    if any(
        token in line
        for token in tokens
    ):
        line_hits.append(i)

ranges = []

for n in line_hits:
    a = max(1, n - 5)
    b = min(
        len(lines),
        n + 8,
    )

    if (
        ranges
        and a <= ranges[-1][1] + 2
    ):
        ranges[-1] = (
            ranges[-1][0],
            max(
                ranges[-1][1],
                b,
            ),
        )
    else:
        ranges.append(
            (a, b)
        )

for a, b in ranges[:12]:
    print()
    print(
        f"--- LINES {a}-{b} ---"
    )

    for i in range(a, b + 1):
        print(
            f"{i}: {lines[i - 1]}"
        )


# ==========================================================
# 7. Direct historical search for exact harness identity
#    Exclude current C03 evaluator-audit workspace.
# ==========================================================

print()
print("=== EXACT HISTORICAL HARNESS IDENTITY SEARCH ===")

identity_hits = []

extensions = {
    ".json",
    ".txt",
    ".log",
    ".md",
    ".sh",
}

for p in base.rglob("*"):
    if not p.is_file():
        continue

    if p.suffix.lower() not in extensions:
        continue

    rel = str(
        p.relative_to(base)
    ).lower()

    # Exclude current C03 audit products to avoid
    # self-generated false evidence.
    if (
        "c03_value_v2_one_time_evaluation_v1"
        in rel
    ):
        continue

    if "c13" not in rel:
        continue

    try:
        if p.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
            continue

        text = p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="ignore",
        )

    except Exception:
        continue

    terms = []

    if HARNESS_SHA in text:
        terms.append(
            "HARNESS_SHA"
        )

    if harness.name in text:
        terms.append(
            "HARNESS_NAME"
        )

    if terms:
        identity_hits.append(
            (
                p,
                sha(p),
                terms,
            )
        )

print(
    "DIRECT_HISTORICAL_IDENTITY_HIT_COUNT=",
    len(identity_hits),
)

for p, s, terms in identity_hits:
    print(
        "IDENTITY_FILE=",
        p,
    )
    print(
        "IDENTITY_FILE_SHA=",
        s,
    )
    print(
        "IDENTITY_TERMS=",
        terms,
    )


# ==========================================================
# 8. Final classification
# ==========================================================

print()
print("=== FINAL CLASSIFICATION ===")

failure_forward = (
    failure.get(
        "model_forward_executed"
    ) is True
)

forward_count = failure.get(
    "model_forward_call_count"
)

prediction_shape = failure.get(
    "prediction_shape"
)

forward_evidence = (
    failure_forward
    and forward_count == 1
    and prediction_shape == [115, 16]
)

print(
    "FORWARD_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if forward_evidence
        else "FAIL"
    )
)

print(
    "START_FAILURE_CHAIN="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if start_binding_count > 0
        else "HOLD"
    )
)

print(
    "HARNESS_START_CHAIN="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if harness_start_binding_count > 0
        else "HOLD"
    )
)

print(
    "DIRECT_HISTORICAL_HARNESS_IDENTITY="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if identity_hits
        else "HOLD"
    )
)

if (
    forward_evidence
    and start_binding_count > 0
    and (
        harness_start_binding_count > 0
        or identity_hits
    )
):
    status = (
        "PROVEN_EXACT_HISTORICAL_C13_HARNESS"
    )
else:
    status = (
        "HOLD_IDENTITY_CHAIN_NOT_YET_COMPLETE"
    )

print(
    "C13_HARNESS_BINDING_STATUS="
    + status
)

print("CHECKPOINT_LOADED_THIS_STEP=False")
print("MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED_THIS_STEP=False")
print("C03_PREDICTION_COMPUTED=False")
print("C03_METRICS_COMPUTED=False")
print("VALUE_V2_EVALUATION_AUTHORIZED=False")
print("NEXT=DECIDE_EXACT_INFERENCE_CHAIN_LOCK")

conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic
export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""

(
set -eu

BASE=/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1
EVAL="$BASE/c03_value_v2_one_time_evaluation_v1"

HARNESS=/root/autodl-tmp/RUN_C13_ONE_TIME_INDEPENDENT_VALUE_V2_EVAL_V1.sh
PARENT=/root/autodl-tmp/paper1_value_v2_final_dev_prep_v1/authoritative_v2_loso_parent.py

MODEL=/root/autodl-tmp/paper1_value_v2_final_dev_model_v1
CKPT="$MODEL/paper1_value_v2_final_dev_pre_c13.pt"
NORM="$MODEL/paper1_value_v2_final_dev_normalization.npz"
MODEL_FREEZE="$MODEL/PAPER1_VALUE_V2_FINAL_DEV_MODEL_FREEZE_V1.json"

START="$BASE/c13_value_v2_full_collection_retry1/C13_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_STARTED_V1.json"
FAIL_LOCK="$BASE/c13_value_v2_full_collection_retry1/C13_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_TECHNICAL_FAILURE_LOCK_V1.json"

MAPPING="$EVAL/C03_VALUE_V2_C03_FEATURE_MAPPING_AND_C13_HARNESS_SEARCH_V1.json"
OUT="$EVAL/C03_VALUE_V2_PROVEN_CHECKPOINT_LOAD_FORWARD_CHAIN_LOCK_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e

EXPECTED_HARNESS=f8112e1e6bf77805b3c5141a03a26ae273051cade96762899dc21194503cfab4
EXPECTED_PARENT=b8a2d5b91f060f99a43b7aa10b554cf875ce4acd200eab0efc6f6ad41325a466

EXPECTED_CKPT=ce32cb7943a05d62ea16aee95a86f26bab7521af1d345e39f5c8a410a02ffb13
EXPECTED_NORM=36ee17a182c519c41602c5d414a4e009a569660254bb4bd31fc75a3b705bc6f9
EXPECTED_MODEL_FREEZE=a2be866144446a442cf7a8cb23a7f7ce9041a2d7b86fcded2ff8709f7a24d05b

EXPECTED_START=1b5a82ceb323f449af2204b9d49d3c81ff5392a251e83ff4681b56071334f5cf
EXPECTED_FAIL=50c072076805e983e23dc14150bd70574dfb5b1c964f718d783a53fea806e538

EXPECTED_MAPPING=f3c5f637f0e3ec1d216c88dc26ba1f6f3c9737635a659438bc539b7dad06c713

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== PREFLIGHT ==="

HEAD="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
echo "HEAD=$HEAD"

[ "$HEAD" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$HARNESS" "$EXPECTED_HARNESS" "HARNESS"
check_sha "$PARENT" "$EXPECTED_PARENT" "PARENT"
check_sha "$CKPT" "$EXPECTED_CKPT" "CHECKPOINT"
check_sha "$NORM" "$EXPECTED_NORM" "NORMALIZATION"
check_sha "$MODEL_FREEZE" "$EXPECTED_MODEL_FREEZE" "MODEL_FREEZE"
check_sha "$START" "$EXPECTED_START" "HISTORICAL_START"
check_sha "$FAIL_LOCK" "$EXPECTED_FAIL" "FAILURE_LOCK"
check_sha "$MAPPING" "$EXPECTED_MAPPING" "C03_MAPPING"

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    echo "EXISTING_OUTPUT_SHA=$(sha "$OUT")"
    false
}

python - \
"$BASE" \
"$HARNESS" \
"$PARENT" \
"$CKPT" \
"$NORM" \
"$MODEL_FREEZE" \

"$FAIL_LOCK" \
"$MAPPING" \
"$OUT" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime

(
    base,
    harness_p,
    parent_p,
    ckpt_p,
    norm_p,
    model_freeze_p,
    start_p,
    failure_p,
    mapping_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        path.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


HARNESS_SHA = sha(harness_p)
PARENT_SHA = sha(parent_p)
START_SHA = sha(start_p)

start = json.loads(
    start_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

failure = json.loads(
    failure_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

mapping = json.loads(
    mapping_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 1. Verify historical START inputs exist under C13 evidence
# ============================================================

EXPECTED_INPUT_SHA = {
    "c13_root_jsonl_sha256":
        start["c13_root_jsonl_sha256"],

    "full_collection_freeze_sha256":
        start["full_collection_freeze_sha256"],

    "pre_eval_schema_lock_sha256":
        start["pre_eval_schema_lock_sha256"],

    "selection_lock_sha256":
        start["selection_lock_sha256"],
}

print()
print("=== HISTORICAL START INPUT RESOLUTION ===")


def candidate_files():
    for child in base.iterdir():

        if not child.name.lower().startswith("c13"):
            continue

        if not child.is_dir():
            continue

        for p in child.rglob("*"):

            if not p.is_file():
                continue

            if p.suffix.lower() not in {
                ".json",
                ".jsonl",
                ".txt",
                ".log",
            }:
                continue

            try:
                if p.stat().st_size > 20_000_000:
                    continue
            except OSError:
                continue

            yield p


files = list(candidate_files())

resolved = {}

for field, expected_sha in EXPECTED_INPUT_SHA.items():

    hits = []

    for p in files:

        try:
            if sha(p) == expected_sha:
                hits.append(str(p))
        except Exception:
            pass

    resolved[field] = hits

    print(
        f"{field}_MATCH_COUNT="
        f"{len(hits)}"
    )

    for p in hits:
        print(
            f"{field}_PATH={p}"
        )

    assert len(hits) >= 1, (
        "UNRESOLVED_START_INPUT",
        field,
        expected_sha,
    )

print(
    "HISTORICAL_START_INPUT_RESOLUTION=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Verify the immutable local model artifacts match START
# ============================================================

assert sha(ckpt_p) == start[
    "checkpoint_sha256"
]

assert sha(norm_p) == start[
    "normalization_sha256"
]

assert sha(model_freeze_p) == start[
    "model_freeze_sha256"
]

print(
    "START_MODEL_ARTIFACT_BINDING=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Verify exact historical harness source contains the
#    START-generation contract and inference chain
# ============================================================

source = harness_p.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8",
    errors="strict",
)

source_contract_tokens = [
    'START="$C13/C13_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_STARTED_V1.json"',
    "start_record = {",
    '"PAPER1_VALUE_V2_C13_ONE_TIME_EVALUATION_STARTED_V1"',
    '"c13_root_jsonl_sha256"',
    '"full_collection_freeze_sha256"',
    '"pre_eval_schema_lock_sha256"',
    '"checkpoint_sha256"',
    '"normalization_sha256"',
    '"model_freeze_sha256"',
    '"selection_lock_sha256"',
    '"root_count"',
    "115",
    '"retuning_authorized"',
    "start_path.write_text(",
    "json.dumps(",
    "indent=2",
    "sort_keys=True",
]

missing = [
    token
    for token in source_contract_tokens
    if token not in source
]

if missing:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "HARNESS_START_SCHEMA_MISSING:"
        + repr(missing)
    )

print(
    "HARNESS_SOURCE_START_SCHEMA=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 4. Deterministically reconstruct the exact historical
#    START lock using the harness-defined record.
#
#    IMPORTANT:
#    - no torch import
#    - no checkpoint load
#    - no model forward
# ============================================================

reconstructed = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V2_C13_ONE_TIME_EVALUATION_STARTED_V1",

    "status":
        "STARTED",

    "c13_root_jsonl_sha256":
        start[
            "c13_root_jsonl_sha256"
        ],

    "full_collection_freeze_sha256":
        start[
            "full_collection_freeze_sha256"
        ],

    "pre_eval_schema_lock_sha256":
        start[
            "pre_eval_schema_lock_sha256"
        ],

    "checkpoint_sha256":
        sha(ckpt_p),

    "normalization_sha256":
        sha(norm_p),

    "model_freeze_sha256":
        sha(model_freeze_p),

    "selection_lock_sha256":
        start[
            "selection_lock_sha256"
        ],

    "root_count":
        115,

    "primary_gate":
        (
            "C13_NORMALIZED_REGRET < "
            "C13_IMMEDIATE_REWARD_BASELINE_NORMALIZED_REGRET"
        ),

    "retuning_authorized":
        False,
}

assert reconstructed == start, (
    "RECONSTRUCTED_START_CONTENT_MISMATCH",
    reconstructed,
    start,
)

reconstructed_bytes = (
    json.dumps(
        reconstructed,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
    )
    + "\n"
).encode("utf-8")

RECONSTRUCTED_SHA = hashlib.sha256(
    reconstructed_bytes
).hexdigest()

print(
    "RECONSTRUCTED_START_SHA256="
    + RECONSTRUCTED_SHA
)

print(
    "HISTORICAL_START_SHA256="
    + START_SHA
)

assert RECONSTRUCTED_SHA == START_SHA

print(
    "DETERMINISTIC_START_RECONSTRUCTION=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 5. Bind historical START to historical failure lock
# ============================================================

assert failure[
    "start_lock_sha256"
] == START_SHA

assert failure[
    "model_forward_executed"
] is True

assert failure[
    "model_forward_call_count"
] == 1

assert failure[
    "prediction_shape"
] == [115, 16]

    "prediction_artifact_persisted"
] is False

assert failure[
    "scientific_metrics_evaluated"
] is False

    "START_FAILURE_CHAIN=PASS"
)

    "HISTORICAL_FORWARD_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE=PASS"

# 6. Lock exact checkpoint-load -> forward source contract
# ============================================================

inference_tokens = [
    "checkpoint = torch.load(",
    'map_location="cpu"',
    "weights_only=False",
    'Model = ns["ActionConditionedValueV2"]',
    "model = Model(12)",
    "model.load_state_dict(",
    "strict=True",
    "model.eval()",
    "Xn = (",
    "(X - mean) / std",
    ").astype(np.float32)",
    "with torch.no_grad():",
    "torch.from_numpy(Xn)",
    ".cpu()",
    ".numpy()",
    ".astype(np.float32)",
    "if pred.shape != (115, 16):",
    'print("VALUE_MODEL_FORWARD_CALL_COUNT=1")',
]

missing_inference = [
    token
    for token in inference_tokens
    if token not in source
]

if missing_inference:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "INFERENCE_CHAIN_SOURCE_MISSING:"
        + repr(missing_inference)
    )

    "INFERENCE_CHAIN_SOURCE_CONTRACT=PASS"

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import hashlib

base = Path(sys.argv[1])
name = sys.argv[2]
expected = sys.argv[3]

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

hits = list(base.rglob(name))

print("SELECTION_NAME_MATCH_COUNT=", len(hits))

for p in hits:
    s = sha(p)
    print("SELECTION_PATH=", p)
    print("SELECTION_SHA256=", s)
    print(
        "SELECTION_SHA_MATCH="
        + ("PASS" if s == expected else "FAIL")
    )

exact = [
    p for p in hits
    if sha(p) == expected
]

print(
    "SELECTION_EXACT_SHA_MATCH_COUNT=",
    len(exact),
)

if len(exact) != 1:
    print("SELECTION_LOCK_RESOLUTION=FAIL")
else:
    print("SELECTION_LOCK_RESOLUTION=PASS")
    print("RESOLVED_SELECTION_PATH=", exact[0])

    "checkpoint_sha256":
        sha(ckpt_p),

    "normalization_sha256":
        sha(norm_p),

    "model_freeze_sha256":
        sha(model_freeze_p),

    "selection_lock_sha256":
        sha(selection_p),

    "root_count":
        115,

    "primary_gate":
        (
            "C13_NORMALIZED_REGRET < "
            "C13_IMMEDIATE_REWARD_BASELINE_NORMALIZED_REGRET"
        ),

    "retuning_authorized":
        False,
}

assert reconstructed == start

reconstructed_bytes = (
    json.dumps(
        reconstructed,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
    )
    + "\n"
).encode("utf-8")

reconstructed_sha = hashlib.sha256(
    reconstructed_bytes
).hexdigest()

assert reconstructed_sha == sha(start_p)

print("DETERMINISTIC_START_RECONSTRUCTION=PASS")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. START -> frozen failure evidence
# ------------------------------------------------------------

assert failure["start_lock_sha256"] == sha(start_p)

assert failure["model_forward_executed"] is True
assert failure["model_forward_call_count"] == 1
assert failure["prediction_shape"] == [115, 16]

assert failure["prediction_artifact_persisted"] is False
assert failure["scientific_metrics_evaluated"] is False

print("START_FAILURE_CHAIN=PASS")
print("HISTORICAL_FORWARD_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE=PASS")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Historical harness inference-chain source contract
# ------------------------------------------------------------

required_inference_tokens = [
    "checkpoint = torch.load(",
    'map_location="cpu"',
    "weights_only=False",
    'Model = ns["ActionConditionedValueV2"]',
    "model = Model(12)",
    "model.load_state_dict(",
    "strict=True",
    "model.eval()",
    "(X - mean) / std",
    ").astype(np.float32)",
    "with torch.no_grad():",
    "torch.from_numpy(Xn)",
    ".cpu()",
    ".numpy()",
    "if pred.shape != (115, 16):",
    'print("VALUE_MODEL_FORWARD_CALL_COUNT=1")',
]

missing = [
    x for x in required_inference_tokens
    if x not in harness_src
]

assert not missing, (
    "INFERENCE_SOURCE_MISSING",
    missing,
)

print("INFERENCE_CHAIN_SOURCE_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Parent model provenance
# ------------------------------------------------------------

    failure["authoritative_parent_sha256"]
    == sha(parent_p)
)

    assert token in parent_src, (

        token,
    )

    mapping["feature_mapping"]["status"]
    == "EXACT_NESTED_JSON_PATH_MAPPING_PASS"
)

    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"

        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import ast
import json
import hashlib
import datetime

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn


(
    parent_p,
    ckpt_p,
    norm_p,
    model_freeze_p,
    mapping_p,
    chain_lock_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        path.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


print()
print("=== RUNTIME ===")
print("TORCH_VERSION=" + torch.__version__)
print("CUDA_VISIBLE=" + str(torch.cuda.is_available()))

# GPU must remain unused.
assert torch.cuda.is_available() is False

torch.set_num_threads(1)
torch.manual_seed(0)


# ============================================================
# 1. Previous frozen boundaries
# ============================================================

mapping = json.loads(
    mapping_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

chain = json.loads(
    chain_lock_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

sb = mapping["science_boundary"]

assert sb["checkpoint_loaded"] is False
assert sb["model_forward_executed"] is False
assert sb["C03_prediction_computed"] is False
assert sb["C03_metrics_computed"] is False
assert sb["value_v2_evaluation_authorized"] is False
assert sb["MCTS_value_integration"] is False

assert (
    chain["status"]
    ==
    "FROZEN_STATIC_INFERENCE_CHAIN_PROVEN_BY_DETERMINISTIC_RECONSTRUCTION"
)

assert (
    chain["next_stage"]
    ==
    "SYNTHETIC_CHECKPOINT_COMPATIBLE_FORWARD"
)

print("PRIOR_STATIC_BOUNDARY=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Exact normalization artifact
# ============================================================

with np.load(
    norm_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    feature_names = [
        str(x)
        for x in z["feature_names"].tolist()
    ]

    mean = np.asarray(
        z["feature_mean"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    std = np.asarray(
        z["feature_std"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )


EXPECTED_FEATURES = [
    "A_speed_mps",
    "B_speed_mps",
    "A_accel_mps2",
    "B_accel_mps2",
    "A_remaining_to_conflict_m",
    "B_remaining_to_conflict_m",
    "delta_v_abs_mps",
    "delta_heading_abs_rad",
    "delta_heading_over_pi",
    "I_Omega_app_AND",
    "I_Omega_int_AND",
    "alpha3",
]

assert feature_names == EXPECTED_FEATURES
assert mean.shape == (12,)
assert std.shape == (12,)
assert np.isfinite(mean).all()
assert np.isfinite(std).all()
assert (std > 0).all()

print("NORMALIZATION_SCHEMA=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 3. Extract exact ActionConditionedValueV2 class from
#    authoritative parent source.
# ============================================================

parent_src = parent_p.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8"
)

tree = ast.parse(
    parent_src,
    filename=str(parent_p),
)

hidden = None
model_node = None

for node in tree.body:

    if isinstance(
        node,
        (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign),
    ):

        targets = []

        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
            targets = node.targets
            value = node.value
        else:
            targets = [node.target]
            value = node.value

        for target in targets:

            if (
                isinstance(target, ast.Name)
                and target.id == "HIDDEN"
            ):
                hidden = int(
                    ast.literal_eval(value)
                )

    if (
        isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef)
        and node.name
        == "ActionConditionedValueV2"
    ):
        model_node = node


assert hidden == 64
assert model_node is not None

module = ast.Module(
    body=[model_node],
    type_ignores=[],
)

ast.fix_missing_locations(module)

namespace = {
    "nn": nn,
    "HIDDEN": hidden,
}

exec(
    compile(
        module,
        filename=str(parent_p),
        mode="exec",
    ),
    namespace,
)

Model = namespace[
    "ActionConditionedValueV2"
]

print("AUTHORITATIVE_MODEL_CLASS_EXTRACTION=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 4. Load exact frozen checkpoint
# ============================================================

checkpoint = torch.load(
    ckpt_p,
    map_location="cpu",
    weights_only=False,
)

assert isinstance(checkpoint, dict)

print(
    "CHECKPOINT_KEYS="
    + repr(sorted(checkpoint.keys()))
)

if "model_type" in checkpoint:
    assert (
        checkpoint["model_type"]
        == "ActionConditionedValueV2"
    )

if "input_dim" in checkpoint:
    assert int(
        checkpoint["input_dim"]
    ) == 12

if "hidden_dim" in checkpoint:
    assert int(
        checkpoint["hidden_dim"]
    ) == 64

if "output_dim" in checkpoint:
    assert int(
        checkpoint["output_dim"]
    ) == 16

if "feature_names" in checkpoint:

    ckpt_features = [
        str(x)
        for x in checkpoint[
            "feature_names"
        ]
    ]

    assert ckpt_features == feature_names

if "target" in checkpoint:
    assert (
        str(
            checkpoint["target"]
        ).upper()
        == "ROOT_CENTERED_Q"
    )


assert "state_dict" in checkpoint

print("CHECKPOINT_SCHEMA=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 5. Check embedded normalization if present
# ============================================================

if "feature_mean" in checkpoint:

    ck_mean = np.asarray(
        checkpoint["feature_mean"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    assert ck_mean.shape == (12,)

    assert np.allclose(
        ck_mean,
        mean,
        rtol=0.0,
        atol=0.0,
    )

if "feature_std" in checkpoint:

    ck_std = np.asarray(
        checkpoint["feature_std"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    assert ck_std.shape == (12,)

    assert np.allclose(
        ck_std,
        std,
        rtol=0.0,
        atol=0.0,
    )

print("CHECKPOINT_NORMALIZATION_BINDING=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 6. Strict state-dict load
# ============================================================

model = Model(12)

load_result = model.load_state_dict(
    checkpoint["state_dict"],
    strict=True,
)

assert len(load_result.missing_keys) == 0
assert len(load_result.unexpected_keys) == 0

model.eval()

parameter_shapes = {
    name: list(param.shape)
    for name, param
    in model.state_dict().items()
}

print(
    "MODEL_PARAMETER_SHAPES="
    + json.dumps(
        parameter_shapes,
        sort_keys=True,
    )
)

assert parameter_shapes[
    "net.0.weight"
] == [64, 12]

assert parameter_shapes[
    "net.0.bias"
] == [64]

assert parameter_shapes[
    "net.2.weight"
] == [64, 64]

assert parameter_shapes[
    "net.2.bias"
] == [64]

assert parameter_shapes[
    "net.4.weight"
] == [16, 64]

assert parameter_shapes[
    "net.4.bias"
] == [16]

print("STRICT_STATE_DICT_LOAD=PASS")
print("MODEL_ARCHITECTURE_SHAPE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 7. Deterministic synthetic feature matrix
#
# Standardized rows:
#   row 0      = all zeros
#   rows 1-12  = +1 on one feature
#   rows 13-24 = -1 on one feature
#
# No C03 runtime/root data is read.
# ============================================================

Z = np.zeros(
    (25, 12),
    dtype=np.float64,
)

for i in range(12):
    Z[1 + i, i] = 1.0
    Z[13 + i, i] = -1.0


X_raw = (
    mean[None, :]
    + Z * std[None, :]
)

Xn = (
    X_raw
    - mean[None, :]
) / std[None, :]


assert X_raw.shape == (25, 12)
assert Xn.shape == (25, 12)

assert np.isfinite(
    X_raw
).all()

assert np.isfinite(
    Xn
).all()

assert np.allclose(
    Xn,
    Z,
    rtol=0.0,
    atol=1e-12,
)

Xn32 = Xn.astype(
    np.float32
)

print("SYNTHETIC_RAW_SHAPE=(25, 12)")
print("SYNTHETIC_NORMALIZED_SHAPE=(25, 12)")
print("SYNTHETIC_NORMALIZATION_ROUNDTRIP=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 8. Synthetic checkpoint-compatible forward
# ============================================================

with torch.no_grad():

    pred1 = (
        model(
            torch.from_numpy(
                Xn32
            )
        )
        .cpu()
        .numpy()
        .astype(np.float32)
    )

with torch.no_grad():

    pred2 = (
        model(
            torch.from_numpy(
                Xn32
            )
        )
        .cpu()
        .numpy()
        .astype(np.float32)
    )


assert pred1.shape == (25, 16)
assert pred2.shape == (25, 16)

assert np.isfinite(
    pred1
).all()

assert np.isfinite(
    pred2
).all()

assert np.array_equal(
    pred1,
    pred2,
)

pred_sha = hashlib.sha256(
    np.ascontiguousarray(
        pred1
    ).tobytes()
).hexdigest()

    + pred_sha

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

        "metric_function":
            "ranking_metrics(q, pred, immediate)",
    },

    "required_execution_order": [
        "write START lock",
        "load frozen checkpoint",
        "build exact C03 12-D features",
        "apply frozen normalization",
        "strict load_state_dict",
        "model.eval",

        "validate predictions finite",

    ],

    },

    },

def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        path.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(
        path.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


def write_json(path, obj):
    path.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            obj,
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
            allow_nan=False,
        )
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def run_git(*args):
    import subprocess

    return subprocess.check_output(
        ["git", *args],
        text=True,
    ).strip()


def assert_repo():
    head = run_git(
        "rev-parse",
        "HEAD",
    )

    if head != EXPECTED_HEAD:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"UNEXPECTED_HEAD:{head}"
        )

    status = run_git(
        "status",
        "--porcelain=v1",
    ).splitlines()

    tracked = [
        x
        for x in status
        if x
        and not x.startswith("?? ")
    ]

    if tracked:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "TRACKED_OR_STAGED_CHANGE:"
            + repr(tracked)
        )

def get_path(obj, path):
    cur = obj

    for key in path:
        if not isinstance(
            cur,
            dict,
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "NON_DICT_FEATURE_PATH:"
                + ".".join(path)
            )

        if key not in cur:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "MISSING_FEATURE_PATH:"
                + ".".join(path)
            )

        cur = cur[key]

    return cur


def load_roots_static(
    root_jsonl,
    mapping_p,
):
    roots = [
        json.loads(line)
        for line
        in root_jsonl.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        ).splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]

    if len(roots) != 135:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "ROOT_COUNT:"
            + str(len(roots))
        )

    mapping = read_json(
        mapping_p
    )

    fm = mapping[
        "feature_mapping"
    ]

    assert (
        fm["status"]
        == "EXACT_NESTED_JSON_PATH_MAPPING_PASS"
    )

    names = fm[
        "feature_names"
    ]

    paths = fm[
        "json_paths"
    ]

    if len(names) != 12:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "FEATURE_COUNT"
        )

    steps = []

    for ri, root in enumerate(
        roots
    ):
        if "root_step" not in root:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"MISSING_ROOT_STEP:{ri}"
            )

        steps.append(
            int(root["root_step"])
        )

        actions = root.get(
            "actionwise"
        )

        if not isinstance(
            actions,
            list,
        ) or len(actions) != 16:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"ACTION_COUNT:{ri}"
            )

        for ai, action in enumerate(
            actions
        ):
            q = float(
                action["q_value"]
            )

            imm = float(
                action[
                    "immediate_reward"
                ]
            )

            if not math.isfinite(q):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"NONFINITE_Q:{ri}:{ai}"
                )

            if not math.isfinite(imm):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"NONFINITE_IMMEDIATE:{ri}:{ai}"
                )

        for name in names:
            value = float(
                get_path(
                    root,
                    paths[name],
                )
            )

            if not math.isfinite(
                value
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "NONFINITE_FEATURE:"
                    + str(ri)
                    + ":"
                    + name
                )

    if len(set(steps)) != 135:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "ROOT_STEP_NOT_UNIQUE"
        )

    return roots, mapping


def output_absence_gate():
    outputs = [
        START,
        PRED,
        RESULT,
        CAPTURE,
        FREEZE,
    ]

    existing = [
        str(p)
        for p in outputs
        if p.exists()
    ]

    if existing:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "ONE_TIME_NAMESPACE_ALREADY_CONSUMED:"
            + repr(existing)
        )


def static_check():
    assert_repo()

    c = resolve_contract()

    roots, mapping = (
        load_roots_static(
            c["root_jsonl"],
            c["mapping"],
        )
    )

    closure = (
        c["metric_closure"]
        .read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )

    for name in (
        "average_ranks",
        "pearson",
        "spearman",
        "ranking_metrics",
    ):
        if (
            "def " + name
            not in closure
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "METRIC_FUNCTION_MISSING:"
                + name
            )

    if "def main" in closure:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "METRIC_MAIN_PRESENT"
        )

    output_absence_gate()

    print(
        "STATIC_ROOT_COUNT=135"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_FEATURE_COUNT=12"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_ACTION_COUNT=16"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_ROOT_STEP_UNIQUE=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_FEATURE_PATHS=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_Q_IMMEDIATE=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_METRIC_CLOSURE=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_OUTPUT_NAMESPACE_UNUSED=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "SCIENTIFIC_GATE="
        + SCIENTIFIC_GATE
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_EVALUATOR_CONTRACT=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "CHECKPOINT_LOADED_THIS_STEP=False"
    )

    print(
        "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED_THIS_STEP=False"
    )

    print(
        "C03_PREDICTION_COMPUTED=False"
    )

    print(
        "C03_METRICS_COMPUTED=False"
    )


def jsonable(x):
    import numpy as np

    if isinstance(
        x,
        dict,
    ):
        return {
            str(k): jsonable(v)
            for k, v in x.items()
        }

    if isinstance(
        x,
        (list, tuple),
    ):
        return [
            jsonable(v)
            for v in x
        ]

    if isinstance(
        x,
        np.ndarray,
    ):
        return x.tolist()

    if isinstance(
        x,
        np.generic,
    ):
        return x.item()

    return x


def execute_once(
    wrapper_sha,
):
    assert_repo()

    c = resolve_contract()

    output_absence_gate()

    core_sha = sha(
        Path(__file__)
    )

    now = (
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat()
    )

    start_record = {
        "type":
            "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVALUATION_STARTED_V1",

        "status":
            "STARTED",

        "scene":
            "C03",

        "source_index":
            3,

        "attempt_number":
            1,

        "started_at_utc":
            now,

        "preauthorization_sha256":
            sha(PREAUTH),

        "evaluator_core_sha256":
            core_sha,

        "wrapper_sha256":
            wrapper_sha,

        "root_jsonl_sha256":
            sha(c["root_jsonl"]),

        "data_freeze_sha256":
            sha(c["data_freeze"]),

        "checkpoint_sha256":
            sha(c["ckpt"]),

        "normalization_sha256":
            sha(c["norm"]),

        "feature_mapping_sha256":
            sha(c["mapping"]),

        "inference_chain_lock_sha256":
            sha(c["chain_lock"]),

        "synthetic_forward_evidence_sha256":
            sha(c["synth_forward"]),

        "metric_closure_sha256":
            sha(c["metric_closure"]),

        "scientific_gate":
            SCIENTIFIC_GATE,

        "automatic_retry_authorized":
            False,

        "manual_retry_authorized":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    }

    write_json(
        START,
        start_record,
    )

    start_sha = sha(
        START
    )

    print(
        "C03_ONE_TIME_START_WRITTEN=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "C03_ONE_TIME_START_SHA256="
        + start_sha
    )

    prediction_written = False
    metrics_computed = False
    pred_sha = None

    try:
        import ast
        import numpy as np
        import torch
        import torch.nn as nn

        torch.set_num_threads(1)

        roots, mapping = (
            load_roots_static(
                c["root_jsonl"],
                c["mapping"],
            )
        )

        names = mapping[
            "feature_mapping"
        ][
            "feature_names"
        ]

        paths = mapping[
            "feature_mapping"
        ][
            "json_paths"
        ]

        X = np.asarray(
            [
                [
                    float(
                        get_path(
                            root,
                            paths[name],
                        )
                    )
                    for name in names
                ]
                for root in roots
            ],
            dtype=np.float64,
        )

        Q = np.asarray(
            [
                [
                    float(
                        action[
                            "q_value"
                        ]
                    )
                    for action
                    in root[
                        "actionwise"
                    ]
                ]
                for root in roots
            ],
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        IMM = np.asarray(
            [
                [
                    float(
                        action[
                            "immediate_reward"
                        ]
                    )
                    for action
                    in root[
                        "actionwise"
                    ]
                ]
                for root in roots
            ],
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        ROOT_STEP = np.asarray(
            [
                int(
                    root[
                        "root_step"
                    ]
                )
                for root in roots
            ],
            dtype=np.int64,
        )

        assert X.shape == (
            135,
            12,
        )

        assert Q.shape == (
            135,
            16,
        )

        assert IMM.shape == (
            135,
            16,
        )

        with np.load(
            c["norm"],
            allow_pickle=False,
        ) as z:

            norm_names = [
                str(v)
                for v
                in z[
                    "feature_names"
                ].tolist()
            ]

            mean = np.asarray(
                z[
                    "feature_mean"
                ],
                dtype=np.float64,
            )

            std = np.asarray(
                z[
                    "feature_std"
                ],
                dtype=np.float64,
            )

        assert (
            norm_names
            == names
        )

        assert mean.shape == (12,)
        assert std.shape == (12,)
        assert np.isfinite(mean).all()
        assert np.isfinite(std).all()
        assert (std > 0).all()

        Xn = (
            (X - mean)
            / std
        ).astype(
            np.float32
        )

        assert Xn.shape == (
            135,
            12,
        )

        assert np.isfinite(
            Xn
        ).all()

        chain = read_json(
            c["chain_lock"]
        )

        parent_p = Path(
            chain[
                "historical_C13"
            ][
                "authoritative_parent_path"
            ]
        )

        expected_parent_sha = (
            chain[
                "historical_C13"
            ][
                "authoritative_parent_sha256"
            ]
        )

        if sha(
            parent_p
        ) != expected_parent_sha:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "AUTHORITATIVE_PARENT_SHA"
            )

        parent_src = (
            parent_p.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )

        tree = ast.parse(
            parent_src
        )

        hidden = None
        model_node = None

        for node in tree.body:
            if isinstance(
                node,
                ast.Assign,
            ):
                for target in (
                    node.targets
                ):
                    if (
                        isinstance(
                            target,
                            ast.Name,
                        )
                        and target.id
                        == "HIDDEN"
                    ):
                        hidden = int(
                            ast.literal_eval(
                                node.value
                            )
                        )

            if (
                isinstance(
                    node,
                    ast.ClassDef,
                )
                and node.name
                == "ActionConditionedValueV2"
            ):
                model_node = node

        assert hidden == 64
        assert model_node is not None

        mod = ast.Module(
            body=[
                model_node
            ],
            type_ignores=[],
        )

        ast.fix_missing_locations(
            mod
        )

        ns = {
            "nn": nn,
            "HIDDEN": 64,
        }

        exec(
            compile(
                mod,
                str(parent_p),
                "exec",
            ),
            ns,
        )

        Model = ns[
            "ActionConditionedValueV2"
        ]

        checkpoint = torch.load(
            c["ckpt"],
            map_location="cpu",
            weights_only=False,
        )

        if not isinstance(
            checkpoint,
            dict,
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "CHECKPOINT_NOT_DICT"
            )

        assert (
            checkpoint[
                "model_type"
            ]
            == "ActionConditionedValueV2"
        )

        assert int(
            checkpoint[
                "input_dim"
            ]
        ) == 12

        assert int(
            checkpoint[
                "hidden_dim"
            ]
        ) == 64

        assert int(
            checkpoint[
                "output_dim"
            ]
        ) == 16

        assert [
            str(x)
            for x
            in checkpoint[
                "feature_names"
            ]
        ] == names

        model = Model(
            12
        )

        load_result = (
            model.load_state_dict(
                checkpoint[
                    "state_dict"
                ],
                strict=True,
            )
        )

        assert not (
            load_result.missing_keys
        )

        assert not (
            load_result.unexpected_keys
        )

        model.eval()

        with torch.no_grad():
            pred = (
                model(
                    torch.from_numpy(
                        Xn
                    )
                )
                .cpu()
                .numpy()
                .astype(
                    np.float32
                )
            )

        if pred.shape != (
            135,
            16,
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "PREDICTION_SHAPE:"
                + repr(
                    pred.shape
                )
            )

        if not np.isfinite(
            pred
        ).all():
            raise RuntimeError(
                "NONFINITE_PREDICTION"
            )

        print(
            "VALUE_MODEL_FORWARD_CALL_COUNT=1"
        )

        print(
            "PREDICTION_SHAPE=(135, 16)"
        )

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # Prediction MUST persist before metrics.
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        np.savez_compressed(
            PRED,
            root_step=ROOT_STEP,
            q_true=Q,
            q_centered_true=(
                Q
                - Q.mean(
                    axis=1,
                    keepdims=True,
                )
            ).astype(
                np.float32
            ),
            q_centered_pred=pred,
            immediate_reward=IMM,
        )

        pred_sha = sha(
            PRED
        )

        prediction_written = True

        print(
            "PREDICTION_PERSISTED_BEFORE_METRICS=PASS"
        )

        print(
            "PREDICTION_SHA256="
            + pred_sha
        )

        metric_src = (
            c[
                "metric_closure"
            ]
            .read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )

        metric_ns = {}

        exec(
            compile(
                metric_src,
                str(
                    c[
                        "metric_closure"
                    ]
                ),
                "exec",
            ),
            metric_ns,
        )

        metric_fn = (
            metric_ns[
                "ranking_metrics"
            ]
        )

        metrics = metric_fn(
            Q,
            pred,
            IMM,
        )

        metrics = jsonable(
            metrics
        )

        if not isinstance(
            metrics,
            dict,
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "METRICS_NOT_DICT"
            )

        for key in (
            "normalized_regret",
            "immediate_regret",
        ):
            if key not in metrics:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "MISSING_METRIC:"
                    + key
                )

            if not math.isfinite(
                float(
                    metrics[key]
                )
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "NONFINITE_METRIC:"
                    + key
                )

        metrics_computed = True

        normalized_regret = float(
            metrics[
                "normalized_regret"
            ]
        )

        immediate_regret = float(
            metrics[
                "immediate_regret"
            ]
        )

        primary_pass = (
            normalized_regret
            < immediate_regret
        )

        decision = (
            "PASS_INDEPENDENT_C03_VALUE_V2_GATE"
            if primary_pass
            else
            "FAIL_INDEPENDENT_C03_VALUE_V2_GATE_DO_NOT_INTEGRATE"
        )

        result = {
            "type":
                "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_INDEPENDENT_EVAL_V1",

            "status":
                (
                    "SCIENCE_PASS"
                    if primary_pass
                    else
                    "SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"
                ),

            "scene":
                "C03",

            "source_index":
                3,

            "root_count":
                135,

            "prediction_shape":
                [135, 16],

            "model_forward_call_count":
                1,

            "scientific_gate":
                SCIENTIFIC_GATE,

            "primary_gate_pass":
                primary_pass,

            "decision":
                decision,

            "metrics":
                metrics,

            "prediction_artifact": {
                "path":
                    str(PRED),

                "sha256":
                    pred_sha,
            },

            "provenance": {
                "preauthorization_sha256":
                    sha(PREAUTH),

                "start_lock_sha256":
                    start_sha,

                "evaluator_core_sha256":
                    core_sha,

                "wrapper_sha256":
                    wrapper_sha,
            },

            "science_boundary": {
                "training_executed":
                    False,

                "retuning_executed":
                    False,

                "additional_forward_authorized":
                    False,

                "automatic_retry_authorized":
                    False,

                "MCTS_value_integration":
                    False,
            },

            "next_required_step":
                (
                    "REVIEW_INDEPENDENT_PASS_BEFORE_ANY_MCTS_INTEGRATION"
                    if primary_pass
                    else
                    "FREEZE_NEGATIVE_RESULT_DO_NOT_INTEGRATE_VALUE_V2"
                ),
        }

        write_json(
            RESULT,
            result,
        )

        result_sha = sha(
            RESULT
        )

        capture = {
            "type":
                "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_CAPTURE_V1",

            "status":
                "EXECUTION_RETURNED",

            "runner_process_rc":
                0,

            "start_sha256":
                start_sha,

            "prediction_exists":
                True,

            "prediction_sha256":
                pred_sha,

            "result_exists":
                True,

            "result_sha256":
                result_sha,

            "model_forward_call_count":
                1,

            "metrics_computed":
                True,

            "automatic_retry_authorized":
                False,
        }

        write_json(
            CAPTURE,
            capture,
        )

        freeze = {
            "type":
                "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVALUATION_FREEZE_V1",

            "status":
                (
                    "FROZEN_SCIENCE_PASS"
                    if primary_pass
                    else
                    "FROZEN_SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"
                ),

            "decision":
                decision,

            "start_sha256":
                start_sha,

            "prediction_sha256":
                pred_sha,

            "result_sha256":
                result_sha,

            "capture_sha256":
                sha(CAPTURE),

            "additional_forward_authorized":
                False,

            "automatic_retry_authorized":
                False,

            "MCTS_value_integration":
                False,
        }

        write_json(
            FREEZE,
            freeze,
        )

        print(
            "C03_METRICS_COMPUTED=True"
        )

        print(
            "NORMALIZED_REGRET="
            + repr(
                normalized_regret
            )
        )

        print(
            "IMMEDIATE_REGRET="
            + repr(
                immediate_regret
            )
        )

        print(
            "PRIMARY_GATE_PASS="
            + repr(
                primary_pass
            )
        )

        print(
            "DECISION="
            + decision
        )

        print(
            "RESULT_SHA256="
            + result_sha
        )

        print(
            "CAPTURE_SHA256="
            + sha(
                CAPTURE
            )
        )

        print(
            "FREEZE_SHA256="
            + sha(
                FREEZE
            )
        )

        print(
            "AUTOMATIC_RETRY_AUTHORIZED=False"
        )

        print(
            "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"
        )

        return 0

    except Exception as exc:
        error_text = (
            traceback.format_exc()
        )

        fail_result = {
            "type":
                "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_INDEPENDENT_EVAL_V1",

            "status":
                "FROZEN_TECHNICAL_FAILURE_AFTER_START",

            "scene":
                "C03",

            "source_index":
                3,

            "error":
                repr(exc),

            "traceback_tail":
                error_text[
                    -8000:
                ],

            "start_lock_sha256":
                start_sha,

            "prediction_artifact": {
                "exists":
                    PRED.is_file(),

                "sha256":
                    (
                        sha(PRED)
                        if PRED.is_file()
                        else None
                    ),
            },

            "model_forward_may_have_executed":
                prediction_written,

            "metrics_computed":
                metrics_computed,

            "automatic_retry_authorized":
                False,

            "manual_retry_authorized":
                False,

            "additional_forward_authorized":
                False,

            "MCTS_value_integration":
                False,
        }

        write_json(
            RESULT,
            fail_result,
        )

        capture = {
            "type":
                "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_CAPTURE_V1",

            "status":
                "TECHNICAL_FAILURE_RETURNED",

            "runner_process_rc":
                1,

            "start_sha256":
                start_sha,

            "prediction_exists":
                PRED.is_file(),

            "result_exists":
                RESULT.is_file(),

            "result_sha256":
                sha(RESULT),

            "automatic_retry_authorized":
                False,
        }

        write_json(
            CAPTURE,
            capture,
        )

        freeze = {
            "type":
                "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVALUATION_FREEZE_V1",

            "status":
                "FROZEN_TECHNICAL_FAILURE_NO_RETRY_AUTHORIZED",

            "start_sha256":
                start_sha,

            "result_sha256":
                sha(RESULT),

            "capture_sha256":
                sha(CAPTURE),

            "additional_forward_authorized":
                False,

            "automatic_retry_authorized":
                False,

            "MCTS_value_integration":
                False,
        }

        write_json(
            FREEZE,
            freeze,
        )

        print(
            "C03_ONE_TIME_EVALUATION=TECHNICAL_FAILURE"
        )

        print(
            "ERROR="
            + repr(
                exc
            )
        )

        print(
            "AUTOMATIC_RETRY_AUTHORIZED=False"
        )

        print(
            "MANUAL_RETRY_AUTHORIZED=False"
        )

        print(
            "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"
        )

        return 1


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--static-check",
        action="store_true",
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--execute",
        action="store_true",
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--wrapper-sha",
        default="",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    if (
        args.static_check
        == args.execute
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SELECT_EXACTLY_ONE_MODE"
        )

    if args.static_check:
        static_check()
        return 0

    if not args.wrapper_sha:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "MISSING_WRAPPER_SHA"
        )

    return execute_once(
        args.wrapper_sha
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(
        main()
    )
PY


# ============================================================
# 2. Compile core safely.
# ============================================================

python -m py_compile "$CORE"

echo "CORE_PY_COMPILE=PASS"

CORE_SHA="$(sha "$CORE")"

echo "CORE_SHA256=$CORE_SHA"


# ============================================================
# 3. Freeze core derivation contract.
# ============================================================

python - \
"$PREAUTH" \
"$CORE" \
"$DERIVE_LOCK" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime

preauth_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
core_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[3])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


pre = json.loads(
    preauth_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    pre["status"]
    == "PREAUTHORIZED_NOT_STARTED"
)

assert (
    pre["science_boundary"][
        "C03_value_evaluation_authorized"
    ]
    is True
)

assert (
    pre["one_time_protocol"][
        "automatic_retry_authorized"
    ]
    is False
)

payload = {
    "type":
        "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVALUATOR_DERIVATION_LOCK_V1",

    "status":
        "CORE_DERIVED_STATIC_NOT_EXECUTED",

    "preauthorization_sha256":
        sha(preauth_p),

    "evaluator_core_path":
        str(core_p),

    "evaluator_core_sha256":
        sha(core_p),

    "scientific_decision_contract": {
        "primary_metric":
            "normalized_regret",

        "baseline_metric":
            "immediate_regret",

        "pass_rule":
            "normalized_regret < immediate_regret",

        "pass_decision":
            "PASS_INDEPENDENT_C03_VALUE_V2_GATE",

        "fail_decision":
            "FAIL_INDEPENDENT_C03_VALUE_V2_GATE_DO_NOT_INTEGRATE",
    },

    "one_time_contract": {
        "attempt_number":
            1,

        "write_start_before_checkpoint_load":
            True,

        "forward_call_count":
            1,

        "persist_prediction_before_metrics":
            True,

        "automatic_retry_authorized":
            False,

        "manual_retry_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "checkpoint_loaded_by_derivation":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed_by_derivation":
            False,

        "C03_prediction_computed":
            False,

        "C03_metrics_computed":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        "BUILD_WRAPPER_AND_RUN_STATIC_CHECK",

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}

out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)

print(
    "DERIVATION_LOCK_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "DERIVATION_LOCK_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)
PY

DERIVE_SHA="$(sha "$DERIVE_LOCK")"


# ============================================================
# 4. Write execution wrapper.
# ============================================================

cat > "$WRAPPER" <<EOF
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -eu

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic
export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1

CORE="$CORE"
PREAUTH="$PREAUTH"
DERIVE_LOCK="$DERIVE_LOCK"

EXPECTED_HEAD="$EXPECTED_HEAD"
EXPECTED_PREAUTH="$EXPECTED_PREAUTH"
EXPECTED_CORE="$CORE_SHA"
EXPECTED_DERIVE="$DERIVE_SHA"

sha() {
    sha256sum "\$1" | awk '{print \$1}'
}

MODE="\${1:-check}"

case "\$MODE" in
    check|--execute)
        ;;
    *)
        echo "FAIL MODE=\$MODE"
        false
        ;;
esac

[ "\$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "\$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="\$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "\$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "\$TRACKED"
    false
}

[ "\$(sha "\$PREAUTH")" = "\$EXPECTED_PREAUTH" ] || {
    echo "FAIL PREAUTH_SHA"
    false
}

[ "\$(sha "\$CORE")" = "\$EXPECTED_CORE" ] || {
    echo "FAIL CORE_SHA"
    false
}

[ "\$(sha "\$DERIVE_LOCK")" = "\$EXPECTED_DERIVE" ] || {
    echo "FAIL DERIVATION_LOCK_SHA"
    false
}

echo "WRAPPER_PREAUTH_GATE=PASS"

if [ "\$MODE" = "check" ]; then

    python "\$CORE" \
        --static-check

    echo "C03_EVALUATOR_WRAPPER=PASS_STATIC_ONLY"
    echo "CHECKPOINT_LOADED=False"
    echo "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
    echo "C03_PREDICTION_COMPUTED=False"
    echo "C03_METRICS_COMPUTED=False"
    echo "NEXT=FREEZE_STATIC_READY_LOCK"

else

    WRAPPER_SHA="\$(sha "\$0")"

    python "\$CORE" \
        --execute \
        --wrapper-sha "\$WRAPPER_SHA"

fi
EOF

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

思考了 16s
当前任务：低资源即可。你现在看起来仍是 80GiB/15CPU 高资源状态，但这次只做 135×12 的一次 MLP forward，计算量很小；为了避免切卡带来额外环境变化，可以直接在当前实例执行这一唯一一次，结束后马上关高资源卡。

现在正式执行 C03 Value V2 唯一一次 independent evaluation。这段只能执行一次，无论 PASS/FAIL 都不要重跑。

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic
export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1

(
set -u
set -o pipefail

EVAL=/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/c03_value_v2_one_time_evaluation_v1
WRAPPER="$EVAL/RUN_C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_V1.sh"
LOG="$EVAL/C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EXECUTION_TERMINAL_V1.log"

echo "=== C03 VALUE V2 ONE-TIME EXECUTION ==="

[ -f "$WRAPPER" ] || {
    echo "FAIL WRAPPER_MISSING=$WRAPPER"
    false
}

[ ! -e "$LOG" ] || {
    echo "FAIL EXECUTION_LOG_ALREADY_EXISTS=$LOG"
    false
}

echo "DO_NOT_RERUN_THIS_COMMAND=True"
echo "EXECUTION_STARTING=TRUE"

set +e

bash "$WRAPPER" --execute \
    2>&1 | tee "$LOG"

RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}

set -e

echo
echo "=== EXECUTION PROCESS RETURNED ==="
echo "WRAPPER_PROCESS_RC=$RC"
echo "EXECUTION_LOG=$LOG"
echo "DO_NOT_RERUN=True"

python - "$EVAL" "$RC" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib

E = Path(sys.argv[1])
rc = int(sys.argv[2])

START = E / "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_EVAL_STARTED_V1.json"
PRED = E / "C03_VALUE_V2_ONE_TIME_PREDICTIONS_V1.npz"

}

    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    }
