    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C03 VALUE V2 FAILURE DIAGNOSTIC V1 ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$PRED" "$EXPECTED_PRED" PREDICTION
check_sha "$RESULT" "$EXPECTED_RESULT" RESULT
check_sha "$FREEZE" "$EXPECTED_FREEZE" FREEZE
check_sha "$FINAL" "$EXPECTED_FINAL" FINAL_DECISION
check_sha "$MAPPING" "$EXPECTED_MAPPING" FEATURE_MAPPING
check_sha "$NORM" "$EXPECTED_NORM" NORMALIZATION
check_sha "$METRIC" "$EXPECTED_METRIC" METRIC_CLOSURE

[ -f "$ROOT_JSONL" ] || {
    echo "FAIL ROOT_JSONL_MISSING"
    false
}

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    echo "EXISTING_OUTPUT_SHA=$(sha "$OUT")"
    false
}

python - \
"$PRED" \
"$RESULT" \
"$FREEZE" \
"$FINAL" \
"$ROOT_JSONL" \
"$MAPPING" \
"$NORM" \
"$METRIC" \
"$OUT" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import math
import numpy as np

(
    pred_p,
    result_p,
    freeze_p,
    final_p,
    roots_p,
    mapping_p,
    norm_p,
    metric_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def read_json(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


def safe_pearson(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(
        a,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    b = np.asarray(
        b,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    mask = (
        np.isfinite(a)
        & np.isfinite(b)
    )

    a = a[mask]
    b = b[mask]

    if len(a) < 2:
        return None

    a = a - a.mean()
    b = b - b.mean()

    denom = math.sqrt(
        float(
            np.sum(a * a)
            * np.sum(b * b)
        )
    )

    if denom <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0

    return float(
        np.sum(a * b)
        / denom
    )


def qtiles(x):
    x = np.asarray(
        x,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    return {
        "min":
            float(np.min(x)),

        "p05":
            float(
                np.quantile(
                    x,
                    0.05,
                )
            ),

        "p25":
            float(
                np.quantile(
                    x,
                    0.25,
                )
            ),

        "median":
            float(
                np.quantile(
                    x,
                    0.50,
                )
            ),

        "p75":
            float(
                np.quantile(
                    x,
                    0.75,
                )
            ),

        "p95":
            float(
                np.quantile(
                    x,
                    0.95,
                )
            ),

        "max":
            float(np.max(x)),

        "mean":
            float(np.mean(x)),
    }


def entropy_normalized(counts):
    counts = np.asarray(
        counts,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    p = counts / counts.sum()
    p = p[p > 0]

    h = -float(
        np.sum(
            p * np.log(p)
        )
    )

    return h / math.log(16.0)


def get_path(obj, path):
    cur = obj

    for key in path:
        cur = cur[key]

    return cur


# ============================================================
# 1. Frozen decision sanity
# ============================================================

result = read_json(
    result_p
)

freeze = read_json(
    freeze_p
)

final = read_json(
    final_p
)

assert (
    result["status"]
    == "SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"
)

assert (
    result["decision"]
    == "FAIL_INDEPENDENT_C03_VALUE_V2_GATE_DO_NOT_INTEGRATE"
)

assert (
    result["primary_gate_pass"]
    is False
)

assert (
    freeze["status"]
    == "FROZEN_SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"
)

assert (
    freeze[
        "additional_forward_authorized"
    ]
    is False
)

assert (
    freeze[
        "automatic_retry_authorized"
    ]
    is False
)

assert (
    freeze[
        "MCTS_value_integration"
    ]
    is False
)

assert (
    final["status"]
    == "CLOSED_SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"
)

assert (
    final["decision"]
    == "DO_NOT_INTEGRATE_VALUE_V2_INTO_MCTS"
)

assert (
    final["protocol"][
        "one_time_protocol_consumed"
    ]
    is True
)

assert (
    final["protocol"][
        "retuning_on_C03_authorized"
    ]
    is False
)

print("FROZEN_NEGATIVE_DECISION_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Load frozen prediction artifact
# ============================================================

with np.load(
    pred_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    print(
        "PREDICTION_KEYS=",
        sorted(z.files),
    )

    ROOT_STEP = np.asarray(
        z["root_step"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    )

    Q = np.asarray(
        z["q_true"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    QC = np.asarray(
        z["q_centered_true"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    P = np.asarray(
        z["q_centered_pred"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    IMM = np.asarray(
        z["immediate_reward"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )


assert ROOT_STEP.shape == (135,)
assert Q.shape == (135, 16)
assert QC.shape == (135, 16)
assert P.shape == (135, 16)
assert IMM.shape == (135, 16)

assert np.isfinite(Q).all()
assert np.isfinite(QC).all()
assert np.isfinite(P).all()
assert np.isfinite(IMM).all()

assert np.allclose(
    QC,
    Q - Q.mean(
        axis=1,
        keepdims=True,
    ),
    rtol=0.0,
    atol=1e-5,
)

print("PREDICTION_ARTIFACT_SCHEMA=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 3. Re-run exact frozen metric on existing predictions only
#    NO model execution.
# ============================================================

metric_ns = {}

exec(
    compile(
        metric_p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        ),
        str(metric_p),
        "exec",
    ),
    metric_ns,
)

ranking_metrics = metric_ns[
    "ranking_metrics"
]

global_metrics = ranking_metrics(
    Q,
    P,
    IMM,
)

expected_metrics = result[
    "metrics"
]

for key in (
    "root_count",
    "mean_root_spearman",
    "top1",
    "top3_contains_true_best",
    "normalized_regret",
    "immediate_regret",
):
    a = float(
        global_metrics[key]
    )

    b = float(
        expected_metrics[key]
    )

    assert math.isclose(
        a,
        b,
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=1e-12,
    ), (
        "GLOBAL_METRIC_MISMATCH",
        key,
        a,
        b,
    )

print("FROZEN_METRIC_REPRODUCTION=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 4. Exact per-root regret decomposition
# ============================================================

model_regret = []
imm_regret = []
root_spearman = []

for i in range(135):

    m = ranking_metrics(
        Q[i:i+1],
        P[i:i+1],
        IMM[i:i+1],
    )

    model_regret.append(
        float(
            m[
                "normalized_regret"
            ]
        )
    )

    imm_regret.append(
        float(
            m[
                "immediate_regret"
            ]
        )
    )

    root_spearman.append(
        float(
            m[
                "mean_root_spearman"
            ]
        )
    )


model_regret = np.asarray(
    model_regret,
    dtype=np.float64,
)

imm_regret = np.asarray(
    imm_regret,
    dtype=np.float64,
)

root_spearman = np.asarray(
    root_spearman,
    dtype=np.float64,
)

regret_delta = (
    model_regret
    - imm_regret
)

EPS = 1e-12

better = (
    regret_delta
    < -EPS
)

worse = (
    regret_delta
    > EPS
)

equal = ~(
    better
    | worse
)

print(
    "MODEL_BETTER_ROOT_COUNT=",
    int(better.sum()),
)

print(
    "MODEL_WORSE_ROOT_COUNT=",
    int(worse.sum()),
)

print(
    "MODEL_EQUAL_ROOT_COUNT=",
    int(equal.sum()),
)

print(
    "MODEL_BETTER_ROOT_RATE=",
    float(better.mean()),
)


# ============================================================
# 5. Action-head behavior
# ============================================================

TRUE_BEST = np.argmax(
    Q,
    axis=1,
)

MODEL_BEST = np.argmax(
    P,
    axis=1,
)

IMM_BEST = np.argmax(
    IMM,
    axis=1,
)

true_counts = np.bincount(
    TRUE_BEST,
    minlength=16,
)

model_counts = np.bincount(
    MODEL_BEST,
    minlength=16,
)

imm_counts = np.bincount(
    IMM_BEST,
    minlength=16,
)


model_top1_rate = float(
    np.mean(
        MODEL_BEST
        == TRUE_BEST
    )
)

imm_top1_rate = float(
    np.mean(
        IMM_BEST
        == TRUE_BEST
    )
)

model_vs_imm_same = (
    MODEL_BEST
    == IMM_BEST
)

different = ~model_vs_imm_same

model_selected_q = Q[
    np.arange(135),
    MODEL_BEST,
]

imm_selected_q = Q[
    np.arange(135),
    IMM_BEST,
]

when_different_model_better = (
    different
    & (
        model_selected_q
        > imm_selected_q
        + 1e-12
    )
)

when_different_model_worse = (
    different
    & (
        model_selected_q
        < imm_selected_q
        - 1e-12
    )
)

when_different_equal = (
    different
    & ~(
        when_different_model_better
        | when_different_model_worse
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_RATE=",
    model_top1_rate,
)

print(
    "IMMEDIATE_TOP1_RATE=",
    imm_top1_rate,
)

print(
    "MODEL_IMMEDIATE_SAME_ACTION_RATE=",
    float(
        model_vs_imm_same.mean()
    ),
)

print(
    "MODEL_UNIQUE_TOP1_ACTIONS=",
    int(
        np.count_nonzero(
            model_counts
        )
    ),
)

print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_MAX_ACTION_SHARE=",
    float(
        model_counts.max()
        / 135.0
    ),
)

print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_ENTROPY_NORMALIZED=",
    entropy_normalized(
        model_counts
    ),
)

print(
    "WHEN_ACTION_DIFF_MODEL_BETTER_COUNT=",
    int(
        when_different_model_better.sum()
    ),
)

print(
    "WHEN_ACTION_DIFF_MODEL_WORSE_COUNT=",
    int(
        when_different_model_worse.sum()
    ),
)

print(
    "WHEN_ACTION_DIFF_EQUAL_COUNT=",
    int(
        when_different_equal.sum()
    ),
)


# ============================================================
# 6. Q/prediction scale and ambiguity
# ============================================================

Q_SPAN = (
    Q.max(axis=1)
    - Q.min(axis=1)
)

Q_STD = Q.std(
    axis=1
)

P_SPAN = (
    P.max(axis=1)
    - P.min(axis=1)
)

P_STD = P.std(
    axis=1
)

sorted_q = np.sort(
    Q,
    axis=1,
)

TRUE_BEST_GAP = (
    sorted_q[:, -1]
    - sorted_q[:, -2]
)

SCALE_RATIO = (
    P_STD
    / np.maximum(
        Q_STD,
        1e-12,
    )
)

print(
    "Q_SPAN_MEDIAN=",
    float(
        np.median(Q_SPAN)
    ),
)

print(
    "TRUE_BEST_GAP_MEDIAN=",
    float(
        np.median(
            TRUE_BEST_GAP
        )
    ),
)

print(
    "PRED_Q_STD_RATIO_MEDIAN=",
    float(
        np.median(
            SCALE_RATIO
        )
    ),
)


# ============================================================

# ============================================================

    mapping_p
)

    "feature_mapping"
][
    "feature_names"

    "feature_mapping"

    for line

        encoding="utf-8"

    if line.strip()
]

    [
        [
            float(
                get_path(
                    root,
                    feature_paths[
                        name
                    ],
                )
            )
            for name
            in feature_names
        ]
        for root in roots
    ],
    dtype=np.float64,
)

assert X.shape == (135, 12)
assert np.isfinite(X).all()

root_steps_jsonl = np.asarray(
    [
        int(
            root[
                "root_step"
            ]
        )
        for root in roots

    dtype=np.int64,

    ROOT_STEP,
    root_steps_jsonl,

with np.load(
    norm_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    norm_names = [
        str(x)
        for x in z[
            "feature_names"
        ].tolist()
    ]

    mean = np.asarray(
        z["feature_mean"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    std = np.asarray(
        z["feature_std"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

assert norm_names == feature_names
assert mean.shape == (12,)
assert std.shape == (12,)
assert (std > 0).all()

Z = (
    X
    - mean[None, :]
) / std[None, :]

ABS_Z = np.abs(Z)

ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z = (
    ABS_Z.max(axis=1)
)

ROOT_MEAN_ABS_Z = (
    ABS_Z.mean(axis=1)
)

root_over_2 = (
    ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z > 2.0
)

root_over_3 = (
    ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z > 3.0
)

root_over_5 = (
    ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z > 5.0
)

print(
    "ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z_GT2_RATE=",
    float(
        root_over_2.mean()
    ),
)

print(
    "ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z_GT3_RATE=",
    float(
        root_over_3.mean()
    ),
)

print(
    "ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z_GT5_RATE=",
    float(
        root_over_5.mean()
    ),
)

print(
    "ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z_MAX=",
    float(
        ROOT_MAX_ABS_Z.max()
    ),
)


feature_ood = {}

for j, name in enumerate(
    feature_names
):
    z = ABS_Z[:, j]

    feature_ood[name] = {
        "mean_abs_z":
            float(
                z.mean()
            ),

        "median_abs_z":
            float(
                np.median(z)
            ),

        "max_abs_z":
            float(
                z.max()
            ),

        "gt2_rate":
            float(
                np.mean(
                    z > 2.0
                )
            ),

        "gt3_rate":
            float(
                np.mean(
                    z > 3.0
                )
            ),

        "gt5_rate":
            float(
                np.mean(
                    z > 5.0
                )
            ),
    }

associations = {

# ============================================================
# 10. Freeze observational diagnostic
# ============================================================

payload = {
    "type":
        "C03_VALUE_V2_FAILURE_DIAGNOSTIC_V1",

        "PASS_OBSERVATIONAL_FAILURE_DIAGNOSTIC",

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"

        false

思考了 20s
当前资源：低资源即可，不开高卡。

这不是新的科学失败，而是我上一版诊断代码的数值类型处理过严：冻结 prediction artifact 本身是 float32，但诊断脚本加载后强制转成了 float64 再重算 metric，导致 normalized_regret 出现约 1.27×10⁻⁹ 的浮点差异。先只把这个原因锁死，不 forward、不训练、不重算模型预测。

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim

(
set -eu

    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

    echo "FAIL PRED_SHA"
    false

    false

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import math
import numpy as np

pred_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
result_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
metric_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[4])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


result = json.loads(
    result_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert result["status"] == "SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"
assert result["model_forward_call_count"] == 1
assert result["prediction_shape"] == [135, 16]

with np.load(
    pred_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    # IMPORTANT:
    # Preserve frozen artifact dtype exactly.
    Q32 = np.asarray(
        z["q_true"]
    )

    P32 = np.asarray(
        z["q_centered_pred"]
    )

    IMM32 = np.asarray(
        z["immediate_reward"]
    )

print("PRED_DTYPE=", P32.dtype)
print("IMMEDIATE_DTYPE=", IMM32.dtype)

assert Q32.dtype == np.float32
assert P32.dtype == np.float32
assert IMM32.dtype == np.float32

assert Q32.shape == (135, 16)
assert P32.shape == (135, 16)
assert IMM32.shape == (135, 16)

print("FROZEN_ARRAY_DTYPE_GATE=PASS")


namespace = {}

exec(
    compile(
        metric_p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        ),
        str(metric_p),
        "exec",
    ),
    namespace,
)

ranking_metrics = namespace[
    "ranking_metrics"
]


# ============================================================
# A. Exact frozen-dtype reproduction
# ============================================================

m32 = ranking_metrics(
    Q32,
    P32,
    IMM32,
)

expected = result[
    "metrics"
]

print()
print("=== FLOAT32 EXACT REPRODUCTION ===")

for key in (
    "root_count",
    "mean_root_spearman",
    "top1",
    "top3_contains_true_best",
    "normalized_regret",
    "immediate_regret",
):
    print(
        f"FLOAT32_{key}={m32[key]}"
    )

    print(
        f"FROZEN_{key}={expected[key]}"
    )


nr32 = float(
    m32["normalized_regret"]
)

nr_expected = float(
    expected["normalized_regret"]
)

ir32 = float(
    m32["immediate_regret"]
)

ir_expected = float(
    expected["immediate_regret"]
)

nr_delta32 = (
    nr32
    - nr_expected
)

ir_delta32 = (
    ir32
    - ir_expected
)

print(
    "FLOAT32_NORMALIZED_REGRET_DELTA="
    + repr(nr_delta32)
)

print(
    "FLOAT32_IMMEDIATE_REGRET_DELTA="
    + repr(ir_delta32)
)


# Exact metric reproduction should match frozen execution
# to numerical roundoff only.
assert math.isclose(
    nr32,
    nr_expected,
    rel_tol=0.0,
    abs_tol=1e-12,
), (
    "FLOAT32_NORMALIZED_REGRET_MISMATCH",
    nr32,
    nr_expected,
)

assert math.isclose(
    ir32,
    ir_expected,
    rel_tol=0.0,
    abs_tol=1e-12,
), (
    "FLOAT32_IMMEDIATE_REGRET_MISMATCH",
    ir32,
    ir_expected,
)

for key in (
    "mean_root_spearman",
    "top1",
    "top3_contains_true_best",
):
    assert math.isclose(
        float(m32[key]),
        float(expected[key]),
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=1e-12,
    ), key

assert int(
    m32["root_count"]
) == int(
    expected["root_count"]
)

print(
    "FROZEN_FLOAT32_METRIC_REPRODUCTION=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# B. Reproduce previous diagnostic's float64 conversion
# ============================================================

Q64 = Q32.astype(
    np.float64
)

P64 = P32.astype(
    np.float64
)

IMM64 = IMM32.astype(
    np.float64
)

    Q64,
    P64,
    IMM64,
)

    m64["normalized_regret"]
)

ir64 = float(
    m64["immediate_regret"]
)

print("=== FLOAT64 CONVERSION EFFECT ===")

    "FLOAT64_NORMALIZED_REGRET="
    + repr(nr64)
)

print(
    "FLOAT64_IMMEDIATE_REGRET="
    + repr(ir64)
)

    "FLOAT64_VS_FROZEN_NORMALIZED_DELTA="
    + repr(
        nr64
        - nr_expected
    )
)

print(
    "FLOAT64_VS_FLOAT32_NORMALIZED_DELTA="
    + repr(
        nr64
        - nr32
    )
)


# Verify the observed failure value from the previous
# diagnostic is explained by dtype conversion.
previous_observed = (
    0.3506035805292045
)

assert math.isclose(
    nr64,
    previous_observed,
    rel_tol=0.0,
    abs_tol=1e-15,
), (
    "PREVIOUS_MISMATCH_NOT_EXPLAINED",
    nr64,
    previous_observed,
)

print(
    "PREVIOUS_DIAGNOSTIC_MISMATCH_EXPLAINED=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# C. Scientific decision must remain invariant
# ============================================================

assert (
    nr32
    > ir32
)

assert (
    nr64
    > ir64
)

assert (
    result["primary_gate_pass"]
    is False
)

assert (
    result["decision"]
    ==

)

    "SCIENTIFIC_DECISION_INVARIANT=PASS"
)


payload = {
    "type":
        "C03_VALUE_V2_FAILURE_DIAGNOSTIC_NUMERIC_REPRO_V1",

    "status":
        "PASS_NUMERIC_DTYPE_ROOT_CAUSE",

        false
    }
}

echo "=== C03 VALUE V2 FAILURE DIAGNOSTIC V2 ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$PRED" "$EXPECTED_PRED" PREDICTION
check_sha "$RESULT" "$EXPECTED_RESULT" RESULT
check_sha "$FREEZE" "$EXPECTED_FREEZE" FREEZE
check_sha "$FINAL" "$EXPECTED_FINAL" FINAL_DECISION

check_sha "$NUMERIC_LOCK" "$EXPECTED_NUMERIC_LOCK" NUMERIC_REPRO_LOCK

check_sha "$MAPPING" "$EXPECTED_MAPPING" FEATURE_MAPPING
check_sha "$NORM" "$EXPECTED_NORM" NORMALIZATION
check_sha "$METRIC" "$EXPECTED_METRIC" METRIC_CLOSURE

[ -f "$ROOT_JSONL" ] || {
    echo "FAIL ROOT_JSONL_MISSING"
    false
}

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    echo "EXISTING_OUTPUT_SHA=$(sha "$OUT")"
    false
}

python - \
"$PRED" \
"$RESULT" \
"$FREEZE" \
"$FINAL" \
"$NUMERIC_LOCK" \
"$ROOT_JSONL" \
"$MAPPING" \
"$NORM" \
"$METRIC" \
"$OUT" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import math
import numpy as np

(
    pred_p,
    result_p,
    freeze_p,
    final_p,
    numeric_p,
    roots_p,
    mapping_p,
    norm_p,
    metric_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def read_json(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


def get_path(obj, path):
    cur = obj

    for key in path:
        cur = cur[key]

    return cur


def quantiles(x):
    x = np.asarray(
        x,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    return {
        "min": float(np.min(x)),
        "p05": float(np.quantile(x, 0.05)),
        "p25": float(np.quantile(x, 0.25)),
        "median": float(np.quantile(x, 0.50)),
        "p75": float(np.quantile(x, 0.75)),
        "p95": float(np.quantile(x, 0.95)),
        "max": float(np.max(x)),
        "mean": float(np.mean(x)),
    }


def safe_pearson(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(
        a,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    b = np.asarray(
        b,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    mask = (
        np.isfinite(a)
        & np.isfinite(b)
    )

    a = a[mask]
    b = b[mask]

    if len(a) < 2:
        return None

    a = a - a.mean()
    b = b - b.mean()

    denom = math.sqrt(
        float(
            np.sum(a * a)
            * np.sum(b * b)
        )
    )

    if denom <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0

    return float(
        np.sum(a * b)
        / denom
    )


def normalized_entropy(counts):
    counts = np.asarray(
        counts,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    p = (
        counts
        / counts.sum()
    )

    p = p[
        p > 0
    ]

    h = -float(
        np.sum(
            p * np.log(p)
        )
    )

    return (
        h
        / math.log(16.0)
    )


# ============================================================
# 1. Frozen negative decision
# ============================================================

result = read_json(
    result_p
)

freeze = read_json(
    freeze_p
)

final = read_json(
    final_p

numeric = read_json(
    numeric_p

assert result["status"] == "SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"

assert (

    ==

    is False

    == 1
)

    is False
)

assert (

    == "CLOSED_SCIENTIFIC_FAIL"

assert (

    == "DO_NOT_INTEGRATE_VALUE_V2_INTO_MCTS"
)

assert (
    final["protocol"][
        "one_time_protocol_consumed"
    ]
    is True

assert (

        "retuning_on_C03_authorized"
    ]
    is False

assert (
    numeric["status"]
    == "PASS_NUMERIC_DTYPE_ROOT_CAUSE"

assert (
    numeric[
        "science_boundary"
    ][
        "scientific_fail_unchanged"
    ]
    is True
)

print(
    "FROZEN_NEGATIVE_DECISION_GATE=PASS"
)

print(
    "NUMERIC_REPRO_GATE=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Frozen prediction artifact
#    KEEP ORIGINAL FLOAT32 FOR ALL EXACT METRICS
# ============================================================

with np.load(
    pred_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    ROOT_STEP = np.asarray(
        z["root_step"]
    )

    Q32 = np.asarray(
        z["q_true"]
    )

    QC32 = np.asarray(
        z["q_centered_true"]
    )

    P32 = np.asarray(
        z["q_centered_pred"]
    )

    IMM32 = np.asarray(
        z["immediate_reward"]
    )


assert ROOT_STEP.dtype == np.int64

assert Q32.dtype == np.float32
assert QC32.dtype == np.float32
assert P32.dtype == np.float32
assert IMM32.dtype == np.float32

assert ROOT_STEP.shape == (135,)
assert Q32.shape == (135, 16)
assert QC32.shape == (135, 16)
assert P32.shape == (135, 16)
assert IMM32.shape == (135, 16)

assert np.isfinite(Q32).all()
assert np.isfinite(QC32).all()
assert np.isfinite(P32).all()
assert np.isfinite(IMM32).all()

print(
    "PREDICTION_ARTIFACT_SCHEMA=PASS"

# ============================================================
# 3. Exact metric reproduction
# ============================================================

metric_ns = {}

exec(
    compile(
        metric_p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        ),
        str(metric_p),
        "exec",
    ),
    metric_ns,
)

ranking_metrics = (
    metric_ns[
        "ranking_metrics"
    ]
)

global_metrics = ranking_metrics(
    Q32,
    P32,
    IMM32,
)

expected_metrics = (
    result[
        "metrics"
    ]
)

for key in (
    "root_count",
    "mean_root_spearman",
    "top1",
    "top3_contains_true_best",
    "normalized_regret",
    "immediate_regret",
):

    a = float(
        global_metrics[key]
    )

    b = float(
        expected_metrics[key]
    )

    assert math.isclose(
        a,
        b,
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=1e-12,
    ), (
        "GLOBAL_METRIC_MISMATCH",
        key,
        a,
        b,
    )


print(
    "FROZEN_FLOAT32_METRIC_REPRODUCTION=PASS"

# ============================================================
# 4. Per-root exact regret decomposition
# ============================================================

model_regret = []
immediate_regret = []
root_spearman = []

for i in range(135):

    m = ranking_metrics(
        Q32[i:i+1],
        P32[i:i+1],
        IMM32[i:i+1],
    )

    model_regret.append(
        float(
            m[
                "normalized_regret"
            ]
        )
    )

    immediate_regret.append(
        float(
            m[
                "immediate_regret"
            ]
        )
    )

    root_spearman.append(
        float(
            m[
                "mean_root_spearman"
            ]
        )
    )


model_regret = np.asarray(
    model_regret,
    dtype=np.float64,
)

immediate_regret = np.asarray(
    immediate_regret,
    dtype=np.float64,
)

root_spearman = np.asarray(
    root_spearman,
    dtype=np.float64,
)

regret_delta = (
    model_regret
    - immediate_regret
)

EPS = 1e-12

better = (
    regret_delta
    < -EPS
)

worse = (
    regret_delta
    > EPS
)

equal = ~(
    better
    | worse
)


print(
    "MODEL_BETTER_ROOT_COUNT="
    + str(
        int(
            better.sum()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_WORSE_ROOT_COUNT="
    + str(
        int(
            worse.sum()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_EQUAL_ROOT_COUNT="
    + str(
        int(
            equal.sum()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_BETTER_ROOT_RATE="
    + repr(
        float(
            better.mean()
        )
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 5. Action-choice behavior
# ============================================================

TRUE_BEST = np.argmax(
    Q32,
    axis=1,
)

MODEL_BEST = np.argmax(
    P32,
    axis=1,
)

IMM_BEST = np.argmax(
    IMM32,
    axis=1,
)


true_counts = np.bincount(
    TRUE_BEST,
    minlength=16,
)

model_counts = np.bincount(
    MODEL_BEST,
    minlength=16,
)

imm_counts = np.bincount(
    IMM_BEST,
    minlength=16,
)


model_top1 = float(
    np.mean(
        MODEL_BEST
        == TRUE_BEST
    )
)

imm_top1 = float(
    np.mean(
        IMM_BEST
        == TRUE_BEST
    )
)

same_action = (
    MODEL_BEST
    == IMM_BEST
)

different = ~same_action


row = np.arange(
    135
)

model_selected_q = (
    Q32[
        row,
        MODEL_BEST,
    ]
)

imm_selected_q = (
    Q32[
        row,
        IMM_BEST,
    ]
)


diff_model_better = (
    different
    & (
        model_selected_q
        > imm_selected_q
    )
)

diff_model_worse = (
    different
    & (
        model_selected_q
        < imm_selected_q
    )
)

diff_equal = (
    different
    & ~(
        diff_model_better
        | diff_model_worse
    )
)


model_max_action = int(
    np.argmax(
        model_counts
    )
)

model_max_share = float(
    model_counts[
        model_max_action
    ]
    / 135.0
)


print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_RATE="
    + repr(
        model_top1
    )
)

print(
    "IMMEDIATE_TOP1_RATE="
    + repr(
        imm_top1
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_IMMEDIATE_SAME_ACTION_RATE="
    + repr(
        float(
            same_action.mean()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_UNIQUE_TOP1_ACTIONS="
    + str(
        int(
            np.count_nonzero(
                model_counts
            )
        )
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_MAX_ACTION="
    + str(
        model_max_action
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_MAX_ACTION_SHARE="
    + repr(
        model_max_share
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_TOP1_ENTROPY_NORMALIZED="
    + repr(
        normalized_entropy(
            model_counts
        )
    )
)

print(
    "TRUE_BEST_ACTION_COUNTS="
    + repr(
        true_counts.tolist()
    )
)

print(
    "MODEL_BEST_ACTION_COUNTS="
    + repr(
        model_counts.tolist()
    )
)

print(
    "IMMEDIATE_BEST_ACTION_COUNTS="
    + repr(
        imm_counts.tolist()
    )
)

print(
    "WHEN_ACTION_DIFF_MODEL_BETTER_COUNT="
    + str(
        int(
            diff_model_better.sum()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "WHEN_ACTION_DIFF_MODEL_WORSE_COUNT="
    + str(
        int(
            diff_model_worse.sum()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "WHEN_ACTION_DIFF_EQUAL_COUNT="
    + str(
        int(
            diff_equal.sum()
        )
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 6. Q/prediction scale + target ambiguity
#    Descriptive analysis only; float64 conversion is fine here.
# ============================================================

Q = Q32.astype(
    np.float64
)

P = P32.astype(
    np.float64
)

IMM = IMM32.astype(
    np.float64
)


Q_SPAN = (
    np.max(
        Q,
        axis=1,
    )
    - np.min(
        Q,
        axis=1,
    )
)

Q_STD = np.std(
    Q,
    axis=1,
)

P_SPAN = (
    np.max(
        P,
        axis=1,
    )
    - np.min(
        P,
        axis=1,
    )
)

P_STD = np.std(
    P,
    axis=1,
)


sorted_q = np.sort(
    Q,
    axis=1,
)

TRUE_BEST_GAP = (
    sorted_q[:, -1]
    - sorted_q[:, -2]
)


PRED_Q_STD_RATIO = (
    P_STD
    / np.maximum(
        Q_STD,
        1e-12,
    )
)


print(
    "Q_SPAN_MEDIAN="
    + repr(
        float(
            np.median(
                Q_SPAN
            )
        )
    )
)

print(
    "Q_STD_MEDIAN="
    + repr(
        float(
            np.median(
                Q_STD
            )
        )
    )
)

print(
    "TRUE_BEST_GAP_MEDIAN="
    + repr(
        float(
            np.median(
                TRUE_BEST_GAP
            )
        )
    )
)

print(
    "PRED_STD_MEDIAN="
    + repr(
        float(
            np.median(
                P_STD
            )
        )
    )
)

print(
    "PRED_Q_STD_RATIO_MEDIAN="
    + repr(
        float(
            np.median(
                PRED_Q_STD_RATIO
            )
        )
    )
)

    std

    std > 0
).all()


Z = (
    X
    - mean[None, :]
) / std[None, :]

    Z
)

    ABS_Z,
    axis=1,

    ABS_Z,

    ROOT_MAX_Z > 2.0

    ROOT_MAX_Z > 3.0

        float(
            root_gt3.mean()
        )
    )

for j, name in enumerate(

                    v > 5.0
                )
            ),

top_ood = sorted(

    key=lambda kv: (
        kv[1][

        ],
        kv[1][
            "max_abs_z"

    reverse=True,

print(

# ============================================================
# 10. Freeze diagnostic
# ============================================================

payload = {
    "type":
        "C03_VALUE_V2_FAILURE_DIAGNOSTIC_V2",

    "status":
        "PASS_OBSERVATIONAL_FAILURE_DIAGNOSTIC",

    "scene":
        "C03",

    "scope":
        (
            "Offline diagnostic using the already-frozen "
            "C03 prediction artifact. Exact ranking metrics "
            "preserve frozen float32 input dtype. No model "
            "forward, training, retuning or MCTS integration."
        ),

    "input_sha256": {
        "prediction":
            sha(pred_p),

        "result":
            sha(result_p),

        "freeze":
            sha(freeze_p),

        "final_decision":
            sha(final_p),

        "numeric_reproduction":
            sha(numeric_p),

        "root_jsonl":
            sha(roots_p),

        "feature_mapping":
            sha(mapping_p),

        "normalization":
            sha(norm_p),

        "metric_closure":
            sha(metric_p),
    },

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false

=== REPO ===
112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
(minesim) root@autodl-container-02b744a905-1841eb5b:~/MineSim-Dynamic# 

思考了 1m 17s
这一步已经足够把失败模式分层了：

不是简单的 action-head 全塌缩：模型仍使用 11/16 个动作，归一化熵 0.690。

但存在明显 action-head 分布错配：真实最优主要集中在 15/14/11，而模型大量选 8/12/3。

最直接的问题是“过度覆盖 baseline”：135 个 root 中模型与 immediate 只有 1 个相同动作；其余 134 个里，模型只改善 32 个，却恶化 102 个。

预测尺度明显过大：median pred_std=5.347，而真实 Q_std=0.697，比例 3.34×。这很像“网络修正过激/过度自信”，而不是保守地改善 immediate。

C03 的 feature shift 也很强：100% root 至少一个 feature |z|>2，91.1% >3，主要是 heading 和 delta-v。但 regret_delta vs max_abs_z=-0.247，所以目前只能说 OOD 是场景迁移事实，不能说它单独导致失败。

regret_delta vs root_spearman=-0.562 说明 ranking 变差时 regret 通常同步恶化，但这是表现关联，不是根因。

而且 C04/C11/C06 已经属于 development evidence，Value V2 final-dev 就是在这三场景上冻结的；不能拿已经揭盲的 C03 来重新选 target/调参。

 早期设计里确实已经规划过 root-centered Q / root-zscore Q / residual-to-immediate 三种 target，因此现在最重要的是先查清 这些 development probe 到底实际跑到了哪一步、final-dev 为什么最终选 ROOT_CENTERED_Q，不能重复做已经做过的实验。


当前资源：低资源。若还在高资源实例，可以关掉。

下一步只做 Value V2 development provenance audit，不训练、不 forward、不碰 C03 指标。

python - "$PREP" "$MODEL" "$PARENT" "$FREEZE" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import ast
import re

prep = Path(sys.argv[1])
model = Path(sys.argv[2])
parent_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
freeze_p = Path(sys.argv[4])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def walk(obj, path=""):
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        for k, v in obj.items():
            p = f"{path}.{k}" if path else str(k)
            yield from walk(v, p)

    elif isinstance(obj, list):
        for i, v in enumerate(obj):
            yield from walk(
                v,
                f"{path}[{i}]"
            )

    else:
        yield path, obj


# ============================================================
# 1. Frozen final-dev contract
# ============================================================

freeze = json.loads(
    freeze_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

print()
print("=== FINAL-DEV FREEZE ===")

for k, v in walk(freeze):
    lk = k.lower()

    if any(
        x in lk
        for x in (
            "status",
            "target",
            "architecture",
            "training_scene",
            "checkpoint",
            "normalization",
            "development",
            "ready",
            "decision",
            "regret",
            "spearman",
            "top1",
            "epoch",
            "seed",
        )
    ):
        print(f"{k} = {v}")

print("FINAL_DEV_FREEZE_READ=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Inspect authoritative V2 source contract
# ============================================================

src = parent_p.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8"
)

tree = ast.parse(
    src
)

print()
print("=== AUTHORITATIVE V2 SOURCE CONSTANTS ===")

for node in tree.body:

    if not isinstance(
        node,
        (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign),
    ):
        continue

    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
        targets = node.targets
        value = node.value
    else:
        targets = [node.target]
        value = node.value

    for t in targets:

        if not isinstance(
            t,
            ast.Name,
        ):
            continue

        name = t.id

        if any(
            x in name.upper()
            for x in (
                "TARGET",
                "FEATURE",
                "HIDDEN",
                "EPOCH",
                "SEED",
                "SCENE",
                "TRAIN",
                "LR",
            )
        ):
            try:
                val = ast.literal_eval(
                    value
                )
            except Exception:
                continue

            print(
                f"{name} = {val}"
            )


# ============================================================
# 3. Locate actual result/output paths encoded in source
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== SOURCE PATH / OUTPUT REFERENCES ===")

path_strings = []

for node in ast.walk(tree):

    if not isinstance(
        node,
        ast.Constant,
    ):
        continue

    if not isinstance(
        node.value,
        str,
    ):
        continue

    s = node.value

    sl = s.lower()

    if (
        "value_v2" in sl
        or "loso" in sl
        or "interaction" in sl
        or "target_probe" in sl
        or "development" in sl
        or "final_dev" in sl
    ):
        path_strings.append(s)

for s in sorted(
    set(path_strings)
):
    print(
        "SOURCE_STRING=",
        repr(s),
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Narrow filesystem inventory:
#    only the two known V2 workspaces.
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== KNOWN WORKSPACE FILE INVENTORY ===")

allowed_suffix = {
    ".json",
    ".txt",
    ".log",
    ".csv",
    ".npz",
    ".pt",
    ".py",
    ".sh",
    ".zip",
}

files = []

for base in (
    prep,
    model,
):

    if not base.is_dir():
        continue

    for p in base.rglob("*"):

        if not p.is_file():
            continue

        if p.suffix.lower() not in allowed_suffix:
            continue

        files.append(p)


files = sorted(
    set(files),
    key=lambda p: str(p),
)

print(
    "KNOWN_WORKSPACE_FILE_COUNT=",
    len(files),
)

for p in files:

    try:
        size = p.stat().st_size
        digest = sha(p)
    except Exception as e:
        print(
            "FILE_ERROR=",
            p,
            repr(e),
        )
        continue

    print()
    print("FILE=", p)
    print("SIZE=", size)
    print("SHA256=", digest)


# ============================================================
# 5. Parse only small JSON evidence in known workspaces
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== JSON DEVELOPMENT EVIDENCE ===")

json_candidates = [
    p
    for p in files
    if p.suffix.lower() == ".json"
    and p.stat().st_size <= 2_000_000
]

interesting = (
    "regret",
    "immediate",
    "spearman",
    "top1",
    "top3",
    "win",
    "ready",
    "decision",
    "target",
    "candidate",
    "heldout",
    "held_out",
    "fold",
    "scene",
    "development",
    "validation",
    "checkpoint",
)

for p in json_candidates:

    try:
        obj = json.loads(
            p.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )
    except Exception:
        continue

    hits = []

    for k, v in walk(obj):

        if any(
            token in k.lower()
            for token in interesting
        ):
            hits.append(
                (k, v)
            )

    if not hits:
        continue

    print()
    print(
        "JSON_EVIDENCE_FILE=",
        p,
    )

    print(
        "JSON_EVIDENCE_SHA256=",
        sha(p),
    )

    for k, v in hits[:150]:
        print(
            f"{k} = {v}"
        )


# ============================================================
# 6. Search source for target/probe/selection logic
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== SOURCE DECISION LOGIC ===")

patterns = (
    r"ROOT_CENTERED_Q",
    r"ROOT_ZSCORE",
    r"RESIDUAL",
    r"immediate",
    r"development_ready",
    r"win_count",
    r"DECISION",
    r"target",
)

lines = src.splitlines()

hit_lines = set()

for i, line in enumerate(
    lines,
    1,
):
    if any(
        re.search(
            pat,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        )
        for pat in patterns
    ):
        hit_lines.add(i)

ranges = []

for n in sorted(
    hit_lines
):
    a = max(
        1,
        n - 3,
    )

    b = min(
        len(lines),
        n + 5,
    )

    if (
        ranges
        and a <= ranges[-1][1] + 1
    ):
        ranges[-1] = (
            ranges[-1][0],
            max(
                ranges[-1][1],
                b,
            ),
        )
    else:
        ranges.append(
            (a, b)
        )

for a, b in ranges[:25]:

    print()
    print(
        f"--- SOURCE LINES {a}-{b} ---"
    )

    for i in range(
        a,
        b + 1,
    ):
        print(
            f"{i}: {lines[i-1]}"
        )


# ============================================================
# 7. Classification
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== FINAL ===")

print(
    "C03_USED_FOR_THIS_AUDIT=False"
)

print(
    "TRAINING_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MODEL_RETUNING_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"
)

print(
    "DEVELOPMENT_PROVENANCE_AUDIT=PASS"
)

print(
    "NEXT=INTERPRET_EXISTING_V2_DEVELOPMENT_EVIDENCE"
)

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import zipfile

(
    probe_zip,
    true_summary_p,
    true_manifest_p,
    true_zip,
    final_recipe_p,
    final_manifest_p,
    final_freeze_p,
    expected_probe_sha,
) = sys.argv[1:]

probe_zip = Path(probe_zip)
true_summary_p = Path(true_summary_p)
true_manifest_p = Path(true_manifest_p)
true_zip = Path(true_zip)
final_recipe_p = Path(final_recipe_p)
final_manifest_p = Path(final_manifest_p)
final_freeze_p = Path(final_freeze_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def bytes_sha(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        b
    ).hexdigest()


def read_json(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


def read_zip_json(zip_path, member):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(
        zip_path,
        "r",
    ) as z:

        names = z.namelist()

        if member not in names:
            return None, None

        raw = z.read(
            member
        )

        return (
            json.loads(
                raw.decode(
                    "utf-8"
                )
            ),
            raw,
        )


def walk(obj, path=""):
    if isinstance(obj, dict):

        for k, v in obj.items():

            p = (
                f"{path}.{k}"
                if path
                else str(k)
            )

            yield from walk(
                v,
                p,
            )

    elif isinstance(obj, list):

        for i, v in enumerate(
            obj
        ):

            yield from walk(
                v,
                f"{path}[{i}]",
            )

    else:
        yield path, obj


def relevant_print(
    title,
    obj,
):
    print()
    print(
        "=== "
        + title
        + " ==="
    )

    tokens = (
        "candidate",
        "target",
        "regret",
        "immediate",
        "win",
        "decision",
        "ready",
        "heldout",
        "held_out",
        "scene",
        "spearman",
        "top1",
        "top3",
        "status",
        "development",
    )

    for k, v in walk(obj):

        if any(
            token in k.lower()
            for token in tokens
        ):
            print(
                f"{k} = {v}"
            )


# ============================================================
# 1. Exact historical target probe
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== TARGET PROBE ARTIFACT ===")

print(
    "PROBE_ZIP_EXISTS=",
    probe_zip.is_file(),
)

probe = None
probe_raw = None
probe_status = False

if probe_zip.is_file():

    probe_sha = sha(
        probe_zip
    )

    print(
        "PROBE_ZIP_SHA256=",
        probe_sha,
    )

    print(
        "PROBE_EXPECTED_SHA256=",
        expected_probe_sha,
    )

    if (
        probe_sha
        != expected_probe_sha
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "PROBE_ZIP_SHA_MISMATCH"
        )

    print(
        "PROBE_ZIP_SHA_GATE=PASS"
    )

    member = (
        "results/"
        "paper1_value_network_v2_interaction_target_probe_v1_summary.json"
    )

    probe, probe_raw = (
        read_zip_json(
            probe_zip,
            member,
        )
    )

    if probe is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "PROBE_SUMMARY_MEMBER_MISSING"
        )

    print(
        "PROBE_SUMMARY_MEMBER_SHA256=",
        bytes_sha(
            probe_raw
        ),
    )

    relevant_print(
        "PROBE SUMMARY",
        probe,
    )

    assert (
        probe.get(
            "status"
        )
        == "PASS"
    )

    assert (
        probe.get(
            "BEST_CANDIDATE"
        )
        == "ROOT_CENTERED_Q"
    )

    assert (
        int(
            probe.get(
                "BEST_CANDIDATE_IMMEDIATE_REGRET_WIN_COUNT"
            )
        )
        == 2
    )

    assert (
        probe.get(
            "DECISION"
        )
        ==
        "PROCEED_TO_TRUE_VALUE_NETWORK_V2_NEURAL_DESIGN"
    )

    probe_text = json.dumps(
        probe,
        sort_keys=True,
    ).upper()

    print(
        "ROOT_CENTERED_Q_PROBED=",
        "ROOT_CENTERED_Q"
        in probe_text,
    )

    print(
        "ROOT_ZSCORE_PROBED=",
        (
            "ROOT_ZSCORE"
            in probe_text
            or "ROOT_Z_SCORE"
            in probe_text
        ),
    )

    print(
        "RESIDUAL_TO_IMMEDIATE_PROBED=",
        (
            "RESIDUAL"
            in probe_text
            and "IMMEDIATE"
            in probe_text
        ),
    )

    probe_status = True

    print(
        "TARGET_PROBE_GATE=PASS"
    )


# ============================================================
# 2. True-neural LOSO result
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== TRUE NEURAL LOSO ARTIFACT ===")

print(
    "TRUE_SUMMARY_FILE_EXISTS=",
    true_summary_p.is_file(),
)

print(
    "TRUE_MANIFEST_FILE_EXISTS=",
    true_manifest_p.is_file(),
)

print(
    "TRUE_UPLOAD_ZIP_EXISTS=",
    true_zip.is_file(),
)


summary_sources = []

if true_summary_p.is_file():

    raw = true_summary_p.read_bytes()

    summary_sources.append(
        (
            "directory",
            json.loads(
                raw.decode(
                    "utf-8"
                )
            ),
            raw,
        )
    )

    print(
        "TRUE_SUMMARY_FILE_SHA256=",
        sha(
            true_summary_p
        ),
    )


if true_zip.is_file():

        sha(
            true_zip
        ),

    with zipfile.ZipFile(
        true_zip,
        "r",
    ) as z:

        names = z.namelist()

        print(
            "TRUE_UPLOAD_ZIP_MEMBER_COUNT=",
            len(names),
        )

        member = (
            "results/"
            "paper1_value_network_v2_true_neural_loso_v1_summary.json"
        )

        if member in names:

            raw = z.read(
                member
            )

            summary_sources.append(
                (
                    "upload_zip",
                    json.loads(
                        raw.decode(
                            "utf-8"
                        )
                    ),
                    raw,
                )
            )

            print(
                "TRUE_ZIP_SUMMARY_SHA256=",
                bytes_sha(
                    raw
                ),
            )


if not summary_sources:

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_SUMMARY_RESOLUTION=HOLD_NOT_FOUND"
    )

    true_status = False
    true_obj = None

else:

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_SUMMARY_SOURCE_COUNT=",
        len(
            summary_sources
        ),
    )

    summary_hashes = {
        bytes_sha(raw)
        for _, _, raw
        in summary_sources
    }

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_SUMMARY_UNIQUE_SHA_COUNT=",
        len(
            summary_hashes
        ),
    )

    if len(
        summary_hashes
    ) != 1:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "TRUE_NEURAL_SUMMARY_SOURCE_MISMATCH"
        )

    true_obj = (
        summary_sources[0][1]
    )

    relevant_print(
        "TRUE NEURAL SUMMARY",
        true_obj,
    )

    print()
    print(
        "=== TRUE NEURAL FOLD METRICS ==="
    )

    folds = true_obj.get(
        "fold_metrics",
        [],
    )

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_FOLD_COUNT=",
        len(folds),
    )

    for i, fold in enumerate(
        folds
    ):

        print(
            f"FOLD_{i}_JSON="
            + json.dumps(
                fold,
                sort_keys=True,
                ensure_ascii=False,
            )
        )

    wins = true_obj.get(
        "immediate_regret_win_count"
    )

    ready = true_obj.get(
        "V2_NEURAL_DEVELOPMENT_READY"
    )

    decision = true_obj.get(
        "DECISION"
    )

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_WIN_COUNT=",
        wins,
    )

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_DEVELOPMENT_READY=",
        ready,
    )

    print(
        "TRUE_NEURAL_DECISION=",
        decision,
    )

    true_status = (
        true_obj.get(
            "status"
        )
        == "PASS"
        and true_obj.get(
            "development_only"
        )
        is True
        and isinstance(
            wins,
            int,
        )
        and wins >= 2
        and ready is True
        and decision
        ==
        "FREEZE_V2_ARCHITECTURE_CANDIDATE_AND_COLLECT_NEW_INDEPENDENT_VALIDATION"
    )

    print(

        + (
            "PASS"
            if true_status
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

if true_manifest_p.is_file():

    true_manifest = (
        read_json(
            true_manifest_p
        )
    )

    relevant_print(

        true_manifest,
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Final-dev recipe / training manifest binding
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== FINAL DEV PROVENANCE BINDING ===")

final_objects = []

for label, p in (
    (
        "FINAL_RECIPE",
        final_recipe_p,
    ),
    (
        "FINAL_MANIFEST",
        final_manifest_p,
    ),
    (
        "FINAL_FREEZE",
        final_freeze_p,
    ),
):

    if not p.is_file():
        print(
            f"{label}_EXISTS=False"
        )
        continue

    obj = read_json(
        p
    )

    final_objects.append(
        (
            label,
            p,

        )
    )

    print()

        "decision",

        "sha",

    for k, v in walk(obj):

            token in k.lower()
            for token in tokens

            )

    p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
    for _, p, _

    sha(
        true_zip
    )
    if true_zip.is_file()
    else None
)

if true_zip_sha:

        true_zip_sha
        in all_final_text,
    )

    summary_sha = next(
        iter(
            summary_hashes
        )
    )

    print(

EXPECTED_C03_FINAL=72efe529593e5307a6bfb7f074d20abe9b7a28acb2631d8185ef04e4af51658e
EXPECTED_C03_DIAG=a8bf4da54076676d2bda0984b174f2ab2bde6518110c9276606319969e40876f
EXPECTED_C03_NUMERIC=2c7429eabf24bdd2e4a9a51208b429aae6b85a6b2151f8064566a7f2e982c29a

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"

    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== VALUE V2 POST-FAILURE EVIDENCE CLOSURE ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$PROBE_ZIP" "$EXPECTED_PROBE_ZIP" PROBE_ZIP
check_sha "$TRUE_ZIP" "$EXPECTED_TRUE_ZIP" TRUE_NEURAL_ZIP
check_sha "$PARENT" "$EXPECTED_PARENT" PARENT

check_sha "$RECIPE" "$EXPECTED_RECIPE" FINAL_RECIPE
check_sha "$MANIFEST" "$EXPECTED_MANIFEST" FINAL_MANIFEST
check_sha "$MODEL_FREEZE" "$EXPECTED_MODEL_FREEZE" FINAL_MODEL_FREEZE

check_sha "$C03_RESULT" "$EXPECTED_C03_RESULT" C03_RESULT
check_sha "$C03_FINAL" "$EXPECTED_C03_FINAL" C03_FINAL
check_sha "$C03_DIAG" "$EXPECTED_C03_DIAG" C03_DIAGNOSTIC
check_sha "$C03_NUMERIC" "$EXPECTED_C03_NUMERIC" C03_NUMERIC

echo "IMMUTABLE_EVIDENCE_GATE=PASS"

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    echo "EXISTING_OUTPUT_SHA=$(sha "$OUT")"
    false
}

mkdir -p "$OUTDIR"

python - \
"$PROBE_ZIP" \
"$TRUE_ZIP" \
"$PARENT" \
"$RECIPE" \
"$MANIFEST" \
"$MODEL_FREEZE" \
"$C03_RESULT" \
"$C03_FINAL" \
"$C03_DIAG" \
"$C03_NUMERIC" \
"$OUT" \
"$EXPECTED_PROBE_SUMMARY" \
"$EXPECTED_TRUE_SUMMARY" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import zipfile
import math

(
    probe_zip,
    true_zip,
    parent_p,
    recipe_p,
    manifest_p,
    model_freeze_p,
    c03_result_p,
    c03_final_p,
    c03_diag_p,
    c03_numeric_p,
    out_p,
    expected_probe_summary,
    expected_true_summary,
) = sys.argv[1:]

probe_zip = Path(probe_zip)
true_zip = Path(true_zip)
parent_p = Path(parent_p)
recipe_p = Path(recipe_p)
manifest_p = Path(manifest_p)
model_freeze_p = Path(model_freeze_p)
c03_result_p = Path(c03_result_p)
c03_final_p = Path(c03_final_p)
c03_diag_p = Path(c03_diag_p)
c03_numeric_p = Path(c03_numeric_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def bsha(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        b
    ).hexdigest()


def read_json(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


def zip_json(zpath, member):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(
        zpath,
        "r",
    ) as z:
        raw = z.read(member)

    return (
        json.loads(
            raw.decode(
                "utf-8"
            )
        ),
        raw,
    )


# ============================================================
# 1. Target probe
# ============================================================

probe, probe_raw = zip_json(
    probe_zip,
    (
        "results/"
        "paper1_value_network_v2_interaction_target_probe_v1_summary.json"
    ),
)

assert bsha(
    probe_raw
) == expected_probe_summary

assert probe["status"] == "PASS"

assert probe[
    "design"
][
    "candidates"
] == [
    "ROOT_CENTERED_Q",
    "ROOT_ZSCORE_Q",
    "RESIDUAL_TO_IMMEDIATE",
]

cs = probe[
    "candidate_summary"
]

assert cs[
    "ROOT_CENTERED_Q"
][
    "win_count"
] == 2

    "ROOT_ZSCORE_Q"
][
    "win_count"
] == 1

    "RESIDUAL_TO_IMMEDIATE"
][
    "win_count"
] == 2

assert math.isclose(
    float(
        cs[
            "ROOT_CENTERED_Q"
        ][
            "mean_regret"
        ]
    ),
    0.20486448397131865,
    rel_tol=0.0,
    abs_tol=1e-15,
)

assert math.isclose(
    float(
        cs[
            "RESIDUAL_TO_IMMEDIATE"
        ][
            "mean_regret"
        ]
    ),
    0.209705957180386,
    rel_tol=0.0,
    abs_tol=1e-15,
)

assert (
    probe[
        "BEST_CANDIDATE"
    ]
    == "ROOT_CENTERED_Q"
)

assert (
    probe[
        "BEST_CANDIDATE_IMMEDIATE_REGRET_WIN_COUNT"

assert bsha(

    == "root_centered_q"

folds = true_summary[

by_scene = {

    for x in folds

    by_scene

c04_win = (

    ]

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import zipfile
import math

(
    probe_p,
    true_p,
    data_p,
    parent_p,
    out_p,
    expected_probe_summary,
    expected_true_summary,
) = sys.argv[1:]

probe_p = Path(probe_p)
true_p = Path(true_p)
data_p = Path(data_p)
parent_p = Path(parent_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def bsha(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        b
    ).hexdigest()


def read_zip_json(zpath, member):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(
        zpath,
        "r",
    ) as z:
        raw = z.read(member)

    return (
        json.loads(
            raw.decode("utf-8")
        ),
        raw,
    )

# ============================================================

    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)

    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}
