def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        path.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(
        path.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


def verify_inputs():
    if sha(PREREG) != EXPECTED_PREREG:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "PREREG_SHA"
        )

    if sha(DATA) != EXPECTED_DATA:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "DATA_SHA"
        )

    if sha(PARENT) != EXPECTED_PARENT:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "PARENT_SHA"
        )

    pre = read_json(
        PREREG
    )

    assert (
        pre["status"]
        == "FROZEN_BEFORE_VALUE_V3_TRAINING"
    )

    d = pre["V3_design"]

    assert (
        d["name"]
        == "ActionConditionedValueV3PairwiseRank"
    )

    assert (
        d["architecture"]
        == "12->64->64->16"
    )

    assert (
        d["input_features"]
        == list(FEATURES)
    )

    assert (
        d["architecture_change_from_V2"]
        is False
    )

    assert (
        d["feature_change_from_V2"]
        is False
    )

    assert (
        d["objective_change_only"]
        is True
    )

    assert (
        d["training_objective"]["name"]
        == "ROOT_NORMALIZED_WEIGHTED_PAIRWISE_LOGISTIC"
    )

    assert (
        d["training_objective"][
            "immediate_reward_used_in_training"
        ]
        is False
    )

    assert d["learning_rate"] == LR
    assert d["epochs"] == EPOCHS
    assert d["seeds"] == list(SEEDS)

    assert (
        pre["independent_validation_boundary"][
            "C03_available_for_tuning"
        ]
        is False
    )

    assert (
        pre["independent_validation_boundary"][
            "C13_available_for_tuning"
        ]
        is False
    )

    assert (
        pre["forbidden"][
            "select_best_seed"
        ]
        is True
    )

    return pre


def verify_parent_contract():
    src = PARENT.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    tree = ast.parse(
        src
    )

    constants = {}

    for node in tree.body:
        if (
            isinstance(node, ast.Assign)
            and len(node.targets) == 1
            and isinstance(
                node.targets[0],
                ast.Name,
            )
        ):
            try:
                constants[
                    node.targets[0].id
                ] = ast.literal_eval(
                    node.value
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

    parent_features = tuple(
        constants["FEATURES"]
    )

    assert (
        parent_features
        == FEATURES
    )

    assert (
        int(
            constants["HIDDEN"]
        )
        == 64
    )

    classes = [
        n
        for n in tree.body
        if (
            isinstance(
                n,
                ast.ClassDef,
            )
            and n.name
            == "ActionConditionedValueV2"
        )
    ]

    assert len(classes) == 1

    print(
        "PARENT_FEATURE_CONTRACT=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "PARENT_ARCHITECTURE_CONTRACT=PASS"
    )


def load_arrays():
    with np.load(
        DATA,
        allow_pickle=False,
    ) as z:

        keys = sorted(
            z.files
        )

        required = {
            "X",
            "q_value",
            "immediate_reward",
            "scene",
            "root_step",
            "action_A",
            "action_B",
        }

        missing = (
            required
            - set(keys)
        )

        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "DATASET_KEYS_MISSING:"
                + repr(
                    sorted(missing)
                )
            )

        arrays = {
            k: np.asarray(z[k])
            for k in required
        }

    print(
        "DATASET_KEYS="
        + repr(keys)
    )

    return arrays


def build_roots():
    arrays = load_arrays()

    X = np.asarray(
        arrays["X"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    q = np.asarray(
        arrays["q_value"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    imm = np.asarray(
        arrays["immediate_reward"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    scene = np.asarray(
        arrays["scene"]
    ).astype(str).reshape(-1)

    root_step = np.asarray(
        arrays["root_step"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_A = np.asarray(
        arrays["action_A"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_B = np.asarray(
        arrays["action_B"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    n = X.shape[0]

    assert X.ndim == 2
    assert X.shape[1] > max(FEATURE_INDICES)

    for arr in (
        q,
        imm,
        scene,
        root_step,
        action_A,
        action_B,
    ):
        assert len(arr) == n

    assert n == 18592

    assert np.isfinite(X).all()
    assert np.isfinite(q).all()
    assert np.isfinite(imm).all()

    assert set(
        np.unique(scene).tolist()
    ) == set(SCENES)

    roots = []

    for sc in SCENES:
        steps = np.unique(
            root_step[
                scene == sc
            ]
        )

        assert (
            len(steps)
            == EXPECTED_ROOT_COUNTS[sc]
        )

        for step in steps:
            rows = np.flatnonzero(
                (scene == sc)
                & (root_step == step)
            )

            assert len(rows) == 16

            joint = (
                action_A[rows] * 4
                + action_B[rows]
            )

            order = np.argsort(
                joint
            )

            rows = rows[order]
            joint = joint[order]

            assert np.array_equal(
                joint,
                np.arange(
                    16,
                    dtype=np.int64,
                ),
            )

            state_rows = X[
                rows
            ][
                :,
                FEATURE_INDICES,
            ]

            assert state_rows.shape == (
                16,
                12,
            )

            assert np.allclose(
                state_rows,
                state_rows[0:1],
                rtol=0.0,
                atol=1e-7,
            )

            qv = q[
                rows
            ].astype(
                np.float32
            )

            iv = imm[
                rows
            ].astype(
                np.float32
            )

            state = (
                state_rows[0]
                .astype(
                    np.float32
                )
            )

            assert qv.shape == (16,)
            assert iv.shape == (16,)
            assert state.shape == (12,)

            roots.append(
                {
                    "scene": sc,
                    "root_step":
                        int(step),
                    "state": state,
                    "q": qv,
                    "immediate": iv,
                }
            )

    assert len(roots) == 1162

    counts = {
        sc:
            sum(
                r["scene"] == sc
                for r in roots
            )
        for sc in SCENES
    }

    assert (
        counts
        == EXPECTED_ROOT_COUNTS
    )

    return roots


def numpy_pairwise_root_loss(
    score,
    q,
):
    score = np.asarray(
        score,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    q = np.asarray(
        q,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    assert score.shape == (16,)
    assert q.shape == (16,)

    span = float(
        q.max()
        - q.min()
    )

    total = 0.0
    total_w = 0.0

    if span <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0

    for i in range(16):
        for j in range(
            i + 1,
            16,
        ):
            d = float(
                q[i]
                - q[j]
            )

            if abs(d) <= 0.0:
                continue

            y = (
                1.0
                if d > 0.0
                else -1.0
            )

            w = (
                abs(d)
                / span
            )

            margin = (
                y
                * float(
                    score[i]
                    - score[j]
                )
            )

            # stable softplus(-margin)
            loss = float(
                np.logaddexp(
                    0.0,
                    -margin,
                )
            )

            total += (
                w * loss
            )

            total_w += w

    if total_w <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0

    return (
        total
        / total_w
    )


def synthetic_objective_check():
    q = np.arange(
        16,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    perfect = q.copy()

    reverse = (
        -q
    )

    perfect_loss = (
        numpy_pairwise_root_loss(
            perfect,
            q,
        )
    )

    reverse_loss = (
        numpy_pairwise_root_loss(
            reverse,
            q,
        )
    )

    assert (
        perfect_loss
        < reverse_loss
    )

    # Target scale + offset invariance.
    q_scaled = (
        q * 7.5
        + 123.0
    )

    scaled_loss = (
        numpy_pairwise_root_loss(
            perfect,
            q_scaled,
        )
    )

    assert math.isclose(
        perfect_loss,
        scaled_loss,
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=1e-12,
    )

    # Score additive-shift invariance.
    shifted_score_loss = (
        numpy_pairwise_root_loss(
            perfect + 1000.0,
            q,
        )
    )

    assert math.isclose(
        perfect_loss,
        shifted_score_loss,
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=1e-12,
    )

    # Equal-Q root contributes zero.
    equal_loss = (
        numpy_pairwise_root_loss(
            perfect,
            np.ones(
                16,
                dtype=np.float64,
            ),
        )
    )

    assert equal_loss == 0.0

    print(
        "PAIRWISE_ORDER_GATE=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "PAIRWISE_Q_SCALE_INVARIANCE=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "PAIRWISE_SCORE_SHIFT_INVARIANCE=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "PAIRWISE_EQUAL_Q_POLICY=PASS"
    )


def load_exact_metric_closure():
    import textwrap

    source = PARENT.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    tree = ast.parse(
        source
    )

    names = (
        "average_ranks",
        "pearson",
        "spearman",
        "ranking_metrics",
    )

    defs = {
        n.name: n
        for n in tree.body
        if (
            isinstance(
                n,
                ast.FunctionDef,
            )
            and n.name
            in names
        )
    }

    assert set(defs) == set(
        names
    )

    ordered = sorted(
        defs.values(),
        key=lambda n: n.lineno,
    )

    source_text = (
        "import math\n"
        "import numpy as np\n\n"
        + "\n\n".join(
            textwrap.dedent(
                ast.get_source_segment(
                    source,
                    node,
                )
            )
            for node in ordered
        )
        + "\n"
    )

    ns = {}

    exec(
        compile(
            source_text,
            str(PARENT),
            "exec",
        ),
        ns,
    )

    fn = ns[
        "ranking_metrics"
    ]

    return fn


def torch_pairwise_root_loss(
    scores,
    q,
):
    # Imports intentionally local:
    # static-check does not import torch.
    import torch
    import torch.nn.functional as F

    assert scores.ndim == 2
    assert q.ndim == 2
    assert scores.shape == q.shape
    assert scores.shape[1] == 16

    d = (
        q.unsqueeze(2)
        - q.unsqueeze(1)
    )

    ds = (
        scores.unsqueeze(2)
        - scores.unsqueeze(1)
    )

    span = (
        q.max(
            dim=1
        ).values
        - q.min(
            dim=1
        ).values
    )

    span = span.clamp_min(
        1e-12
    )

    weights = (
        d.abs()
        / span[:, None, None]
    )

    signs = d.sign()

    upper = torch.triu(
        torch.ones(
            (16, 16),
            dtype=torch.bool,
            device=scores.device,
        ),
        diagonal=1,
    )

    valid = (
        upper[None, :, :]
        & (d != 0)
    )

    pair_loss = (
        weights
        * F.softplus(
            -signs * ds
        )
    )

    numerator = (
        (
            pair_loss
            * valid
        )
        .sum(
            dim=(1, 2)
        )
    )

    denominator = (
        (
            weights
            * valid
        )
        .sum(
            dim=(1, 2)
        )
    )

    zero = (
        scores.sum(
            dim=1
        )
        * 0.0
    )

    return torch.where(
        denominator > 1e-12,
        numerator
        / denominator.clamp_min(
            1e-12
        ),
        zero,
    )


def build_model():
    import torch.nn as nn

    class ActionConditionedValueV3PairwiseRank(
        nn.Module
    ):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__()

            self.net = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(
                    12,
                    64,
                ),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Linear(
                    64,
                    64,
                ),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Linear(
                    64,
                    16,
                ),
            )

        def forward(
            self,
            x,
        ):
            return self.net(x)

    return (
        ActionConditionedValueV3PairwiseRank()
    )


def train_fold(
    roots,
    heldout,
    seed,
    run_dir,
):
    import torch

    torch.set_num_threads(1)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
        True
    )

    train = [
        r
        for r in roots
        if r["scene"] != heldout
    ]

    val = [
        r
        for r in roots
        if r["scene"] == heldout
    ]

    Xtr = np.stack(
        [
            r["state"]
            for r in train
        ]
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    Qtr = np.stack(
        [
            r["q"]
            for r in train
        ]
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    Str = np.asarray(
        [
            r["scene"]
            for r in train
        ]
    )

    Xva = np.stack(
        [
            r["state"]
            for r in val
        ]
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    Qva = np.stack(
        [
            r["q"]
            for r in val
        ]
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    Iva = np.stack(
        [
            r["immediate"]
            for r in val
        ]
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    steps = np.asarray(
        [
            r["root_step"]
            for r in val
        ],
        dtype=np.int64,
    )

    mean = Xtr.mean(
        axis=0
    )

    std = Xtr.std(
        axis=0
    )

    std[
        std <= 1e-12
    ] = 1.0

    Xtr_n = (
        (Xtr - mean)
        / std
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    Xva_n = (
        (Xva - mean)
        / std
    ).astype(
        np.float32
    )

    model = build_model()

    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=LR,
    )

    xt = torch.from_numpy(
        Xtr_n
    )

    qt = torch.from_numpy(
        Qtr
    )

    train_scenes = sorted(
        set(
            Str.tolist()
        )
    )

    scene_masks = {
        sc:
            torch.from_numpy(
                Str == sc
            )
        for sc in train_scenes
    }

    final_loss = None

    model.train()

    for _ in range(EPOCHS):
        optimizer.zero_grad(
            set_to_none=True
        )

        scores = model(
            xt
        )

        root_loss = (
            torch_pairwise_root_loss(
                scores,
                qt,
            )
        )

        scene_loss = []

        for sc in train_scenes:
            scene_loss.append(
                root_loss[
                    scene_masks[sc]
                ].mean()
            )

        loss = torch.stack(
            scene_loss
        ).mean()

        loss.backward()

        optimizer.step()

        final_loss = float(
            loss.detach().cpu()
        )

    model.eval()

    with torch.no_grad():
        pred = (
            model(
                torch.from_numpy(
                    Xva_n
                )
            )
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
            .astype(
                np.float32
            )
        )

    assert pred.shape == (
        len(val),
        16,
    )

    assert np.isfinite(
        pred
    ).all()

    ranking_metrics = (
        load_exact_metric_closure()
    )

    metrics = ranking_metrics(
        Qva,
        pred,
        Iva,
    )

    run_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=False,
    )

    ckpt = (
        run_dir
        / "checkpoint.pt"
    )

    pred_path = (
        run_dir
        / "predictions.npz"
    )

    torch.save(
        {
            "model_type":
                "ActionConditionedValueV3PairwiseRank",
            "architecture":
                "12->64->64->16",
            "feature_names":
                list(FEATURES),
            "feature_indices":
                list(FEATURE_INDICES),
            "objective":
                "ROOT_NORMALIZED_WEIGHTED_PAIRWISE_LOGISTIC",
            "feature_mean":
                mean,
            "feature_std":
                std,
            "state_dict":
                model.state_dict(),
            "heldout_scene":
                heldout,
            "seed":
                seed,
            "preregistration_sha256":
                sha(PREREG),
        },
        ckpt,
    )

    np.savez_compressed(
        pred_path,
        root_step=steps,
        q_true=Qva,
        score_pred=pred,
        immediate_reward=Iva,
    )

    out = {
        **{
            k:
                (
                    int(v)
                    if k == "root_count"
                    else float(v)
                )
            for k, v
            in metrics.items()
        },
        "heldout":
            heldout,
        "seed":
            seed,
        "train_scenes":
            train_scenes,
        "final_train_loss":
            final_loss,
        "checkpoint_sha256":
            sha(ckpt),
        "predictions_sha256":
            sha(pred_path),
    }

    return out


def execute_development(
    authorization_lock,
):
    import datetime

    verify_inputs()
    verify_parent_contract()

    authorization_lock = Path(
        authorization_lock
    )

    if not authorization_lock.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "AUTHORIZATION_LOCK_MISSING"
        )

    auth = read_json(
        authorization_lock
    )

    assert (
        auth["type"]
        == "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_TRAINING_AUTHORIZATION_V1"
    )

    assert (
        auth["status"]
        == "AUTHORIZED_FIXED_PREREGISTERED_DEVELOPMENT_RUN"
    )

    assert (
        auth["preregistration_sha256"]
        == sha(PREREG)
    )

    assert (
        auth["pipeline_sha256"]
        == sha(
            Path(__file__)
        )
    )

    if RESULT_DIR.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "DEVELOPMENT_RESULT_NAMESPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS"
        )

    RESULT_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=False,
    )

    roots = build_roots()

    fold_metrics = []

    for seed in SEEDS:
        for heldout in SCENES:
            run_dir = (
                RESULT_DIR
                / (
                    f"seed_{seed}"
                    f"_heldout_{heldout}"
                )
            )

            m = train_fold(
                roots,
                heldout,
                seed,
                run_dir,
            )

            fold_metrics.append(
                m
            )

            print(
                "FOLD "
                + f"seed={seed} "
                + f"heldout={heldout} "
                + f"regret={m['normalized_regret']} "
                + f"immediate={m['immediate_regret']}",
                flush=True,
            )

    pre = read_json(
        PREREG
    )

    frozen_baseline = (
        pre[
            "development_validation"
        ][
            "immediate_baseline_regret"
        ]
    )

    scene_summary = {}

    scene_wins = 0

    for sc in SCENES:
        rows = [
            x
            for x in fold_metrics
            if x["heldout"] == sc
        ]

        assert len(rows) == 3

        regrets = np.asarray(
            [
                x[
                    "normalized_regret"
                ]
                for x in rows
            ],
            dtype=np.float64,
        )

        immediate = np.asarray(
            [
                x[
                    "immediate_regret"
                ]
                for x in rows
            ],
            dtype=np.float64,
        )

        median_regret = float(
            np.median(
                regrets
            )
        )

        expected_imm = float(
            frozen_baseline[
                sc
            ]
        )

        assert np.allclose(
            immediate,
            expected_imm,
            rtol=0.0,
            atol=1e-8,
        )

        win = (
            median_regret
            < expected_imm
        )

        scene_wins += int(
            win
        )

        scene_summary[sc] = {
            "seed_regrets":
                regrets.tolist(),
            "median_normalized_regret":
                median_regret,
            "frozen_immediate_regret":
                expected_imm,
            "win":
                bool(win),
        }

    mean_scene_median = float(
        np.mean(
            [
                scene_summary[sc][
                    "median_normalized_regret"
                ]
                for sc in SCENES
            ]
        )
    )

    mean_baseline = float(
        np.mean(
            [
                scene_summary[sc][
                    "frozen_immediate_regret"
                ]
                for sc in SCENES
            ]
        )
    )

    primary_pass = (
        scene_wins == 3
    )

    secondary_pass = (
        mean_scene_median
        < mean_baseline
    )

    development_ready = (
        primary_pass
        and secondary_pass
    )

    decision = (
        "FREEZE_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_CANDIDATE_AND_PREREGISTER_BRAND_NEW_INDEPENDENT_SCENE"
        if development_ready
        else
        "VALUE_V3_NOT_READY_EXPAND_DEVELOPMENT_EPISODES_BEFORE_ANY_FURTHER_ARCHITECTURE_CHANGE"
    )

    summary = {
        "type":
            "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_LOSO_V1",
        "status":
            "PASS_EXECUTION_COMPLETE",
        "development_only":
            True,
        "preregistration_sha256":
            sha(PREREG),
        "pipeline_sha256":
            sha(
                Path(__file__)
            ),
        "authorization_lock_sha256":
            sha(
                authorization_lock
            ),
        "fold_metrics":
            fold_metrics,
        "scene_summary":
            scene_summary,
        "scene_win_count":
            scene_wins,
        "mean_scene_median_regret":
            mean_scene_median,
        "mean_immediate_baseline_regret":
            mean_baseline,
        "primary_gate_pass":
            primary_pass,
        "secondary_gate_pass":
            secondary_pass,
        "V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY":
            development_ready,
        "DECISION":
            decision,
        "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION":
            False,
        "FINAL_GENERALIZATION_CLAIM":
            False,
        "completed_at_utc":
            datetime.datetime.now(
                datetime.timezone.utc
            ).isoformat(),
    }

    summary_path = (
        RESULT_DIR
        / "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_LOSO_SUMMARY_V1.json"
    )

    summary_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            summary,
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
            allow_nan=False,
        )
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print(
        "VALUE_V3_SCENE_WIN_COUNT="
        + str(scene_wins)
    )

    print(
        "VALUE_V3_PRIMARY_GATE_PASS="
        + repr(primary_pass)
    )

    print(
        "VALUE_V3_SECONDARY_GATE_PASS="
        + repr(secondary_pass)
    )

    print(
        "VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY="
        + repr(development_ready)
    )

    print(
        "DECISION="
        + decision
    )

    print(
        "SUMMARY_SHA256="
        + sha(summary_path)
    )

    print(
        "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"
    )


def static_check():
    pre = verify_inputs()

    verify_parent_contract()

    roots = build_roots()

    counts = {
        sc:
            sum(
                r["scene"] == sc
                for r in roots
            )
        for sc in SCENES
    }

    print(
        "DATASET_ROW_COUNT=18592"
    )

    print(
        "ROOT_COUNT=1162"
    )

    print(
        "SCENE_ROOT_COUNTS="
        + repr(counts)
    )

    print(
        "JOINT_ACTION_COUNT_PER_ROOT=16"
    )

    print(
        "FEATURE_COUNT=12"
    )

    print(
        "FEATURE_INDICES="
        + repr(
            list(
                FEATURE_INDICES
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        "ROOT_STATE_INVARIANCE_GATE=PASS"
    )

    synthetic_objective_check()

    # Exact frozen metric can be extracted without
    # executing a neural network.
    metric = load_exact_metric_closure()

    q = np.asarray(
        [
            np.arange(
                16,
                dtype=np.float32,
            )
        ],
        dtype=np.float32,
    )

    pred = q.copy()
    imm = q.copy()

    m = metric(
        q,
        pred,
        imm,
    )

    assert (
        m["root_count"]
        == 1
    )

    assert math.isclose(
        float(
            m["normalized_regret"]
        ),
        0.0,
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=1e-12,
    )

    print(
        "EXACT_METRIC_EXTRACTION_GATE=PASS"
    )

    assert (
        pre[
            "development_validation"
        ][
            "primary_gate"
        ][
            "required_scene_wins"
        ]
        == 3
    )

    assert (
        pre[
            "development_validation"
        ][
            "per_scene_seed_aggregation"
        ]
        == "MEDIAN"
    )

    print(
        "V3_GATE_IMPLEMENTATION_CONTRACT=PASS"
    )

    print(
        "TORCH_IMPORTED_THIS_STEP=False"
    )

    print(
        "TRAINING_EXECUTED=False"
    )

    print(
        "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
    )

    print(
        "C03_USED=False"
    )

    print(
        "C13_USED=False"
    )

    print(
        "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"
    )

    print(
        "STATIC_PIPELINE_CONTRACT=PASS"
    )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--static-check",
        action="store_true",
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--execute-development",
        action="store_true",
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--authorization-lock",
        default="",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    if (
        args.static_check
        == args.execute_development
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SELECT_EXACTLY_ONE_MODE"
        )

    if args.static_check:
        static_check()
        return

    if not args.authorization_lock:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "MISSING_DEVELOPMENT_TRAINING_AUTHORIZATION_LOCK"
        )

    execute_development(
        args.authorization_lock
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== VALUE V3 PAIRWISE PIPELINE STATIC DERIVATION ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import importlib.util

import numpy as np
import torch


pipeline_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
preauth_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[3])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


preauth = json.loads(
    preauth_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    preauth["status"]
    == "AUTHORIZED_SYNTHETIC_TECHNICAL_SMOKE_ONLY"
)

assert (
    preauth["forbidden"][
        "development_dataset_training"
    ]
    is True
)

assert (
    preauth["forbidden"][
        "C03_read"
    ]
    is True
)

assert (
    preauth["forbidden"][
        "C13_read"
    ]
    is True
)


spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
    "value_v3_pipeline",
    pipeline_p,
)

module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(
    spec
)

spec.loader.exec_module(
    module
)

torch.set_num_threads(1)
torch.manual_seed(20260824)

model = module.build_model()

model.train()


# ============================================================
# Synthetic batch only
# ============================================================

X = torch.tensor(
    [
        [0.0] * 12,
        [0.1 * i for i in range(12)],
        [-0.1 * i for i in range(12)],
        [1.0] * 12,
    ],
    dtype=torch.float32,
)

Q = torch.tensor(
    [
        list(range(16)),
        list(range(15, -1, -1)),
        [0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14,
         1, 3, 5, 7, 9, 11, 13, 15],
        [1.0] * 16,
    ],
    dtype=torch.float32,
)

assert X.shape == (4, 12)
assert Q.shape == (4, 16)


scores0 = model(
    X
)

assert scores0.shape == (
    4,
    16,
)

assert torch.isfinite(
    scores0
).all()


root_loss0 = (
    module.torch_pairwise_root_loss(
        scores0,
        Q,
    )
)

assert root_loss0.shape == (4,)
assert torch.isfinite(
    root_loss0
).all()

# Equal-Q root must contribute zero.
assert float(
    root_loss0[3]
) == 0.0


optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
    model.parameters(),
    lr=0.001,
)

optimizer.zero_grad(
    set_to_none=True
)

loss = root_loss0.mean()

loss.backward()


grad_norm_sq = 0.0
grad_parameter_count = 0

for p in model.parameters():

    if p.grad is None:
        continue

    assert torch.isfinite(
        p.grad
    ).all()

    grad_norm_sq += float(
        torch.sum(
            p.grad.detach()
            * p.grad.detach()
        )
    )

    grad_parameter_count += 1


assert grad_parameter_count > 0
assert grad_norm_sq > 0.0

optimizer.step()


model.eval()

with torch.no_grad():

    scores1 = model(
        X
    )

    root_loss1 = (
        module.torch_pairwise_root_loss(
            scores1,
            Q,
        )
    )


assert scores1.shape == (
    4,
    16,
)

assert torch.isfinite(
    scores1
).all()

assert torch.isfinite(
    root_loss1
).all()

assert float(
    root_loss1[3]
) == 0.0


parameter_changed = False

for a, b in zip(
    scores0.detach().flatten(),
    scores1.detach().flatten(),
):

    if float(a) != float(b):
        parameter_changed = True
        break

assert parameter_changed is True


print("TORCH_VERSION=" + torch.__version__)
print("SYNTHETIC_MODEL_SHAPE=4x12->4x16")
print("SYNTHETIC_FORWARD_FINITE=PASS")
print("SYNTHETIC_PAIRWISE_LOSS_FINITE=PASS")
print("SYNTHETIC_EQUAL_Q_ZERO_LOSS=PASS")
print("SYNTHETIC_BACKWARD_FINITE=PASS")
print("SYNTHETIC_GRAD_PARAMETER_COUNT=" + str(grad_parameter_count))
print("SYNTHETIC_GRAD_NORM_SQ=" + repr(grad_norm_sq))
print("SYNTHETIC_OPTIMIZER_STEP=PASS")
print("SYNTHETIC_PARAMETER_UPDATE=PASS")


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_SYNTHETIC_TORCH_SMOKE_RESULT_V1",

    "status":
        "PASS_SYNTHETIC_TORCH_TECHNICAL_SMOKE",

    "pipeline_sha256":
        sha(pipeline_p),

    "preauthorization_sha256":
        sha(preauth_p),

    "torch_version":
        torch.__version__,

    "protocol": {
        "synthetic_batch_shape":
            [4, 12],

        "synthetic_output_shape":
            [4, 16],

        "single_optimizer_step":
            True,

        "gradient_parameter_count":
            grad_parameter_count,

        "gradient_norm_sq":
            grad_norm_sq,

        "equal_q_zero_loss":
            True,

        "finite_forward":
            True,

        "finite_loss":
            True,

        "finite_backward":
            True,

        "parameter_update":
            True,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "development_dataset_read":
            False,

        "development_training_executed":
            False,

        "C03_read":
            False,

        "C13_read":
            False,

        "formal_checkpoint_written":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        "BUILD_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_TRAINING_AUTHORIZATION",

    "completed_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print("SYNTHETIC_SMOKE_RESULT_WRITE=PASS")
print(
    "SYNTHETIC_SMOKE_RESULT_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)

print("DEVELOPMENT_DATASET_READ=False")
print("DEVELOPMENT_TRAINING_EXECUTED=False")
print("C03_READ=False")
print("C13_READ=False")
print("MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False")
print(
    "NEXT="
    "BUILD_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_TRAINING_AUTHORIZATION"
)

    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()

思考了两秒
当前资源：低资源即可。这 9 个 fold 都是小型 MLP、CPU 训练，没必要开 4090D；只是 0.5 CPU 可能慢一点。建议用 screen 跑，避免 SSH 中断。

现在可以正式执行唯一一次固定 V3 development run。设计、3 个 seed、3 个 held-out scene、600 epochs 都已经锁死；这一步禁止中途改参数。

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic
export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1

(
set -eu
set -o pipefail

ROOT=/root/autodl-tmp
D="$ROOT/paper1_value_v3_development_only_v1"

PREREG="$D/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_ONLY_PREREGISTRATION_V1.json"
PIPELINE="$D/paper1_value_v3_pairwise_rank_training_pipeline_v1.py"
DERIV="$D/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_PAIRWISE_RANK_PIPELINE_DERIVATION_LOCK_V1.json"
READY="$D/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_PAIRWISE_RANK_PIPELINE_STATIC_READY_V1.json"
SMOKE="$D/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_SYNTHETIC_TORCH_SMOKE_RESULT_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e

EXPECTED_PREREG=ff6c9714925bfa3d264a1d9eac8d236cea8e9a7a5f88e4dc67394201feb6e914

EXPECTED_READY=258c1bd386f58202067456bbd0f899e89d61a9c3608b9c69305efea26f5344b6

EXPECTED_AUTH=a83f1abd1c646324a57205e52ce24b41b98505dd141b3285a1ecf50ffd87720d

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== VALUE V3 FIXED DEVELOPMENT EXECUTION ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$PREREG" "$EXPECTED_PREREG" PREREG
check_sha "$PIPELINE" "$EXPECTED_PIPELINE" PIPELINE
check_sha "$DERIV" "$EXPECTED_DERIV" DERIVATION_LOCK
check_sha "$READY" "$EXPECTED_READY" STATIC_READY
check_sha "$SMOKE" "$EXPECTED_SMOKE" SYNTHETIC_SMOKE
check_sha "$AUTH" "$EXPECTED_AUTH" DEVELOPMENT_AUTHORIZATION

echo "IMMUTABLE_EXECUTION_GATE=PASS"

[ ! -e "$RESULT_DIR" ] || {
    echo "FAIL RESULT_NAMESPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS=$RESULT_DIR"
    false
}

[ ! -e "$LOG" ] || {
    echo "FAIL EXECUTION_LOG_ALREADY_EXISTS=$LOG"
    false
}

echo "RESULT_NAMESPACE_UNUSED=PASS"
echo "EXECUTION_LOG_UNUSED=PASS"

echo
echo "FIXED_FOLD_COUNT=9"
echo "FIXED_SEEDS=[20260824,20260825,20260826]"
echo "FIXED_SCENES=[C04,C11,C06]"
echo "FIXED_EPOCHS_PER_FOLD=600"
echo "HYPERPARAMETER_SEARCH=False"
echo "BEST_SEED_SELECTION=False"
echo "C03_USED=False"
echo "C13_USED=False"
echo "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"

    --execute-development \

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib

result_dir = Path(sys.argv[1])
summary_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
rc = int(sys.argv[3])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


print(
    "RESULT_DIR_EXISTS=",
    result_dir.is_dir(),
)

if result_dir.is_dir():

    run_dirs = sorted(
        p
        for p in result_dir.iterdir()
        if (
            p.is_dir()
            and p.name.startswith("seed_")
        )
    )

    print(
        "COMPLETED_FOLD_DIRECTORY_COUNT=",
        len(run_dirs),
    )

    for p in run_dirs:
        print(
            "FOLD_DIR=",
            p.name,
        )


print(
    "SUMMARY_EXISTS=",
    summary_p.is_file(),
)

if not summary_p.is_file():

    if rc == 0:
        print(
            "RESULT_CLASSIFICATION=HOLD_MISSING_SUMMARY_AFTER_RC0"
        )
    else:
        print(
            "RESULT_CLASSIFICATION=TECHNICAL_FAILURE_NO_FINAL_SUMMARY"
        )

    print(
        "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False"
    )

    raise SystemExit(0)


x = json.loads(
    summary_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

print(
    "SUMMARY_SHA256=",
    sha(summary_p),
)

print(
    "SUMMARY_STATUS=",
    x.get("status"),
)

print(
    "DEVELOPMENT_ONLY=",
    x.get("development_only"),
)

folds = x.get(
    "fold_metrics",
    [],
)

print(
    "FOLD_METRIC_COUNT=",
    len(folds),
)

for row in folds:

    print(
        "FOLD_RESULT="
        + "seed="
        + str(row.get("seed"))
        + " heldout="
        + str(row.get("heldout"))
        + " regret="
        + repr(row.get("normalized_regret"))
        + " immediate="
        + repr(row.get("immediate_regret"))
        + " spearman="
        + repr(row.get("mean_root_spearman"))
        + " top1="
        + repr(row.get("top1"))
    )

    "scene_summary",
    {}
)

for scene in (
    "C04",
    "C11",
    "C06",
):

    row = scene_summary.get(
        scene,
        {}
    )

    print(
        f"{scene}_SEED_REGRETS="
        + repr(
            row.get(
                "seed_regrets"
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        f"{scene}_MEDIAN_REGRET="
        + repr(
            row.get(
                "median_normalized_regret"
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        f"{scene}_IMMEDIATE_BASELINE="
        + repr(
            row.get(
                "frozen_immediate_regret"
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        f"{scene}_WIN="
        + repr(
            row.get(
                "win"
            )
        )
    )


print(
    "VALUE_V3_SCENE_WIN_COUNT=",
    x.get(
        "scene_win_count"
    ),
)

print(
    "VALUE_V3_MEAN_SCENE_MEDIAN_REGRET=",
    x.get(
        "mean_scene_median_regret"
    ),
)

print(
    "VALUE_V3_MEAN_IMMEDIATE_BASELINE_REGRET=",
    x.get(
        "mean_immediate_baseline_regret"
    ),
)

print(
    "VALUE_V3_PRIMARY_GATE_PASS=",
    x.get(
        "primary_gate_pass"
    ),
)

print(
    "VALUE_V3_SECONDARY_GATE_PASS=",
    x.get(
        "secondary_gate_pass"
    ),
)

print(
    "VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY=",
    x.get(
        "V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY"
    ),
)

print(
    "DECISION=",
    x.get(
        "DECISION"
    ),
)

print(
    "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=",
    x.get(
        "MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION"
    ),
)

print(
    "FINAL_GENERALIZATION_CLAIM=",
    x.get(
        "FINAL_GENERALIZATION_CLAIM"
    ),
)

if (
    x.get("primary_gate_pass") is True
    and x.get("secondary_gate_pass") is True
    and x.get("V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY") is True
):
    print(
        "RESULT_CLASSIFICATION=DEVELOPMENT_PASS"
    )

    print(
        "NEXT=FREEZE_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_PASS_AND_PREREGISTER_NEW_INDEPENDENT_SCENE"
    )

else:
    print(
        "RESULT_CLASSIFICATION=DEVELOPMENT_FAIL"
    )

    print(
        "NEXT=FREEZE_VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_FAIL_AND_EXPAND_DEVELOPMENT_EPISODES"
    )

和 V2 对比
现在可以得到一个比较清晰的结论：

V2
失败模式：

C03 独立验证失败

regret:

Value V2: 0.3506

immediate: 0.1832

不接入 MCTS。

V3
pairwise ranking objective 有明显改善：

平均：

0.1699 < 0.2111
说明：

“优化 action ordering 而不是绝对 Q 回归”方向是有效的。

但是：

泛化仍然不稳定。

核心问题从“Q尺度错误”变成：

跨场景策略偏移，尤其 C11。

当前科学结论
V3 不能冻结为候选模型。

原因：

预注册要求：

C04 win
C11 win
C06 win
= 3/3
实际：

C04 ✅
C11 ❌
C06 ✅

VALUE_V3_NOT_READY_EXPAND_DEVELOPMENT_EPISODES_BEFORE_ANY_FURTHER_ARCHITECTURE_CHANGE


下一步
不要改网络，不要改 loss。

当前最合理路线：

V3.1：扩大 development coverage，而不是继续调模型。

原因：

C04/C06 已经证明 pairwise ranking 有效；

C11 是单场景失败；

现在缺的是 C11 类型状态覆盖。

下一步应该：

保留 V3 architecture；

保留 pairwise objective；

增加 C11 类 episode/root coverage；

重新 preregister；

再跑 fixed LOSO。

不要：

加 C03；

改 seed；

改 epoch；

换 target。

当前项目状态：

MineSim复现                 ✅
MCTS                        ✅
双车冲突                    ✅
FullMine                    ✅
Fleet-MCTS                  ✅
Value V2                    ❌ closed
Value V3 pairwise           ⚠️ promising but not ready
MCTS integration            ❌
下一步就是 V3 development coverage expansion preregistration。


现在是什么情况

思考了两秒
现在项目走到这里：

当前资源：低资源即可。

神经网络这条线的状态是：Value V2 已经正式淘汰，Value V3 方向有明显进步，但还不能接入 MCTS。

V2 的独立验证已经彻底结束：在 C03 上 normalized regret=0.3506，明显差于 immediate baseline 的 0.1832，所以已经冻结为 DO_NOT_INTEGRATE，不能重跑，也不能再拿 C03 调参。

V3 这次改成了 pairwise ranking objective，网络结构和 12 维特征都没改，只改训练目标。固定 3 个 seed × C04/C11/C06，共 9 个 fold 已经全部跑完，技术执行没有问题。结果是：

C04：PASS，median regret 0.1747 < 0.2347

C06：PASS，median regret 0.1023 < 0.2419

C11：FAIL，median regret 0.2327 > 0.1567

整体平均其实已经改善：0.1699 < 0.2111，所以 V3 的 pairwise ranking 思路是有效的；但我们事先规定必须 3/3 场景全部胜过 baseline，现在只有 2/3，因此正式结论仍是：

VALUE_V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY=False

也就是说，目前不是“神经网络彻底不行”，而是：

模型设计方向已经比 V2 好很多，但 development 数据覆盖还不够，主要卡在 C11 这一类场景。

所以现在的 blocker 已经从“网络/目标设计失败”转成了：

development episode coverage 不足，尤其是 C11 类型状态覆盖不足。

下一步不应该继续改 loss、改网络、调 seed 或 epoch，而是先做 V3 development coverage expansion 的预注册：保持现有 V3 网络和 pairwise loss 不动，增加新的 development episodes/root coverage，再重新做固定 LOSO。

目前总路线可以理解成：

MineSim复现 ✅ → MCTS ✅ → 双车冲突 ✅ → FullMine/Fleet-MCTS ✅ → Value V2 ❌ → Value V3 方向有效但 2/3 场景通过 ⚠️ → 扩充 development 数据 → 再验证 → 通过后才考虑接 MCTS。


那继续

思考了 9s
继续。现在先不直接采新数据，先做 C11 development coverage inventory：只看现有 C11 数据/episode 有多少、是不是实际上只有一个动态 episode，避免重复采已经存在的东西。

当前资源：低资源。

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import re

cross = Path(sys.argv[1])
prereg_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
summary_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[4])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


prereg = json.loads(
    prereg_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

summary = json.loads(
    summary_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 1. Confirm V3 failure is specifically C11
# ============================================================

assert (
    summary["status"]
    == "PASS_EXECUTION_COMPLETE"
)

assert (
    summary["scene_summary"]["C04"]["win"]
    is True
)

assert (
    summary["scene_summary"]["C11"]["win"]
    is False
)

assert (
    summary["scene_summary"]["C06"]["win"]
    is True
)

assert (
    summary["V3_DEVELOPMENT_READY"]
    is False
)

assert (
    prereg["decision_rule"][
        "if_any_primary_scene_fails"
    ]
    ==
    "VALUE_V3_NOT_READY_EXPAND_DEVELOPMENT_EPISODES_BEFORE_ANY_FURTHER_ARCHITECTURE_CHANGE"
)

print("V3_FAILURE_LOCALIZATION=PASS")
print("FAILED_DEVELOPMENT_SCENE=C11")


# ============================================================
# 2. Narrow C11 artifact inventory only
# ============================================================

patterns = (
    re.compile("c11", re.I),
    re.compile("cross.scene.c11", re.I),
)

allowed_suffixes = {
    ".json",
    ".jsonl",
    ".txt",
    ".log",
    ".npz",
    ".zip",
}

files = []

for p in cross.rglob("*"):

    if not p.is_file():
        continue

    if p.suffix.lower() not in allowed_suffixes:
        continue

    text = str(p)

    if any(
        pat.search(text)
        for pat in patterns
    ):
        files.append(p)

files = sorted(
    set(files),
    key=lambda p: str(p),
)

print(
    "C11_RELEVANT_FILE_COUNT=",
    len(files),
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Parse JSON/JSONL metadata without any model execution
# ============================================================

records = []

for p in files:

    rec = {
        "path": str(p),
        "size_bytes": p.stat().st_size,
        "sha256": sha(p),
        "suffix": p.suffix.lower(),
    }

    if p.suffix.lower() == ".json":

        try:
            obj = json.loads(
                p.read_text(
                    encoding="utf-8",
                    errors="ignore",
                )
            )

            for key in (
                "scene",
                "seed",
                "status",
                "outcome",
                "benchmark_success",
                "root_record_count",
                "root_count",
                "derived_action_record_count",
                "timeline_sync",
                "error",
            ):
                if key in obj:
                    rec[key] = obj[key]

        except Exception as e:
            rec["parse_error"] = repr(e)

    elif p.suffix.lower() == ".jsonl":

        line_count = 0
        first = None
        last = None

        with p.open(
            "r",
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="ignore",
        ) as f:

            for line in f:

                if not line.strip():
                    continue

                line_count += 1

                try:
                    obj = json.loads(line)
                except Exception:
                    continue

                if first is None:
                    first = obj

                last = obj

        rec["jsonl_record_count"] = line_count

        if isinstance(first, dict):
            rec["first_scene"] = first.get("scene")
            rec["first_root_step"] = first.get("root_step")
            rec["first_seed"] = first.get("seed")

        if isinstance(last, dict):
            rec["last_root_step"] = last.get("root_step")

    records.append(rec)


# ============================================================
# 4. Identify root-diagnostic datasets / episode manifests
# ============================================================

root_jsonls = [
    r
    for r in records
    if (
        r["suffix"] == ".jsonl"
        and (
            "neural-root-diagnostic"
            in r["path"].lower()
        )
    )
]

episode_manifests = [
    r
    for r in records
    if (
        r["suffix"] == ".json"
        and (
            "episode-manifest"
            in r["path"].lower()
            or "episode_manifest"
            in r["path"].lower()
        )
    )
]

result_jsons = [
    r
    for r in records
    if (
        r["suffix"] == ".json"
        and (
            "result"
            in Path(
                r["path"]
            ).name.lower()
        )
    )
]


print()
print("=== C11 ROOT DATASETS ===")

print(
    "C11_ROOT_JSONL_COUNT=",
    len(root_jsonls),
)

for r in root_jsonls:
    print(
        "C11_ROOT_JSONL="
        + r["path"]
    )

    print(
        "C11_ROOT_RECORD_COUNT="
        + str(
            r.get(
                "jsonl_record_count"
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        "C11_ROOT_JSONL_SHA256="
        + r["sha256"]
    )


print()
print("=== C11 EPISODE MANIFESTS ===")

print(
    "C11_EPISODE_MANIFEST_COUNT=",
    len(episode_manifests),
)

for r in episode_manifests:
    print(
        "C11_EPISODE_MANIFEST="
        + r["path"]
    )

    print(
        "C11_EPISODE_MANIFEST_SHA256="
        + r["sha256"]
    )


print()
print("=== C11 RESULT FILES ===")

for r in result_jsons[:30]:

    print(
        "C11_RESULT="
        + r["path"]
    )

    for key in (
        "seed",
        "status",
        "outcome",
        "benchmark_success",
        "root_count",
        "root_record_count",
        "error",
    ):
        if key in r:
            print(
                f"  {key}={r[key]}"
            )


# ============================================================
# 5. Conservative coverage classification
# ============================================================

root_counts = sorted(
    {
        int(
            r["jsonl_record_count"]
        )
        for r in root_jsonls
        if isinstance(
            r.get(
                "jsonl_record_count"
            ),
            int,
        )
    }
)

if (
    len(root_jsonls) == 1
    and root_counts == [890]
):
    coverage = (
        "SINGLE_CURRENT_SEMANTIC_C11_ROOT_EPISODE_CONFIRMED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "PREREGISTER_NEW_C11_DEVELOPMENT_EPISODE_COLLECTION"
    )

elif len(root_jsonls) > 1:
    coverage = (
        "MULTIPLE_C11_ROOT_DATASETS_FOUND_REQUIRES_PROVENANCE_DEDUP"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "DEDUP_C11_DEVELOPMENT_EPISODES_BEFORE_NEW_COLLECTION"
    )

else:
    coverage = (
        "C11_ROOT_DATASET_RESOLUTION_HOLD"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_EXISTING_C11_ROOT_DATASET_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_COVERAGE_INVENTORY_V1",

    "status":
        "PASS_READ_ONLY_C11_COVERAGE_INVENTORY",

    "V3_failure_scene":
        "C11",

    "c11_relevant_file_count":
        len(records),

    "root_diagnostic_jsonls":
        root_jsonls,

    "episode_manifests":
        episode_manifests,

    "result_files":
        result_jsons,

    "coverage_classification":
        coverage,

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "C11_COVERAGE_CLASSIFICATION="
    + coverage
)

print(
    "C11_COVERAGE_INVENTORY_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "C11_COVERAGE_INVENTORY_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)

print("TRAINING_EXECUTED=False")
print("MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False")
print("NEW_COLLECTION_EXECUTED=False")
print("C03_USED=False")
print("C13_USED=False")
print("V3_DESIGN_CHANGED=False")
print("MCTS_VALUE_INTEGRATION=False")

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

PY

echo
echo "=== REPO ==="
git rev-parse HEAD
git status --short --untracked-files=no
)
只发：

C11_EPISODE_MANIFEST_COUNT

如果确认目前确实只有那一个 890-root C11 episode，下一步就正式预注册新的 C11 development episodes，而不是继续调网络。



    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

[ -f "$PREREG" ] || {

)it status --short --untracked-files=noVENTORY",V1",OLLECTION"DEDUP"ER_ARCHITECTURE_CHANGE"

思考了几秒

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic

(

V3="$ROOT/paper1_value_v3_development_only_v1"
COVERAGE="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_COVERAGE_INVENTORY_V1.json"

OUT="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_890_ROOT_PROVENANCE_RESOLUTION_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_DATA=1fbc594cab90a5acad6f0d87b5f5719d1728c3c5969e9f949aba0ba37caf4c82
EXPECTED_COVERAGE=efd9cccbef52f5ac7a26d2fee449d485bd31893c0b8967d03dbfa4f230cc4a15

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

echo "=== C11 890-ROOT PROVENANCE RESOLUTION ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

[ -f "$DATA" ] || {
    echo "FAIL DATASET_MISSING"
    false
}

[ "$(sha "$DATA")" = "$EXPECTED_DATA" ] || {
    echo "FAIL DATASET_SHA"
    false
}

[ -f "$COVERAGE" ] || {
    echo "FAIL COVERAGE_LOCK_MISSING"
    false
}

[ "$(sha "$COVERAGE")" = "$EXPECTED_COVERAGE" ] || {
    echo "FAIL COVERAGE_LOCK_SHA"
    false
}

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false
}

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

python - \
"$ROOT" \
"$CROSS" \
"$DATA" \
"$COVERAGE" \
"$OUT" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import zipfile
import numpy as np

root = Path(sys.argv[1])
cross = Path(sys.argv[2])
data_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
coverage_p = Path(sys.argv[4])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[5])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


# ============================================================
# 1. Establish exact C11 membership in frozen dataset
# ============================================================

with np.load(
    data_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    scene = np.asarray(
        z["scene"]
    ).astype(str).reshape(-1)

    root_step = np.asarray(
        z["root_step"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    q = np.asarray(
        z["q_value"]
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_A = np.asarray(
        z["action_A"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_B = np.asarray(
        z["action_B"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)


mask = scene == "C11"

assert int(mask.sum()) == 14240

c11_steps = np.unique(
    root_step[mask]
)

assert len(c11_steps) == 890

print("C11_DATASET_ACTION_ROWS=14240")
print("C11_DATASET_ROOT_COUNT=890")

print(
    "C11_ROOT_STEP_MIN="
    + str(
        int(
            c11_steps.min()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "C11_ROOT_STEP_MAX="
    + str(
        int(
            c11_steps.max()
        )
    )
)

# exact 16-action contract
for step in c11_steps:

    m = (
        mask
        & (root_step == step)
    )

    assert int(m.sum()) == 16

    joint = (
        action_A[m] * 4
        + action_B[m]
    )

    assert np.array_equal(
        np.sort(joint),
        np.arange(16),
    )

print("C11_16_ACTION_ROOT_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Search candidate source artifacts by CONTENT,
#    not filename.
# ============================================================

candidates = []

allowed = {
    ".json",
    ".jsonl",
    ".zip",
    ".npz",
}

search_roots = [
    cross,
    root,
]

seen = set()

for base in search_roots:

    if not base.exists():
        continue

    for p in base.rglob("*"):

        if not p.is_file():
            continue

        if p in seen:
            continue

        seen.add(p)

        if p.suffix.lower() not in allowed:
            continue

        # avoid scanning current V3 execution products
        if (
            "paper1_value_v3_development_only_v1"
            in str(p)
        ):
            continue

        try:
            size = p.stat().st_size
        except Exception:
            continue

        # keep audit bounded
        if size > 100_000_000:
            continue

        rec = {
            "path": str(p),
            "size_bytes": size,
            "sha256": sha(p),
            "type": p.suffix.lower(),
            "signals": [],
        }


        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # JSONL
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        if p.suffix.lower() == ".jsonl":

            count = 0
            c11_count = 0
            root_steps_found = set()

            try:
                with p.open(
                    "r",
                    encoding="utf-8",
                    errors="ignore",
                ) as f:

                    for line in f:

                        if not line.strip():
                            continue

                        count += 1

                        try:
                            obj = json.loads(
                                line
                            )
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                        text_scene = str(
                            obj.get(
                                "scene",
                                ""
                            )
                        )

                        if text_scene == "C11":
                            c11_count += 1

                        if (
                            text_scene == "C11"
                            and obj.get(
                                "root_step"
                            )
                            is not None
                        ):
                            try:
                                root_steps_found.add(
                                    int(
                                        obj[
                                            "root_step"
                                        ]
                                    )
                                )
                            except Exception:
                                pass

            except Exception:
                continue

            if c11_count:
                rec["signals"].append(
                    "JSONL_SCENE_C11"
                )

                rec["record_count"] = count
                rec["c11_record_count"] = c11_count
                rec["c11_unique_root_steps"] = len(
                    root_steps_found
                )


        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # JSON
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        elif p.suffix.lower() == ".json":

            try:
                obj = json.loads(
                    p.read_text(
                        encoding="utf-8",
                        errors="ignore",
                    )
                )
            except Exception:
                continue

            blob = json.dumps(
                obj,
                ensure_ascii=False,
            )

            if (
                '"C11"'
                in blob
                or "cross-scene-c11"
                in blob.lower()
            ):
                rec["signals"].append(
                    "JSON_REFERENCES_C11"
                )

            for key in (
                "root_record_count",
                "root_count",
                "derived_action_record_count",
                "scene",
                "seed",
                "status",
                "outcome",
                "benchmark_success",
            ):
                if isinstance(
                    obj,
                    dict,
                ) and key in obj:
                    rec[key] = obj[key]


        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # NPZ
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        elif p.suffix.lower() == ".npz":

            try:
                with np.load(
                    p,
                    allow_pickle=False,
                ) as z:

                    if "scene" in z.files:

                        ss = np.asarray(
                            z["scene"]
                        ).astype(str).reshape(-1)

                        n11 = int(
                            np.sum(
                                ss == "C11"
                            )
                        )

                        if n11:
                            rec["signals"].append(
                                "NPZ_SCENE_C11"
                            )

                            rec[
                                "c11_rows"
                            ] = n11

                            if (
                                "root_step"
                                in z.files
                            ):
                                rr = np.asarray(
                                    z[
                                        "root_step"
                                    ]
                                ).reshape(-1)

                                rec[
                                    "c11_unique_root_steps"
                                ] = int(
                                    len(
                                        np.unique(
                                            rr[
                                                ss
                                                == "C11"
                                            ]
                                        )
                                    )
                                )

            except Exception:
                continue


        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # ZIP: inspect names + small json summaries
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        elif p.suffix.lower() == ".zip":

            try:
                with zipfile.ZipFile(
                    p,
                    "r",
                ) as z:

                    names = z.namelist()

                    c11_names = [
                        n
                        for n in names
                        if "c11" in n.lower()
                    ]

                    if c11_names:
                        rec["signals"].append(
                            "ZIP_MEMBER_NAME_C11"
                        )

                        rec[
                            "c11_member_count"
                        ] = len(
                            c11_names
                        )

                        rec[
                            "c11_members_sample"
                        ] = c11_names[:20]

                    json_hits = []

                    for name in names:

                        if not name.endswith(
                            ".json"
                        ):
                            continue

                        info = z.getinfo(
                            name
                        )

                        if info.file_size > 2_000_000:
                            continue

                        try:
                            text = z.read(
                                name
                            ).decode(
                                "utf-8",
                                errors="ignore",
                            )
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                        if (
                            '"C11"'
                            in text
                            or "cross-scene-c11"
                            in text.lower()
                        ):
                            json_hits.append(
                                name
                            )

                    if json_hits:
                        rec["signals"].append(
                            "ZIP_JSON_REFERENCES_C11"
                        )

                        rec[
                            "c11_json_members"
                        ] = json_hits[:30]

            except Exception:
                continue


        if rec["signals"]:
            candidates.append(
                rec
            )


# ============================================================
# 3. Rank likely authoritative sources
# ============================================================

def score(rec):
    s = 0

    if (
        rec.get(
            "c11_unique_root_steps"
        )
        == 890
    ):
        s += 100

    if (
        rec.get(
            "c11_record_count"
        )
        == 890
    ):
        s += 80

    if (
        rec.get(
            "c11_rows"
        )
        == 14240
    ):
        s += 80

    if (
        rec.get(
            "root_record_count"
        )
        == 890
    ):
        s += 70

    if (
        rec.get(
            "derived_action_record_count"
        )
        == 14240
    ):
        s += 70

    text = rec["path"].lower()

    if "root" in text:
        s += 5

    if "neural" in text:
        s += 5

    if "collector" in text:
        s += 5

    if "manifest" in text:
        s += 3

    return s


for rec in candidates:
    rec["resolution_score"] = score(
        rec
    )


candidates.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x["resolution_score"],
        x["path"],
    ),
    reverse=True,
)


print()
print("=== C11 CONTENT-BASED CANDIDATES ===")

print(
    "C11_CONTENT_CANDIDATE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(candidates)
    )
)

for i, rec in enumerate(
    candidates[:25]
):
    print()
    print(
        "CANDIDATE_RANK="
        + str(i + 1)
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_PATH="
        + rec["path"]
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_SHA256="
        + rec["sha256"]
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_SCORE="
        + str(
            rec[
                "resolution_score"
            ]
        )
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_SIGNALS="
        + repr(
            rec[
                "signals"
            ]
        )
    )

    for key in (
        "record_count",
        "c11_record_count",
        "c11_rows",
        "c11_unique_root_steps",
        "root_record_count",
        "derived_action_record_count",
        "scene",
        "seed",
        "status",
        "outcome",
        "benchmark_success",
        "c11_member_count",
        "c11_members_sample",
        "c11_json_members",
    ):
        if key in rec:
            print(
                f"{key}="
                + repr(
                    rec[key]
                )
            )


# ============================================================
# 4. Conservative resolution
# ============================================================

exact = [
    r
    for r in candidates
    if (
        r.get(
            "c11_unique_root_steps"
        )
        == 890
        or r.get(
            "c11_rows"
        )
        == 14240
        or r.get(
            "root_record_count"
        )
        == 890
        or r.get(
            "derived_action_record_count"
        )
        == 14240
    )
]


if exact:

    classification = (
        "EXISTING_C11_890_ROOT_SOURCE_FOUND"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "AUDIT_C11_SOURCE_PROVENANCE_AND_EPISODE_IDENTITY"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "C11_890_ROOT_SOURCE_NOT_YET_RESOLVED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "SEARCH_HISTORICAL_C11_COLLECTOR_PROVENANCE_NARROWLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_890_ROOT_PROVENANCE_RESOLUTION_V1",

    "status":
        "PASS_READ_ONLY_PROVENANCE_RESOLUTION",

    "frozen_development_dataset": {
        "sha256":
            sha(data_p),

        "C11_action_rows":
            14240,

        "C11_roots":
            890,

        "C11_root_step_min":
            int(
                c11_steps.min()
            ),

        "C11_root_step_max":
            int(
                c11_steps.max()
            ),

        "joint_actions_per_root":
            16,
    },

    "prior_coverage_inventory_sha256":
        sha(
            coverage_p
        ),

    "content_based_candidates":
        candidates,

    "exact_resolution_candidates":
        exact,

    "classification":
        classification,

    "science_boundary": {
        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "C11_EXACT_SOURCE_CANDIDATE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(exact)
    )
)

for rec in exact[:10]:

    print(
        "C11_EXACT_SOURCE="
        + rec["path"]
    )

    print(
        "C11_EXACT_SOURCE_SHA256="
        + rec["sha256"]
    )


print(
    "C11_PROVENANCE_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "C11_PROVENANCE_RESOLUTION_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "C11_PROVENANCE_RESOLUTION_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)

print("NEW_COLLECTION_EXECUTED=False")
print("TRAINING_EXECUTED=False")
print("MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False")
print("C03_USED=False")
print("C13_USED=False")
print("V3_DESIGN_CHANGED=False")

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

思考了 4s
现在已经比上一步清楚很多了：

C11 的 890 roots 确实存在，不是数据缺失；

两个“exact source”其实是同一份 NPZ 的两个路径副本，SHA 完全相同，所以它们还不是我们要找的“原始采集 provenance”。


真正值得继续追的是 PAPER1_NEURAL_C11_C06_CURRENT_SEMANTIC_COLLECTOR_V1_UPLOAD.zip：里面明确有 C11 的 episode-manifest、neural-root-diagnostic-v1.jsonl、summary、Fleet result 和 collector source。


当前资源：低资源。

下一步只做一件事：证明 development dataset 中 C11 的 14240 action rows / 890 roots，是否与这个 collector ZIP 中的 C11 root JSONL 逐 root、逐 action 完全一致。如果一致，我们就能把 C11 现有数据正式锁定为一个 seed0/current-semantic episode，然后才有依据去新增 development episode。

(

EXPECTED_DATA=1fbc594cab90a5acad6f0d87b5f5719d1728c3c5969e9f949aba0ba37caf4c82
EXPECTED_ZIP=94ec9778a2899825125eaaed5c2ece50b8d0cd29390481c1e6f2ac568f052300
EXPECTED_RESOLUTION=beb86025ceb91e94a36342edd104522d19b8f833e0d9af2bacc460bc46c00808

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"

    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== VALUE V3 C11 EPISODE IDENTITY AUDIT ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$ZIP" "$EXPECTED_ZIP" C11_C06_COLLECTOR_ZIP
check_sha "$RESOLUTION" "$EXPECTED_RESOLUTION" PRIOR_RESOLUTION

    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false
}

"$ZIP" \
