"$OUT" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import zipfile
import numpy as np

data_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
zip_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
resolution_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[4])


def sha_bytes(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        b
    ).hexdigest()


def sha_file(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


# ============================================================
# 1. Read frozen development C11 rows
# ============================================================

with np.load(
    data_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    scene = np.asarray(
        z["scene"]
    ).astype(str).reshape(-1)

    root_step = np.asarray(
        z["root_step"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_A = np.asarray(
        z["action_A"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_B = np.asarray(
        z["action_B"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    q_value = np.asarray(
        z["q_value"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    immediate = np.asarray(
        z["immediate_reward"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    ).reshape(-1)


mask = scene == "C11"

assert int(mask.sum()) == 14240

dataset_records = {}

for idx in np.flatnonzero(mask):

    step = int(
        root_step[idx]
    )

    jid = int(
        action_A[idx] * 4
        + action_B[idx]
    )

    key = (
        step,
        jid,
    )

    assert key not in dataset_records

    dataset_records[key] = {
        "q_value":
            float(
                q_value[idx]
            ),

        "immediate_reward":
            float(
                immediate[idx]
            ),

        "action_A":
            int(
                action_A[idx]
            ),

        "action_B":
            int(
                action_B[idx]
            ),
    }


assert len(
    dataset_records
) == 14240

dataset_steps = sorted(
    {
        k[0]
        for k
        in dataset_records
    }
)

assert dataset_steps == list(
    range(890)
)

print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_ROWS=14240")
print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_ROOTS=890")
print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_STEP_RANGE=0..889")
print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_STRUCTURE_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Read authoritative C11 collector artifacts from ZIP
# ============================================================

ROOT_MEMBER = (
    "C11/"
    "cross-scene-c11-fleet-mcts-seed0-"
    "paper1-neural-root-diagnostic-v1.jsonl"
)

SUMMARY_MEMBER = (
    "C11/"
    "cross-scene-c11-fleet-mcts-seed0-"
    "paper1-neural-root-diagnostic-v1-summary.json"
)

EPISODE_MEMBER = (
    "C11/"
    "cross-scene-c11-fleet-mcts-seed0-"
    "paper1-neural-episode-manifest-v1.json"
)

RESULT_MEMBER = (
    "C11/"
    "cross-scene-c11-fleet-mcts-seed0-v2.json"
)

SOURCE_MEMBER = (
    "src/"
    "cross_scene_fleet_mcts_c11_seed0_"
    "paper1_neural_collector_v1.py"
)


with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    names = set(
        z.namelist()
    )

    for required in (
        ROOT_MEMBER,
        SUMMARY_MEMBER,
        EPISODE_MEMBER,
        RESULT_MEMBER,
        SOURCE_MEMBER,
    ):
        assert required in names, (
            "MISSING_ZIP_MEMBER",
            required,
        )

    root_raw = z.read(
        ROOT_MEMBER
    )

    summary_raw = z.read(
        SUMMARY_MEMBER
    )

    episode_raw = z.read(
        EPISODE_MEMBER
    )

    result_raw = z.read(
        RESULT_MEMBER
    )

    source_raw = z.read(
        SOURCE_MEMBER
    )


print(
    "C11_ROOT_JSONL_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(root_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_ROOT_SUMMARY_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(summary_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_EPISODE_MANIFEST_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(episode_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_FLEET_RESULT_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(result_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_COLLECTOR_SOURCE_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(source_raw)
)


summary = json.loads(
    summary_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    )
)

episode = json.loads(
    episode_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    )
)

fleet_result = json.loads(
    result_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Parse root JSONL
# ============================================================

root_records = []

for line in root_raw.decode(
    "utf-8"
).splitlines():

    if not line.strip():
        continue

    obj = json.loads(
        line
    )

    root_records.append(
        obj
    )


print(
    "COLLECTOR_C11_ROOT_RECORD_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(root_records)
    )
)

assert len(
    root_records
) == 890


collector_records = {}

root_steps_seen = []


for ri, root in enumerate(
    root_records
):

    step = int(
        root["root_step"]
    )

    root_steps_seen.append(
        step
    )

    actions = root[
        "actionwise"
    ]

    assert len(
        actions
    ) == 16

    joint_ids = []

    for action in actions:

        jid = int(
            action[
                "joint_action_id"
            ]
        )

        joint_ids.append(
            jid
        )

        # Prefer explicit action pair if present.
        act = action.get(
            "action"
        )

        if isinstance(
            act,
            dict,
        ):
            a = int(
                act.get(
                    "A",
                    act.get(
                        "action_A"
                    )
                )
            )

            b = int(
                act.get(
                    "B",
                    act.get(
                        "action_B"
                    )
                )
            )

        elif isinstance(
            act,
            (list, tuple),
        ):
            assert len(act) == 2

            a = int(act[0])
            b = int(act[1])

        else:
            # Frozen joint action contract:
            # A_action * 4 + B_action.
            a = jid // 4
            b = jid % 4

        assert (
            a * 4 + b
        ) == jid

        key = (
            step,
            jid,
        )

        assert key not in collector_records

        collector_records[
            key
        ] = {
            "q_value":
                float(
                    action[
                        "q_value"
                    ]
                ),

            "immediate_reward":
                float(
                    action[
                        "immediate_reward"
                    ]
                ),

            "action_A":
                a,

            "action_B":
                b,
        }

    assert sorted(
        joint_ids
    ) == list(
        range(16)
    )


assert sorted(
    root_steps_seen
) == list(
    range(890)
)

assert len(
    collector_records
) == 14240

print("COLLECTOR_C11_ROOTS=890")
print("COLLECTOR_C11_ACTION_ROWS=14240")
print("COLLECTOR_C11_STEP_RANGE=0..889")
print("COLLECTOR_16_ACTION_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 4. Exact identity comparison
# ============================================================

assert set(
    dataset_records
) == set(
    collector_records
)

q_mismatch = []
imm_mismatch = []
action_mismatch = []

max_q_abs_error = 0.0
max_imm_abs_error = 0.0


for key in sorted(
    dataset_records
):

    d = dataset_records[
        key
    ]

    c = collector_records[
        key
    ]

    if (
        d["action_A"]
        != c["action_A"]
        or d["action_B"]
        != c["action_B"]
    ):
        action_mismatch.append(
            key
        )

    qerr = abs(
        d["q_value"]
        - c["q_value"]
    )

    ierr = abs(
        d["immediate_reward"]
        - c["immediate_reward"]
    )

    max_q_abs_error = max(
        max_q_abs_error,
        qerr,
    )

    max_imm_abs_error = max(
        max_imm_abs_error,
        ierr,
    )

    if qerr != 0.0:
        q_mismatch.append(
            (
                key,
                d["q_value"],
                c["q_value"],
                qerr,
            )
        )

    if ierr != 0.0:
        imm_mismatch.append(
            (
                key,
                d["immediate_reward"],
                c["immediate_reward"],
                ierr,
            )
        )


print(
    "ACTION_IDENTITY_MISMATCH_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            action_mismatch
        )
    )
)

print(
    "Q_VALUE_MISMATCH_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            q_mismatch
        )
    )
)

print(
    "IMMEDIATE_REWARD_MISMATCH_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            imm_mismatch
        )
    )
)

print(
    "MAX_Q_VALUE_ABS_ERROR="
    + repr(
        max_q_abs_error
    )
)

print(
    "MAX_IMMEDIATE_REWARD_ABS_ERROR="
    + repr(
        max_imm_abs_error
    )
)


# Dataset construction may have passed through float32;
# accept only numerical representation-level differences.
Q_TOL = 1e-5
IMM_TOL = 1e-5

q_identity = (
    max_q_abs_error
    <= Q_TOL
)

imm_identity = (
    max_imm_abs_error
    <= IMM_TOL
)

action_identity = (
    len(
        action_mismatch
    )
    == 0
)


print(
    "C11_ACTION_IDENTITY="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if action_identity
        else "FAIL"
    )
)

print(
    "C11_Q_IDENTITY="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if q_identity
        else "FAIL"
    )
)

print(
    "C11_IMMEDIATE_IDENTITY="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if imm_identity
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 5. Episode identity / outcome
# ============================================================

print()
print("=== C11 EPISODE IDENTITY ===")

for label, obj in (
    (
        "EPISODE",
        episode,
    ),
    (
        "FLEET",
        fleet_result,
    ),
    (
        "ROOT_SUMMARY",
        summary,
    ),
):

    for key in (
        "scene",
        "seed",
        "status",
        "outcome",
        "benchmark_success",
        "root_record_count",
        "root_count",
        "derived_action_record_count",
        "duration_s",
        "iterations",
        "timeline_sync",
        "timeline_synchronized",
        "error",
    ):
        if (
            isinstance(
                obj,
                dict,
            )
            and key in obj
        ):
            print(
                f"{label}_{key}="
                + repr(
                    obj[key]
                )
            )

if (

payload = {

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"

    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

思考了几秒
当前不是科学失败，是解析器对 action 字段格式的假设错了。

报错：

ValueError: invalid literal for int() with base 10: '0,0'
说明 collector JSONL 里的 action 不是整数对/list，而很可能是字符串形式：

"0,0"
上一版代码把它直接 int() 了，所以在真正做 identity comparison 之前就停了。现有证据已经确认 collector 本身有 890 root records，因此先只锁定 action / joint_action_id 的真实 schema，不重新跑 identity audit。当前资源：低资源。

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
from collections import Counter

zip_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
member = sys.argv[2]

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert member in z.namelist()

    raw = z.read(
        member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


roots = [
    json.loads(line)
    for line in raw.splitlines()
    if line.strip()
]

assert len(roots) == 890

print("ROOT_COUNT=890")

type_counts = Counter()
examples = []
joint_mismatch = []
parsed_count = 0


def parse_action(value):
    if isinstance(value, dict):

        if (
            "A" in value
            and "B" in value
        ):
            return (
                int(value["A"]),
                int(value["B"]),
                "dict_A_B",
            )

        if (
            "action_A" in value
            and "action_B" in value
        ):
            return (
                int(value["action_A"]),
                int(value["action_B"]),
                "dict_action_A_B",
            )

        return None

    if isinstance(
        value,
        (list, tuple),
    ):

        if len(value) == 2:
            return (
                int(value[0]),
                int(value[1]),
                "sequence",
            )

        return None

    if isinstance(
        value,
        str,
    ):

        s = value.strip()

        for sep in (
            ",",
            "|",
            "/",
            ":",
            " ",
        ):

            parts = [
                x.strip()
                for x in s.split(sep)
                if x.strip()
            ]

            if len(parts) == 2:

                try:
                    return (
                        int(parts[0]),
                        int(parts[1]),
                        "string_" + repr(sep),
                    )

                except ValueError:
                    pass

        return None

    return None


for ri, root in enumerate(
    roots
):

    actions = root[
        "actionwise"
    ]

    assert len(actions) == 16

    for ai, item in enumerate(
        actions
    ):

        value = item.get(
            "action"
        )

        type_counts[
            type(value).__name__
        ] += 1

        if len(examples) < 32:
            examples.append(
                {
                    "root_index": ri,
                    "root_step":
                        root.get(
                            "root_step"
                        ),
                    "action_index": ai,
                    "joint_action_id":
                        item.get(
                            "joint_action_id"
                        ),
                    "action_type":
                        type(value).__name__,
                    "action_repr":
                        repr(value),
                }
            )

        parsed = parse_action(
            value
        )

        if parsed is None:

            print(
                "UNPARSED_ACTION="
                + repr(
                    {
                        "root_index": ri,
                        "root_step":
                            root.get(
                                "root_step"
                            ),
                        "action_index": ai,
                        "joint_action_id":
                            item.get(
                                "joint_action_id"
                            ),
                        "action":
                            value,
                    }
                )
            )

            raise RuntimeError(
                "ACTION_SCHEMA_UNRESOLVED"
            )

        a, b, parser = parsed

        jid = int(
            item[
                "joint_action_id"
            ]
        )

        parsed_count += 1

        if (
            a * 4 + b
            != jid
        ):
            joint_mismatch.append(
                {
                    "root_index": ri,
                    "root_step":
                        root.get(
                            "root_step"
                        ),
                    "joint_action_id":
                        jid,
                    "action":
                        value,
                    "parsed_A":
                        a,
                    "parsed_B":
                        b,
                    "parser":
                        parser,
                }
            )


print(
    "ACTION_TYPE_COUNTS="
    + repr(
        dict(type_counts)
    )
)

print()
print("=== ACTION EXAMPLES ===")

for x in examples:
    print(
        "ACTION_EXAMPLE="
        + repr(x)
    )


print()

    "PARSED_ACTION_COUNT="
    + str(parsed_count)
)

print(
    "EXPECTED_ACTION_COUNT="
    + str(
        890 * 16
    )
)

print(
    "JOINT_ACTION_MISMATCH_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            joint_mismatch
        )
    )
)

for x in joint_mismatch[:20]:
    print(
        "JOINT_ACTION_MISMATCH="
        + repr(x)
    )


assert parsed_count == 14240

assert (
    len(
        joint_mismatch
    )
    == 0
)

    "ACTION_SCHEMA_RESOLUTION=PASS"
)

    "IDENTITY_AUDIT_NOT_RERUN=True"
)

)

当前资源：低资源即可。

现在 schema 已经完全锁死：

action：14240 条全部是 dict {'A':x,'B':y}

joint_action_id：14240 条全部是字符串 "A,B"

16 种组合各出现 890 次

所以精确映射就是
joint_index = A * 4 + B
而字符串 "A,B" 只是 collector 的外部标识，不是整数 id。

下一步只修 identity audit 的 parser，然后继续原来那次逐 root / 逐 action 对齐。不训练、不采集、不改科研设计。

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 EPISODE IDENTITY AUDIT V2 ==="

    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$DATA" "$EXPECTED_DATA" DEVELOPMENT_DATASET
check_sha "$ZIP" "$EXPECTED_ZIP" COLLECTOR_ZIP
check_sha "$RESOLUTION" "$EXPECTED_RESOLUTION" PRIOR_RESOLUTION

    false

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import zipfile
import numpy as np

(
    data_p,
    zip_p,
    resolution_p,
    out_p,
    root_member,
    summary_member,
    episode_member,
    result_member,
    source_member,
) = sys.argv[1:]

data_p = Path(data_p)
zip_p = Path(zip_p)
resolution_p = Path(resolution_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha_file(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def sha_bytes(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        b
    ).hexdigest()


# ============================================================
# 1. Frozen development C11 rows
# ============================================================

with np.load(
    data_p,
    allow_pickle=False,
) as z:

    scene = np.asarray(
        z["scene"]
    ).astype(str).reshape(-1)

    root_step = np.asarray(
        z["root_step"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_A = np.asarray(
        z["action_A"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    action_B = np.asarray(
        z["action_B"],
        dtype=np.int64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    q_value = np.asarray(
        z["q_value"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    ).reshape(-1)

    immediate = np.asarray(
        z["immediate_reward"],
        dtype=np.float64,
    ).reshape(-1)


mask = scene == "C11"

assert int(
    mask.sum()
) == 14240

dataset = {}

for idx in np.flatnonzero(
    mask
):

    step = int(
        root_step[idx]
    )

    a = int(
        action_A[idx]
    )

    b = int(
        action_B[idx]
    )

    assert 0 <= a <= 3
    assert 0 <= b <= 3

    j = (
        a * 4 + b
    )

    key = (
        step,
        j,
    )

    assert key not in dataset

    dataset[key] = {
        "A": a,
        "B": b,
        "joint_index": j,
        "q_value":
            float(
                q_value[idx]
            ),
        "immediate_reward":
            float(
                immediate[idx]
            ),
    }


assert len(dataset) == 14240

steps = sorted(
    {
        k[0]
        for k in dataset
    }
)

assert steps == list(
    range(890)
)

print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_ROOTS=890")
print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_ROWS=14240")
print("DEVELOPMENT_C11_STEP_RANGE=0..889")
print("DEVELOPMENT_ACTION_MAPPING=A*4+B")
print("DEVELOPMENT_STRUCTURE_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Read collector artifacts
# ============================================================

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    names = set(
        z.namelist()
    )

    for member in (
        root_member,
        summary_member,
        episode_member,
        result_member,
        source_member,
    ):
        assert member in names, (
            "MISSING_MEMBER",
            member,
        )

    root_raw = z.read(
        root_member
    )

    summary_raw = z.read(
        summary_member
    )

    episode_raw = z.read(
        episode_member
    )

    result_raw = z.read(
        result_member
    )

    source_raw = z.read(
        source_member
    )


print(
    "C11_ROOT_JSONL_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(root_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_ROOT_SUMMARY_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(summary_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_EPISODE_MANIFEST_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(episode_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_FLEET_RESULT_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(result_raw)
)

print(
    "C11_COLLECTOR_SOURCE_SHA256="
    + sha_bytes(source_raw)
)


summary = json.loads(
    summary_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    )
)

episode = json.loads(
    episode_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    )
)

fleet = json.loads(
    result_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    )
)


roots = [
    json.loads(line)
    for line in root_raw.decode(
        "utf-8"
    ).splitlines()
    if line.strip()
]

assert len(roots) == 890

print(
    "COLLECTOR_C11_ROOT_RECORD_COUNT=890"
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Exact frozen action mapping
# ============================================================

collector = {}

mapping_mismatch = []

for root in roots:

    step = int(
        root["root_step"]
    )

    actions = root[
        "actionwise"
    ]

    assert len(actions) == 16

    seen = set()

    for item in actions:

        act = item[
            "action"
        ]

        assert isinstance(
            act,
            dict,
        )

        assert set(
            act.keys()
        ) >= {
            "A",
            "B",
        }

        a = int(
            act["A"]
        )

        b = int(
            act["B"]
        )

        assert 0 <= a <= 3
        assert 0 <= b <= 3

        external_id = str(
            item[
                "joint_action_id"
            ]
        )

        expected_external = (
            f"{a},{b}"
        )

        joint_index = (
            a * 4 + b
        )

        if (
            external_id
            != expected_external
        ):
            mapping_mismatch.append(
                {
                    "root_step":
                        step,
                    "A":
                        a,
                    "B":
                        b,
                    "joint_action_id":
                        external_id,
                    "expected":
                        expected_external,
                }
            )

        seen.add(
            joint_index
        )

        key = (
            step,
            joint_index,
        )

        assert key not in collector

        collector[key] = {
            "A": a,
            "B": b,
            "joint_index":
                joint_index,
            "joint_action_id":
                external_id,
            "q_value":
                float(
                    item[
                        "q_value"
                    ]
                ),
            "immediate_reward":
                float(
                    item[
                        "immediate_reward"
                    ]
                ),
        }

    assert seen == set(
        range(16)
    )


assert len(
    collector
) == 14240

assert sorted(
    {
        k[0]
        for k
        in collector
    }
) == list(
    range(890)
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_C11_ROOTS=890"
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_C11_ACTION_ROWS=14240"
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_ACTION_DICT_SCHEMA=PASS"
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_EXTERNAL_ACTION_ID_SCHEMA=A_COMMA_B"
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_INTERNAL_JOINT_INDEX=A*4+B"
)

print(
    "ACTION_MAPPING_MISMATCH_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            mapping_mismatch
        )
    )
)

assert not mapping_mismatch

print(
    "ACTION_MAPPING_CONTRACT=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 4. Exact root/action identity
# ============================================================

assert set(
    dataset.keys()
) == set(
    collector.keys()
)

action_mismatch = 0
q_mismatch_count = 0
imm_mismatch_count = 0

max_q_error = 0.0
max_imm_error = 0.0


for key in sorted(
    dataset.keys()
):

    d = dataset[
        key
    ]

    c = collector[
        key
    ]

    if (
        d["A"] != c["A"]
        or d["B"] != c["B"]
        or d["joint_index"]
        != c["joint_index"]
    ):
        action_mismatch += 1

    qerr = abs(
        d["q_value"]
        - c["q_value"]
    )

    ierr = abs(
        d["immediate_reward"]
        - c["immediate_reward"]
    )

    max_q_error = max(
        max_q_error,
        qerr,
    )

    max_imm_error = max(
        max_imm_error,
        ierr,
    )

    if qerr != 0.0:
        q_mismatch_count += 1

    if ierr != 0.0:
        imm_mismatch_count += 1


Q_TOL = 1e-5
IMM_TOL = 1e-5

action_pass = (
    action_mismatch == 0
)

q_pass = (
    max_q_error <= Q_TOL
)

imm_pass = (
    max_imm_error <= IMM_TOL
)

        if action_pass
        else "FAIL"

思考了 7s
结果已经锁死：C11 的 890 roots 确实全部来自同一个 current-semantic、seed0、89 秒的连续 episode，不是 890 个独立样本 episode。 Q_VALUE_MISMATCH_COUNT=14240 和 IMMEDIATE...=14239 只是 dataset 转为 float32 后的表示差异，最大误差分别只有 3.81e-6 和 2.96e-8，均在预先设定的 1e-5 identity tolerance 内，所以 provenance identity 是 PASS。

当前资源：低资源。

下一步先把这个结论正式冻结，同时只读检查 C11 collector 的可控 episode 变化参数。在看清 source 之前不擅自决定“新 seed 就算新 coverage”还是必须改变初始状态。

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import zipfile
import ast
import re

(
    identity_p,
    zip_p,
    freeze_p,

    source_member,
    episode_member,
    result_member,
) = sys.argv[1:]

identity_p = Path(identity_p)
zip_p = Path(zip_p)
freeze_p = Path(freeze_p)
inventory_p = Path(inventory_p)


def sha_file(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def sha_bytes(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        b
    ).hexdigest()


identity = json.loads(
    identity_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    identity["status"]
    == "PASS_EXACT_EPISODE_IDENTITY"
)

assert (
    identity["classification"]
    ==
    "C11_DEVELOPMENT_DATA_EXACTLY_BOUND_TO_CURRENT_SEMANTIC_SEED0_EPISODE"
)

assert (
    identity[
        "identity"
    ][
        "dataset_roots"
    ]
    == 890
)

assert (
    identity[
        "identity"
    ][
        "dataset_action_rows"
    ]
    == 14240
)

assert (
    identity[
        "identity"
    ][
        "action_identity"
    ]
    is True
)

assert (
    identity[
        "identity"
    ][
        "q_identity_within_1e_5"
    ]
    is True
)

assert (
    identity[
        "identity"
    ][
        "immediate_identity_within_1e_5"
    ]
    is True
)

print(
    "C11_SINGLE_EPISODE_IDENTITY_GATE=PASS"

# ============================================================
# 1. Read exact historical source / episode metadata
# ============================================================

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    names = set(
        z.namelist()
    )

    for name in (
        source_member,
        episode_member,
        result_member,
    ):
        assert name in names

    source_raw = z.read(
        source_member
    )

    episode_raw = z.read(
        episode_member
    )

# ============================================================
# 3. Static AST inventory of collector knobs
# ============================================================

tree = ast.parse(
    source
)

assignments = []

for node in ast.walk(
    tree
):

    if isinstance(
        node,
        ast.Assign,
    ):

        for target in node.targets:

            if not isinstance(
                target,
                ast.Name,
            ):
                continue

            name = target.id

            try:
                value = ast.literal_eval(
                    node.value
                )
            except Exception:
                continue

            assignments.append(
                {
                    "name":
                        name,

                    "value":
                        value,

                    "line":
                        node.lineno,
                }
            )

    elif isinstance(
        node,
        ast.AnnAssign,
    ):

        if not isinstance(
            node.target,
            ast.Name,
        ):
            continue

        try:
            value = ast.literal_eval(
                node.value
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        assignments.append(
            {
                "name":
                    node.target.id,

                "value":
                    value,

                "line":
                    node.lineno,
            }
        )


tokens = (
    "SEED",
    "START",
    "INITIAL",
    "SPEED",
    "ACCEL",
    "OFFSET",
    "ROUTE",
    "SCENARIO",
    "MAX_STEP",
    "MAX_ITER",
    "DURATION",
    "HORIZON",
    "BUDGET",
    "DEPTH",
    "DT",
    "CONFLICT",
    "GOAL",
)


knobs = [
    x
    for x in assignments
    if any(
        token in x[
            "name"
        ].upper()
        for token in tokens
    )
]


print()
print(
    "C11_SOURCE_LITERAL_KNOB_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(knobs)
    )
)

for x in knobs:

    print(
        "C11_SOURCE_KNOB="
        + repr(x)
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Search for environment/CLI/runtime variation surfaces
# ============================================================

variation_lines = []

patterns = (
    r"os\.environ",
    r"os\.getenv",
    r"argparse",
    r"add_argument",
    r"random\.seed",
    r"np\.random",
    r"manual_seed",
    r"seed",
    r"initial",
    r"start_s",
    r"speed",
    r"route",
    r"scenario",
    r"budget",
    r"depth",
)


for i, line in enumerate(
    source.splitlines(),
    1,
):

    if any(
        re.search(
            p,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        )
        for p in patterns
    ):
        variation_lines.append(
            {
                "line":
                    i,

                "text":
                    line.strip(),
            }
        )


print()
print(
    "C11_VARIATION_SOURCE_LINE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            variation_lines
        )
    )
)

for x in variation_lines[:120]:

    print(
        "C11_VARIATION_SOURCE_LINE="
        + repr(x)
    )


# ============================================================
# 5. Episode/result metadata
# ============================================================

def flatten(
    obj,
    prefix="",
):
    rows = []

    if isinstance(
        obj,
        dict,
    ):

        for k, v in obj.items():

            name = (
                f"{prefix}.{k}"
                if prefix
                else str(k)
            )

            rows.extend(
                flatten(
                    v,
                    name,
                )
            )

    elif isinstance(
        obj,
        list,
    ):

        if len(obj) <= 20:
            rows.append(
                (
                    prefix,
                    obj,
                )
            )

    else:
        rows.append(
            (
                prefix,
                obj,
            )
        )

    return rows


interesting_meta = []

for source_name, obj in (
    (
        "episode",
        episode,
    ),
    (
        "fleet",
        result,
    ),
):

    for key, value in flatten(
        obj
    ):

        lk = key.lower()

        if any(
            token in lk
            for token in (
                "seed",
                "start",
                "initial",
                "speed",
                "route",
                "scenario",
                "conflict",
                "duration",
                "iteration",
                "budget",
                "depth",
                "goal",
                "outcome",
                "status",
            )
        ):
            interesting_meta.append(
                {
                    "source":
                        source_name,

                    "key":
                        key,

                    "value":
                        value,
                }
            )


print()
print(
    "C11_EPISODE_VARIATION_METADATA_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            interesting_meta
        )
    )
)

for x in interesting_meta:

    print(
        "C11_EPISODE_VARIATION_METADATA="
        + repr(x)
    )


# ============================================================
# 6. Freeze inventory only — no expansion design yet
# ============================================================

inventory = {
    "type":

    "status":
        "PASS_STATIC_VARIATION_KNOB_INVENTORY",

    "single_episode_freeze_sha256":
        sha_file(
            freeze_p

        sha_bytes(
            source_raw

    "source_literal_knobs":

        "new_seed_selected":

            False,

        "new_speed_selected":

        "new_route_selected":
            False,

    },

inventory_p.write_text(

文档

思考了 11s
现在可以定方向了：

C11 当前 development 数据确实只有 1 个 seed0 episode，890 个 root 只是同一条 89s 轨迹的连续时间点。


不能靠换 seed 扩 coverage：runner 明确 SEED != 0 就直接报错。


MCTS budget/depth、target speed 都是当前算法配置，不应该为了“扩数据”去动。

当前初始位置恰好是 A、B 各距冲突点约 80m；最干净的扩展维度就是保持路线、速度、MCTS 参数全部不变，只改变双方相对冲突点的初始 route-s 距离。


但在正式预注册 60m/100m 等新 episode 前，先做一个静态可行域检查，确认同两条 frozen route 上这些位置确实存在且有足够前后余量。不要先造 scenario。

当前资源：低资源。

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_FREEZE=37dc72decdc819a243161b42e68248c48d1361e460897c9cd1373a20e903c4d5
EXPECTED_KNOBS=b57d554da84cfd7f28e0b5afa145a4ac860a4c6e4fe92b3dde06a3c83eec0aa7
EXPECTED_MANIFEST=7bc4a6e327557bb038cd5da32c8b4af76976f470f2e8369201248a0cec7cfe12

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 START-DISTANCE COVERAGE FEASIBILITY ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$FREEZE" "$EXPECTED_FREEZE" SINGLE_EPISODE_FREEZE
check_sha "$KNOBS" "$EXPECTED_KNOBS" KNOB_INVENTORY
check_sha "$MANIFEST" "$EXPECTED_MANIFEST" C11_MANIFEST

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false
}

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()

    freeze_p.read_text(

assert (

    == "PASS_STATIC_VARIATION_KNOB_INVENTORY"

    knobs["decision_boundary"][
        "new_collection_authorized"
    ]
    is False
)

# ============================================================

# ============================================================

    manifest["route_a"]
)

route_b = list(
    manifest["route_b"]
)

    manifest[
        "conflict_route_s"
    ]["A"]
)

conflict_B = float(
    manifest[
        "conflict_route_s"
    ]["B"]
)

    manifest[
        "start_route_s"
    ]["A"]
)

start_B = float(
    manifest[
        "start_route_s"
    ]["B"]
)

distance_A = (
    conflict_A
    - start_A
)

distance_B = (
    conflict_B
    - start_B
)

print("C11_ROUTE_A=" + repr(route_a))
print("C11_ROUTE_B=" + repr(route_b))

print(
    "C11_CONFLICT_S_A="
    + repr(conflict_A)
)

    "C11_CONFLICT_S_B="

)

    + repr(distance_B)
)


assert abs(
    distance_A - 80.0
) < 1e-4

assert abs(
    distance_B - 80.0
) < 1e-4

print(
    "ORIGINAL_80M_SYMMETRIC_START_GATE=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Candidate coverage offsets
#
# Development coverage only:
# fixed routes, fixed MCTS, fixed target speed.
#
# Existing 80m remains historical episode.
# New candidate distances are +/-20m around 80m.
# ============================================================

candidate_distances = [
    60.0,
    100.0,
]

candidates = []

for d in candidate_distances:

    a_s = (
        conflict_A - d
    )

    b_s = (
        conflict_B - d
    )

    valid_nonnegative = (
        a_s >= 0.0
        and b_s >= 0.0
    )

    distinct_from_existing = (
        abs(d - 80.0)
        > 1e-9
    )

    candidate = {
        "distance_to_conflict_m":
            d,

        "A_start_route_s":
            a_s,

        "B_start_route_s":
            b_s,

        "nonnegative_route_s":
            valid_nonnegative,

        "distinct_from_existing_80m":
            distinct_from_existing,
    }

    candidates.append(
        candidate
    )

    print()
    print(
        "CANDIDATE_DISTANCE_M="
        + repr(d)
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_A_START_S="
        + repr(a_s)
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_B_START_S="
        + repr(b_s)
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_NONNEGATIVE_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(valid_nonnegative)
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_DISTINCT_FROM_80M="
        + repr(distinct_from_existing)
    )


assert all(
    x["nonnegative_route_s"]
    for x in candidates
)

assert all(
    x[
        "distinct_from_existing_80m"
    ]
    for x in candidates
)

print()
print(
    "START_DISTANCE_BASIC_FEASIBILITY=PASS"

payload = {

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"

    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 60M/100M SCENARIO CONSTRUCTIBILITY AUDIT ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$FEAS" "$EXPECTED_FEAS" START_DISTANCE_FEASIBILITY
check_sha "$SCENARIO" "$EXPECTED_SCENARIO" C11_SCENARIO
check_sha "$MANIFEST" "$EXPECTED_MANIFEST" C11_MANIFEST
check_sha "$ZIP" "$EXPECTED_ZIP" COLLECTOR_ZIP

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false
}

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

python - \
"$FEAS" \
"$SCENARIO" \
"$MANIFEST" \
"$ZIP" \
"$OUT" \
"$SOURCE_MEMBER" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import datetime
import re

(
    feas_p,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
    zip_p,
    out_p,
    source_member,
) = sys.argv[1:]

feas_p = Path(feas_p)
scenario_p = Path(scenario_p)
manifest_p = Path(manifest_p)
zip_p = Path(zip_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


feas = json.loads(
    feas_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

manifest = json.loads(
    manifest_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

scenario = json.loads(
    scenario_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    feas["status"]
    == "PASS_BASIC_ROUTE_S_FEASIBILITY"
)

assert (
    feas["important_boundary"][
        "new_collection_authorized"
    ]
    is False
)

print("PRIOR_FEASIBILITY_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Read exact collector source
# ============================================================

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


# ============================================================
# 2. Inventory scenario structure
# ============================================================

print(
    "SCENARIO_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS="
    + repr(
        sorted(
            scenario.keys()
        )
    )
)

traj = scenario.get(
    "TrajSegmentInfo"
)

assert isinstance(
    traj,
    list,
)

assert len(traj) >= 1

segment = traj[0]

print(
    "TRAJ_SEGMENT_KEYS="
    + repr(
        sorted(
            segment.keys()
        )
    )
)

states = segment.get(
    "states"
)

assert isinstance(
    states,
    list,
)

assert len(states) >= 2

print(
    "SCENARIO_STATE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(states)
    )
)

print(
    "STATE0_KEYS="
    + repr(
        sorted(
            states[0].keys()
        )
    )
)

print(
    "STATE1_KEYS="
    + repr(
        sorted(
            states[1].keys()
        )
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Print first states conservatively
# ============================================================

def compact(obj):
    if not isinstance(
        obj,
        dict,
    ):
        return obj

    keep = {}

    for k, v in obj.items():

        lk = k.lower()

        if any(
            token in lk
            for token in (
                "x",
                "y",
                "heading",
                "yaw",
                "velocity",
                "speed",
                "time",
                "timestamp",
                "token",
                "id",
                "agent",
                "ego",
            )
        ):
            keep[k] = v

    return keep


print()
print(
    "STATE0_COMPACT="
    + repr(
        compact(
            states[0]
        )
    )
)

print(
    "STATE1_COMPACT="
    + repr(
        compact(
            states[1]
        )
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 4. Determine which initial-state surfaces collector uses
# ============================================================

source_lines = source.splitlines()

required_patterns = {
    "scenario_doc_states":
        r'scenario_doc\s*=\s*json\.loads.*ASSET',

    "raw_states":
        r'raw_states\s*=\s*scenario_doc\["TrajSegmentInfo"\]\[0\]\["states"\]',

    "scenario_time0":
        r'get_time_point\(0\)',

    "initial_ego_state":
        r'initial_ego_state',

    "b_initial":
        r'b_initial',

    "raw_n1":
        r'raw_n1',

    "location_control":
        r'LOCATION_POINT_MATCHES_CONTROLLED_REAR_AXLE',
}


hits = {}

for name, pattern in required_patterns.items():

    matched = []

    for lineno, line in enumerate(
        source_lines,
        1,
    ):

        if re.search(
            pattern,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        ):
            matched.append(
                {
                    "line":
                        lineno,

                    "text":
                        line.strip(),
                }
            )

    hits[name] = matched

    print()
    print(
        name.upper()
        + "_HIT_COUNT="
        + str(
            len(matched)
        )
    )

    for x in matched[:20]:

        print(
            name.upper()
            + "_LINE="
            + repr(x)
        )


# ============================================================
# 5. Inspect manifest identity
# ============================================================

route_a = list(
    manifest["route_a"]
)

route_b = list(
    manifest["route_b"]
)

start_A = float(
    manifest[
        "start_route_s"
    ]["A"]
)

start_B = float(
    manifest[
        "start_route_s"
    ]["B"]
)

conflict_A = float(
    manifest[
        "conflict_route_s"
    ]["A"]
)

conflict_B = float(
    manifest[
        "conflict_route_s"
    ]["B"]
)


print()
print(
    "ROUTE_A="
    + repr(route_a)
)

print(
    "ROUTE_B="
    + repr(route_b)
)

print(
    "START_S_A="
    + repr(start_A)
)

print(
    "START_S_B="
    + repr(start_B)
)

print(
    "CONFLICT_S_A="
    + repr(conflict_A)
)

print(
    "CONFLICT_S_B="
    + repr(conflict_B)
)


# ============================================================
# 6. Candidate start_route_s from frozen feasibility lock
# ============================================================

candidates = (
    feas[
        "candidate_start_distance_probe"
    ][
        "candidates"
    ]
)

assert len(candidates) == 2

for c in candidates:

    print()
    print(
        "CANDIDATE_DISTANCE_M="
        + repr(
            c[
                "distance_to_conflict_m"
            ]
        )
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_A_START_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(
            c[
                "A_start_route_s"
            ]
        )
    )

    print(
        "CANDIDATE_B_START_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(
            c[
                "B_start_route_s"
            ]
        )
    )


# ============================================================
# 7. Static constructibility decision
#
# We are NOT claiming drivability/runtime success.
# Only establish whether scenario initial states are explicit,
# isolated, and the collector derives initial state from them.
# ============================================================

scenario_state_surface_present = (
    isinstance(states, list)
    and len(states) >= 2
)

collector_reads_raw_states = (
    len(
        hits[
            "raw_states"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

collector_reads_time0 = (
    len(
        hits[
            "scenario_time0"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

collector_uses_initial_state = (
    len(
        hits[
            "initial_ego_state"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "b_initial"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

location_contract_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "location_control"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)


static_constructibility = all(
    (
        scenario_state_surface_present,
        collector_reads_raw_states,
        collector_reads_time0,
        collector_uses_initial_state,
        location_contract_present,
    )
)


print()
print(
    "SCENARIO_STATE_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        scenario_state_surface_present
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_READS_RAW_STATES="
    + repr(
        collector_reads_raw_states
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_READS_TIME0_STATE="
    + repr(
        collector_reads_time0
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_USES_INITIAL_STATE="
    + repr(
        collector_uses_initial_state
    )
)

print(
    "LOCATION_CONTROL_CONTRACT_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        location_contract_present
    )
)

print(
    "STATIC_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if static_constructibility
        else "HOLD"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 8. Freeze audit
# ============================================================

if static_constructibility:

    classification = (
        "C11_60M_100M_INITIAL_STATE_VARIANTS_STATICALLY_CONSTRUCTIBLE"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "PREREGISTER_C11_60M_100M_COVERAGE_EXPANSION"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "C11_INITIAL_STATE_CONSTRUCTION_SURFACE_NOT_FULLY_RESOLVED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_C11_SCENARIO_INITIAL_STATE_FIELDS_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_STATIC_CONSTRUCTIBILITY"
            if static_constructibility
            else
            "HOLD_STATIC_CONSTRUCTIBILITY"
        ),

    "start_distance_feasibility_sha256":
        sha(feas_p),

    "scenario_sha256":
        sha(scenario_p),

    "manifest_sha256":
        sha(manifest_p),

    "scenario_structure": {
        "top_level_keys":
            sorted(
                scenario.keys()
            ),

        "traj_segment_keys":
            sorted(
                segment.keys()
            ),

        "state_count":
            len(states),

        "state0_keys":
            sorted(
                states[0].keys()
            ),

        "state1_keys":
            sorted(
                states[1].keys()
            ),
    },

    "collector_initial_state_contract": {
        "scenario_state_surface_present":
            scenario_state_surface_present,

        "reads_raw_states":
            collector_reads_raw_states,

        "reads_time0_state":
            collector_reads_time0,

        "uses_initial_state":
            collector_uses_initial_state,

        "location_control_contract_present":
            location_contract_present,
    },

    "candidate_design": {
        "distances_m":
            [
                60.0,
                100.0,
            ],

        "route_tokens_changed":
            False,

        "conflict_point_changed":
            False,

        "MCTS_budget_changed":
            False,

        "MCTS_depth_changed":
            False,

        "target_speed_changed":
            False,

        "seed_changed":
            False,
    },

    "important_boundary": {
        "static_constructibility_only":
            static_constructibility,

        "candidate_scenario_files_created":
            False,

        "scenario_loader_executed":
            False,

        "bitmap_drivability_proven":
            False,

        "runtime_initialization_proven":
            False,

        "causal_conflict_preservation_proven":
            False,

        "new_collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "V3_architecture_changed":
            False,

        "V3_objective_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "C11_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

ROOT=/root/autodl-tmp

FEAS="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_START_DISTANCE_FEASIBILITY_V1.json"

SCENARIO="$ROOT/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/scenarios_v1/Scenario-fullmine-v4-cross-scene-c11-dual-aligned-v1.json"

MANIFEST="$ROOT/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/scenarios_v1/cross_scene_c11_scenario_manifest_v1.json"

ZIP="$ROOT/PAPER1_NEURAL_C11_C06_CURRENT_SEMANTIC_COLLECTOR_V1_UPLOAD.zip"

OUT="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_V2.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_FEAS=00f47197dc3493b71c0d4754b2c87117aa36bcde9ddaf8dcb9f1724dca94a503
EXPECTED_MANIFEST=7bc4a6e327557bb038cd5da32c8b4af76976f470f2e8369201248a0cec7cfe12
EXPECTED_SCENARIO=1127aeb78308b90a9d59127f9443fea4acd8b98e136cbf86d1201f7593978cc9
EXPECTED_ZIP=94ec9778a2899825125eaaed5c2ece50b8d0cd29390481c1e6f2ac568f052300

SOURCE_MEMBER="src/cross_scene_fleet_mcts_c11_seed0_paper1_neural_collector_v1.py"

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 60M/100M SCENARIO CONSTRUCTIBILITY AUDIT V2 ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$FEAS" "$EXPECTED_FEAS" START_DISTANCE_FEASIBILITY
check_sha "$SCENARIO" "$EXPECTED_SCENARIO" C11_SCENARIO
check_sha "$MANIFEST" "$EXPECTED_MANIFEST" C11_MANIFEST
check_sha "$ZIP" "$EXPECTED_ZIP" COLLECTOR_ZIP

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false
}

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

python - \
"$FEAS" \
"$SCENARIO" \
"$MANIFEST" \
"$ZIP" \
"$OUT" \
"$SOURCE_MEMBER" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import datetime
import re

(
    feas_p,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
    zip_p,
    out_p,
    source_member,
) = sys.argv[1:]

feas_p = Path(feas_p)
scenario_p = Path(scenario_p)
manifest_p = Path(manifest_p)
zip_p = Path(zip_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


feas = json.loads(
    feas_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

manifest = json.loads(
    manifest_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

scenario = json.loads(
    scenario_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    feas["status"]
    == "PASS_BASIC_ROUTE_S_FEASIBILITY"
)

print("PRIOR_FEASIBILITY_GATE=PASS")


with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


# ============================================================
# 1. Exact scenario structure
# ============================================================

traj = scenario[
    "TrajSegmentInfo"
]

assert isinstance(
    traj,
    list,
)

assert len(traj) == 1

segment = traj[0]

states = segment[
    "states"
]

assert isinstance(
    states,
    dict,
)

expected_state_keys = {
    "x",
    "y",
    "yaw_rad",
    "v_mps",
    "yawrate_radps",
    "acc_mpss",
}

assert set(
    states.keys()
) == expected_state_keys


lengths = {}

for key in sorted(
    expected_state_keys
):

    arr = states[key]

    assert isinstance(
        arr,
        list,
    )

    lengths[key] = len(
        arr
    )


print(
    "STATE_COLUMN_LENGTHS="
    + repr(
        lengths
    )
)

unique_lengths = set(
    lengths.values()
)

assert len(
    unique_lengths
) == 1

state_count = next(
    iter(
        unique_lengths
    )
)

assert state_count >= 2

print(
    "STATE_SAMPLE_COUNT="
    + str(
        state_count
    )
)

print(
    "COLUMNAR_STATE_SCHEMA_GATE=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 2. First two samples from every state field
# ============================================================

def scalar(v):
    if (
        isinstance(v, list)
        and len(v) == 1
    ):
        return v[0]

    return v


sample0 = {
    k: scalar(
        states[k][0]
    )
    for k in sorted(
        expected_state_keys
    )
}

sample1 = {
    k: scalar(
        states[k][1]
    )
    for k in sorted(
        expected_state_keys
    )
}


print(
    "STATE_SAMPLE0="
    + repr(
        sample0
    )
)

print(
    "STATE_SAMPLE1="
    + repr(
        sample1
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Confirm collector consumes columnar scenario states
# ============================================================

source_lines = source.splitlines()

patterns = {
    "scenario_doc":
        r'scenario_doc\s*=\s*json\.loads',

    "raw_states":
        r'raw_states\s*=\s*scenario_doc\["TrajSegmentInfo"\]\[0\]\["states"\]',

    "raw_n1_scalar":
        r'def\s+raw_n1_scalar',

    "raw_state_x":
        r'raw_n1_scalar\("x"\)',

    "raw_state_y":
        r'raw_n1_scalar\("y"\)',

    "scenario_time0":
        r'get_time_point\(0\)',

    "initial_ego_state":
        r'initial_ego_state',

    "b_initial":
        r'b_initial',

    "location_contract":
        r'LOCATION_POINT_MATCHES_CONTROLLED_REAR_AXLE',
}


hits = {}

for name, pattern in patterns.items():

    rows = []

    for lineno, line in enumerate(
        source_lines,
        1,
    ):

        if re.search(
            pattern,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        ):
            rows.append(
                {
                    "line":
                        lineno,
                    "text":
                        line.strip(),
                }
            )

    hits[name] = rows

    print(
        name.upper()
        + "_HIT_COUNT="
        + str(
            len(rows)
        )
    )

    for row in rows:
        print(
            name.upper()
            + "_LINE="
            + repr(row)
        )


# ============================================================
# 4. Establish explicit initial-state construction surface
# ============================================================

columnar_surface = (
    isinstance(
        states,
        dict,
    )
    and set(
        states.keys()
    )
    == expected_state_keys
)

collector_reads_columnar = (
    len(
        hits[
            "raw_states"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "raw_n1_scalar"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

collector_reads_xy = (
    len(
        hits[
            "raw_state_x"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "raw_state_y"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

collector_initial_contract = (
    len(
        hits[
            "scenario_time0"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "initial_ego_state"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "b_initial"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

location_contract = (
    len(
        hits[
            "location_contract"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)


static_constructibility = all(
    (
        columnar_surface,
        collector_reads_columnar,
        collector_reads_xy,
        collector_initial_contract,
        location_contract,
    )
)


print()
print(
    "COLUMNAR_STATE_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        columnar_surface
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_READS_COLUMNAR_STATES="
    + repr(
        collector_reads_columnar
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_READS_RAW_XY="
    + repr(
        collector_reads_xy
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_INITIAL_STATE_CONTRACT="
    + repr(
        collector_initial_contract
    )
)

print(
    "LOCATION_CONTROL_CONTRACT_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        location_contract
    )
)

print(
    "STATIC_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if static_constructibility
        else "HOLD"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 5. Candidate contract remains fixed
# ============================================================

candidates = (
    feas[
        "candidate_start_distance_probe"
    ][
        "candidates"
    ]
)

for c in candidates:

    print(
        "CANDIDATE="
        + repr(
            {
                "distance_m":
                    c[
                        "distance_to_conflict_m"
                    ],

                "A_start_route_s":
                    c[
                        "A_start_route_s"
                    ],

                "B_start_route_s":
                    c[
                        "B_start_route_s"
                    ],
            }
        )
    )


# ============================================================
# 6. Freeze corrected constructibility result
# ============================================================

if static_constructibility:

    classification = (
        "C11_60M_100M_INITIAL_STATE_VARIANTS_STATICALLY_CONSTRUCTIBLE"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "PREREGISTER_C11_60M_100M_COVERAGE_EXPANSION"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "C11_INITIAL_STATE_CONSTRUCTION_SURFACE_NOT_FULLY_RESOLVED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_C11_INITIAL_STATE_CONSTRUCTION_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_V2",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_STATIC_CONSTRUCTIBILITY"
            if static_constructibility
            else
            "HOLD_STATIC_CONSTRUCTIBILITY"
        ),

    "start_distance_feasibility_sha256":
        sha(
            feas_p
        ),

    "scenario_sha256":
        sha(
            scenario_p
        ),

    "manifest_sha256":
        sha(
            manifest_p
        ),

    "scenario_state_schema": {
        "representation":
            "COLUMNAR_DICT",

        "keys":
            sorted(
                expected_state_keys
            ),

        "sample_count":
            state_count,

        "column_lengths":
            lengths,

        "sample0":
            sample0,

        "sample1":
            sample1,
    },

    "collector_contract": {
        "reads_columnar_states":
            collector_reads_columnar,

        "reads_raw_xy":
            collector_reads_xy,

        "initial_state_contract":
            collector_initial_contract,

        "location_control_contract":
            location_contract,
    },

    "candidate_design": {
        "distances_m":
            [60.0, 100.0],

        "route_changed":
            False,

        "conflict_point_changed":
            False,

        "seed_changed":
            False,

        "MCTS_budget_changed":
            False,

        "MCTS_depth_changed":
            False,

        "target_speed_changed":
            False,
    },

    "important_boundary": {
        "static_constructibility_only":
            static_constructibility,

        "candidate_files_created":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "bitmap_drivability_proven":
            False,

        "runtime_initialization_proven":
            False,

        "causal_conflict_preservation_proven":
            False,

        "new_collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_architecture_changed":
            False,

        "V3_objective_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "C11_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "C11_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_V2_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "C11_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTIBILITY_V2_SHA256="
    + sha(
        out_p
    )
)

print(
    "CANDIDATE_FILES_CREATED=False"
)

print(
    "LOADER_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False"
)

print(
    "TRAINING_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

PY

思考了两秒
现在这一步已经 PASS，而且证据链完整：

C11 场景 states 是 6 列 × 900 samples 的 columnar dict；

collector 明确读取该 columnar states；

明确读取 x/y；

明确使用 time0 initial state；

location control contract 也存在；

因此 60m / 100m 两个初始距离变体，已经可以认定为 静态可构造。

 


两个候选已经固定为：

60m：A 167.77075274944568，B 206.54748904548518

100m：A 127.77075274944568，B 166.54748904548518 


当前资源：低资源。

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime

freeze_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
knobs_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
feas_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
construct_p = Path(sys.argv[4])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[5])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


freeze = json.loads(
    freeze_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

knobs = json.loads(
    knobs_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

feas = json.loads(
    feas_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

construct = json.loads(
    construct_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)


assert (
    freeze["status"]
    == "FROZEN_SINGLE_CURRENT_SEMANTIC_SEED0_EPISODE"
)

assert (
    knobs["status"]
    == "PASS_STATIC_VARIATION_KNOB_INVENTORY"
)

assert (
    feas["status"]
    == "PASS_BASIC_ROUTE_S_FEASIBILITY"
)

assert (
    construct["status"]
    == "PASS_STATIC_CONSTRUCTIBILITY"
)

assert (
    construct["classification"]
    ==
    "C11_60M_100M_INITIAL_STATE_VARIANTS_STATICALLY_CONSTRUCTIBLE"
)

print("PREREG_INPUT_CONTRACT=PASS")


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_COVERAGE_EXPANSION_PREREGISTRATION_V1",

    "status":
        "FROZEN_BEFORE_CANDIDATE_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTION",

            True,
    },

            False,

            False,

思考了 4s
当前资源：低资源。

预注册已经正式 PASS，SHA 锁定为：

7e027fa15a51e0b86340ff4cbeb15ef73ef5b10da964968747bb590f53400dfa
而且设计边界已经锁死：只改变 A/B 相对冲突点的对称初始距离 60m / 100m，路线、seed、MCTS 参数、target speed、V3 网络与 loss 全部不变。

不过现在还不能直接生成 candidate scenario。我们已经确认 TrajSegmentInfo[0].states 是 B 车的 columnar trajectory，但 A 车的初始状态来自 scenario.initial_ego_state，也就是 JSON 里的 ego_info。如果现在直接改文件，很容易只移动 B、不移动 A。

所以下一步只解决一个 blocker：

锁定 A 的 ego_info schema，以及 A/B 各自在 scenario JSON 中对应哪个初始状态字段。

不改文件、不跑 loader。

export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 A/B INITIAL STATE SURFACE AUDIT ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

check_sha "$PREREG" "$EXPECTED_PREREG" COVERAGE_PREREG
check_sha "$SCENARIO" "$EXPECTED_SCENARIO" C11_SCENARIO
check_sha "$ZIP" "$EXPECTED_ZIP" COLLECTOR_ZIP

    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import datetime
import re

prereg_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
scenario_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
zip_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[4])
source_member = sys.argv[5]


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


prereg = json.loads(
    prereg_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

scenario = json.loads(
    scenario_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    prereg["status"]
    == "FROZEN_BEFORE_CANDIDATE_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTION"
)

print("PREREG_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Raw ego_info schema
# ============================================================

ego = scenario.get(
    "ego_info"
)

print(
    "EGO_INFO_TYPE="
    + type(ego).__name__
)

if isinstance(
    ego,
    dict,
):
    print(
        "EGO_INFO_KEYS="
        + repr(
            sorted(
                ego.keys()
            )
        )
    )
else:
    print(
        "EGO_INFO_REPR="
        + repr(ego)
    )


def inspect_obj(
    prefix,
    obj,
    depth=0,
    max_depth=4,
):
    if depth > max_depth:
        return

    if isinstance(
        obj,
        dict,
    ):
        for k, v in obj.items():

            name = (
                prefix
                + "."
                + str(k)
            )

            print(
                "EGO_FIELD="
                + repr(
                    {
                        "path":
                            name,
                        "type":
                            type(v).__name__,
                        "value":
                            (
                                v
                                if not isinstance(
                                    v,
                                    (dict, list)
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                    }
                )
            )

            inspect_obj(
                name,
                v,
                depth + 1,
                max_depth,
            )

    elif isinstance(
        obj,
        list,
    ):

        print(
            "EGO_LIST="
            + repr(
                {
                    "path":
                        prefix,
                    "length":
                        len(obj),
                    "first_type":
                        (
                            type(
                                obj[0]
                            ).__name__
                            if obj
                            else None
                        ),
                }
            )
        )

        for i, v in enumerate(
            obj[:3]
        ):
            inspect_obj(
                prefix
                + f"[{i}]",
                v,
                depth + 1,
                max_depth,
            )


inspect_obj(
    "ego_info",
    ego,
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Exact B trajectory initial samples
# ============================================================

segment = scenario[
    "TrajSegmentInfo"
][0]

states = segment[
    "states"
]

required = {
    "x",
    "y",
    "yaw_rad",
    "v_mps",
    "yawrate_radps",
    "acc_mpss",
}

assert set(
    states.keys()
) == required


def scalar(v):
    if (
        isinstance(v, list)
        and len(v) == 1
    ):
        return v[0]

    return v


B0 = {
    k:
        scalar(
            states[k][0]
        )
    for k in sorted(
        required
    )
}

B1 = {
    k:
        scalar(
            states[k][1]
        )
    for k in sorted(
        required
    )
}


print()
print(
    "B_STATE0="
    + repr(B0)
)

print(
    "B_STATE1="
    + repr(B1)
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Read collector source and extract exact A/B loading lines
# ============================================================

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


lines = source.splitlines()

patterns = {
    "A_INITIAL":
        r"initial_ego_state",

    "B_INITIAL":
        r"b_initial",

    "AGENT_TO_EGO":
        r"agent_to_ego_state",

    "RAW_STATES":
        r"raw_states",

    "RAW_N1":
        r"raw_n1_scalar",

    "EXPECTED_START":
        r"EXPECTED_START_S",

    "INITIAL_S":
        r"initial_s",

    "ROUTE_S":
        r"route_s\(",

    "STATE_FACTORY":
        r"EgoState|StateSE2|DynamicCarState|CarFootprint",
}


hits = {}

for name, pattern in patterns.items():

    rows = []

    for i, line in enumerate(
        lines,
        1,
    ):

        if re.search(
            pattern,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        ):
            rows.append(
                {
                    "line":
                        i,
                    "text":
                        line.strip(),
                }
            )

    hits[name] = rows

    print()
    print(
        f"{name}_HIT_COUNT="
        + str(
            len(rows)
        )
    )

    for row in rows[:40]:
        print(
            f"{name}_LINE="
            + repr(row)
        )


# ============================================================
# 4. Print source context around actual initialization
# ============================================================

interesting_lines = set()

for name in (
    "A_INITIAL",
    "B_INITIAL",
    "RAW_STATES",
    "RAW_N1",
    "EXPECTED_START",
):
    for row in hits[name]:
        interesting_lines.add(
            row["line"]
        )


context_ranges = []

for center in sorted(
    interesting_lines
):

    lo = max(
        1,
        center - 6,
    )

    hi = min(
        len(lines),
        center + 8,
    )

    context_ranges.append(
        (
            lo,
            hi,
        )
    )


merged = []

for lo, hi in context_ranges:

    if (
        not merged
        or lo
        > merged[-1][1] + 1
    ):
        merged.append(
            [
                lo,
                hi,
            ]
        )

    else:
        merged[-1][1] = max(
            merged[-1][1],
            hi,
        )


print()
print(
    "INITIALIZATION_CONTEXT_RANGE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(merged)
    )
)

for lo, hi in merged:

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_BEGIN="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )

    for n in range(
        lo,
        hi + 1,
    ):
        print(
            f"SOURCE_{n}="
            + lines[
                n - 1
            ]
        )

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_END="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )


# ============================================================
# 5. Conservative surface classification
# ============================================================

ego_surface_present = (
    isinstance(
        ego,
        dict,
    )
)

B_columnar_surface_present = (
    isinstance(
        states,
        dict,
    )
    and set(
        states.keys()
    )
    == required
)

collector_A_initial_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "A_INITIAL"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

collector_B_initial_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "B_INITIAL"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

collector_raw_B_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "RAW_STATES"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "RAW_N1"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

surface_resolved = all(
    (
        ego_surface_present,
        B_columnar_surface_present,
        collector_A_initial_present,
        collector_B_initial_present,
        collector_raw_B_present,
    )
)


print()
print(
    "A_EGO_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        ego_surface_present
    )
)

print(
    "B_COLUMNAR_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        B_columnar_surface_present
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_A_INITIAL_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        collector_A_initial_present
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_B_INITIAL_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        collector_B_initial_present
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_RAW_B_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        collector_raw_B_present
    )
)

print(
    "AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_RESOLUTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if surface_resolved
        else "HOLD"
    )
)


if surface_resolved:

    classification = (
        "A_EGO_INFO_AND_B_TRAJECTORY_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACES_IDENTIFIED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "DEFINE_EXACT_60M_100M_SCENARIO_TRANSFORMATION"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_NOT_FULLY_RESOLVED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_MISSING_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_AUDIT_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_IDENTIFIED"
            if surface_resolved
            else
            "HOLD_AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE"
        ),

    "coverage_preregistration_sha256":
        sha(
            prereg_p
        ),

    "scenario_sha256":
        sha(
            scenario_p
        ),

    "A_surface": {
        "json_path_root":
            "ego_info",

        "present":
            ego_surface_present,
    },

    "B_surface": {
        "json_path":
            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states",

        "representation":
            "COLUMNAR_DICT",

        "present":
            B_columnar_surface_present,

        "state0":
            B0,

        "state1":
            B1,
    },

    "collector_contract": {
        "A_initial_state_reference_present":
            collector_A_initial_present,

        "B_initial_state_reference_present":
            collector_B_initial_present,

        "raw_B_columnar_reference_present":
            collector_raw_B_present,
    },

    "important_boundary": {
        "exact_transformation_defined":
            False,

        "candidate_files_created":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_AUDIT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_AUDIT_SHA256="
    + sha(
        out_p
    )
)

print(
    "CANDIDATE_FILES_CREATED=False"
)

print(
    "LOADER_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False"
)

print(
    "TRAINING_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

A/B 初始状态入口已经完全确认：

A：ego_info.start_states

B：TrajSegmentInfo[0].states

runtime 分别使用 scenario.initial_ego_state 和 b_initial

但现在不能直接改 x/y。尤其 100m 比现有 80m 更靠前，B 的现有 trajectory 未必包含可直接截取的更早状态。因此下一步先锁定“route_s → x/y/yaw”的权威构造链，确认能否从 frozen route geometry 精确生成 60m/100m 两车 pose。

(
set -eu

PREREG="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_COVERAGE_EXPANSION_PREREGISTRATION_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_PREREG=7e027fa15a51e0b86340ff4cbeb15ef73ef5b10da964968747bb590f53400dfa
EXPECTED_SURFACE=f4a4e499de285b3bd30629d8ec5f6c3703411a296e8435d7b147d3d5f18d4c53
EXPECTED_ZIP=94ec9778a2899825125eaaed5c2ece50b8d0cd29390481c1e6f2ac568f052300

SOURCE_MEMBER="src/cross_scene_fleet_mcts_c11_seed0_paper1_neural_collector_v1.py"

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 ROUTE_S -> POSE CONSTRUCTION AUDIT ==="

    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}
