from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import datetime
import re

prereg_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
surface_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
zip_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[4])
source_member = sys.argv[5]


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


prereg = json.loads(
    prereg_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

surface = json.loads(
    surface_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    prereg["status"]
    == "FROZEN_BEFORE_CANDIDATE_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTION"
)

assert (
    surface["status"]
    == "PASS_AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_IDENTIFIED"
)

print("PRIOR_CONTRACT=PASS")


with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


lines = source.splitlines()


# ============================================================
# 1. Locate authoritative route geometry construction
# ============================================================

patterns = {
    "MAKE_LINE":
        r"def\s+make_line",

    "ROUTES_ASSIGN":
        r"ROUTES\s*=",

    "PATHS_ASSIGN":
        r"PATHS\s*=",

    "ROUTE_ADAPTER":
        r"class\s+RouteAdapter",

    "ROUTE_S":
        r"def\s+route_s",

    "INTERPOLATE":
        r"\.interpolate\(",

    "PROJECT":
        r"\.project\(",

    "HEADING":
        r"heading|yaw|atan2",

    "POINTS":
        r"points\s*=",

    "BASELINE":
        r"baseline_path|baseline",

    "CENTERLINE":
        r"centerline",

    "MAP_API":
        r"map_api|load_map|path_obj|path-",

    "STATE_SE2":
        r"StateSE2",

    "PROGRESS_STATE":
        r"ProgressStateSE2",
}


hits = {}

for name, pattern in patterns.items():

    rows = []

    for i, line in enumerate(
        lines,
        1,
    ):

        if re.search(
            pattern,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        ):
            rows.append(
                {
                    "line": i,
                    "text": line.strip(),
                }
            )

    hits[name] = rows

    print()
    print(
        f"{name}_HIT_COUNT="
        + str(len(rows))
    )

    for row in rows[:80]:
        print(
            f"{name}_LINE="
            + repr(row)
        )


# ============================================================
# 2. Print complete source contexts around geometry functions
# ============================================================

centers = set()

for name in (
    "MAKE_LINE",
    "ROUTES_ASSIGN",
    "PATHS_ASSIGN",
    "ROUTE_ADAPTER",
    "ROUTE_S",
    "INTERPOLATE",
    "PROJECT",
):
    for row in hits[name]:
        centers.add(
            row["line"]
        )


ranges = []

for center in sorted(centers):

    lo = max(
        1,
        center - 12,
    )

    hi = min(
        len(lines),
        center + 25,
    )

    ranges.append(
        [lo, hi]
    )


merged = []

for lo, hi in ranges:

    if (
        not merged
        or lo > merged[-1][1] + 1
    ):
        merged.append(
            [lo, hi]
        )
    else:
        merged[-1][1] = max(
            merged[-1][1],
            hi,
        )


print()
print(
    "GEOMETRY_CONTEXT_RANGE_COUNT="
    + str(len(merged))
)

for lo, hi in merged:

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_BEGIN="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )

    for n in range(
        lo,
        hi + 1,
    ):
        print(
            f"SOURCE_{n}="
            + lines[n - 1]
        )

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_END="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )


# ============================================================
# 3. Determine whether exact route_s -> pose machinery exists
# ============================================================

make_line_present = (
    len(hits["MAKE_LINE"]) >= 1
)

routes_present = (
    len(hits["ROUTES_ASSIGN"]) >= 1
)

route_adapter_present = (
    len(hits["ROUTE_ADAPTER"]) >= 1
)

route_s_present = (
    len(hits["ROUTE_S"]) >= 1
)

interpolation_present = (
    len(hits["INTERPOLATE"]) >= 1
)

projection_present = (
    len(hits["PROJECT"]) >= 1
)

heading_surface_present = (
    len(hits["HEADING"]) >= 1
)


geometry_chain_resolved = all(
    (
        make_line_present,
        routes_present,
        route_adapter_present,
        route_s_present,
        projection_present,
    )
)


print()
print(
    "MAKE_LINE_PRESENT="
    + repr(make_line_present)
)

print(
    "ROUTES_PRESENT="
    + repr(routes_present)
)

print(
    "ROUTE_ADAPTER_PRESENT="
    + repr(route_adapter_present)
)

print(
    "ROUTE_S_PRESENT="
    + repr(route_s_present)
)

print(
    "INTERPOLATION_PRESENT="
    + repr(interpolation_present)
)

print(
    "PROJECTION_PRESENT="
    + repr(projection_present)
)

print(
    "HEADING_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(heading_surface_present)
)

print(
    "ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CHAIN_RESOLUTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if geometry_chain_resolved
        else "HOLD"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 4. Freeze only the construction-chain audit
# ============================================================

if geometry_chain_resolved:

    classification = (
        "AUTHORITATIVE_C11_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CHAIN_IDENTIFIED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "COMPUTE_PREREGISTERED_60M_100M_TARGET_POSES_READ_ONLY"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "C11_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CHAIN_NOT_FULLY_RESOLVED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_SOURCE_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_ROUTE_S_TO_POSE_CONSTRUCTION_AUDIT_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CHAIN"
            if geometry_chain_resolved
            else
            "HOLD_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CHAIN"
        ),

    "coverage_preregistration_sha256":
        sha(prereg_p),

    "AB_initial_state_surface_sha256":
        sha(surface_p),

    "geometry_contract": {
        "make_line_present":
            make_line_present,

        "routes_present":
            routes_present,

        "route_adapter_present":
            route_adapter_present,

        "route_s_present":
            route_s_present,

        "interpolation_present":
            interpolation_present,

        "projection_present":
            projection_present,

        "heading_surface_present":
            heading_surface_present,
    },

    "candidate_contract": {
        "distances_m":
            [60.0, 100.0],

        "A_target_route_s": [
            167.77075274944568,
            127.77075274944568,
        ],

        "B_target_route_s": [
            206.54748904548518,
            166.54748904548518,
        ],

        "route_changed":
            False,

        "seed_changed":
            False,

        "planner_changed":
            False,
    },

    "important_boundary": {
        "target_pose_computed":
            False,

        "candidate_files_created":
            False,

        "scenario_modified":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "ROUTE_GEOMETRY_AUDIT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "ROUTE_GEOMETRY_AUDIT_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)

print("TARGET_POSE_COMPUTED=False")
print("CANDIDATE_FILES_CREATED=False")
print("SCENARIO_MODIFIED=False")
print("LOADER_EXECUTED=False")
print("NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False")
print("TRAINING_EXECUTED=False")
print("MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False")

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

粘贴的文本 (1)(20260824-070807).txt
文档

思考了 14s
当前资源：低资源即可。

route_s → pose 的几何链已经 PASS：LineString.interpolate(s) 给位置，前后插值得到 yaw；route_s 则用同一条 ROUTES[token].project(...) 反算。


现在只差一个很小的 provenance blocker：make_line() 的 waypoint 来自 records[token]["waypoints"]，但上一轮输出没有把 records 从哪里加载 打出来。


先把这个锁死，再计算 60/80/100m pose，避免复制一套并非权威的地图加载逻辑。

(

GEOM="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_ROUTE_S_TO_POSE_CONSTRUCTION_AUDIT_V1.json"
ZIP="$ROOT/PAPER1_NEURAL_C11_C06_CURRENT_SEMANTIC_COLLECTOR_V1_UPLOAD.zip"

OUT="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_ROUTE_RECORDS_SOURCE_AUDIT_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_GEOM=506ae423bbf2663e1d7ac9be3d5036e1292fc45ca5100a185fb48169aa164692
EXPECTED_ZIP=94ec9778a2899825125eaaed5c2ece50b8d0cd29390481c1e6f2ac568f052300

SOURCE_MEMBER="src/cross_scene_fleet_mcts_c11_seed0_paper1_neural_collector_v1.py"

    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 ROUTE RECORDS SOURCE AUDIT ==="

    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import datetime
import ast
import re

geom_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
zip_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
source_member = sys.argv[4]


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


geom = json.loads(
    geom_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    geom["status"]
    == "PASS_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_CHAIN"
)

print("PRIOR_GEOMETRY_GATE=PASS")


with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


lines = source.splitlines()
tree = ast.parse(source)


# ============================================================
# 1. Locate assignments involving `records`
# ============================================================

record_nodes = []

for node in ast.walk(tree):

    names = set()

    for sub in ast.walk(node):

        if isinstance(
            sub,
            ast.Name,
        ):
            names.add(
                sub.id
            )

    if "records" not in names:
        continue

    if isinstance(
        node,
        (
            ast.Assign,
            ast.AnnAssign,
            ast.AugAssign,
            ast.For,
            ast.With,
            ast.Expr,
        ),
    ):

        lineno = getattr(
            node,
            "lineno",
            None,
        )

        end_lineno = getattr(
            node,
            "end_lineno",
            lineno,
        )

        if lineno is None:
            continue

        record_nodes.append(
            (
                lineno,
                end_lineno,
                type(node).__name__,
            )
        )


record_nodes = sorted(
    set(record_nodes)
)

print(
    "RECORDS_AST_REFERENCE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(record_nodes)
    )
)

for item in record_nodes:
    print(
        "RECORDS_AST_REFERENCE="
        + repr(item)
    )


# ============================================================
# 2. Text hits around all likely map/record loaders
# ============================================================

patterns = {
    "RECORDS":
        r"\brecords\b",

    "JSON_LOAD":
        r"json\.load|json\.loads",

    "READ_TEXT":
        r"read_text",

    "OPEN":
        r"\bopen\(",

    "WAYPOINT":
        r"waypoints",

    "MAP":
        r"map|bitmap",

    "PATH_FILE":
        r"\.json|Path\(",

    "STATIC_CONFIG":
        r"STATIC_CONFIG",

    "FULLMINE":
        r"fullmine|FullMine",
}


hits = {}

for name, pattern in patterns.items():

    rows = []

    for i, line in enumerate(
        lines,
        1,
    ):

        if re.search(
            pattern,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        ):
            rows.append(
                {
                    "line": i,
                    "text": line.strip(),
                }
            )

    hits[name] = rows

    print()
    print(
        f"{name}_HIT_COUNT="
        + str(
            len(rows)
        )
    )

    for row in rows[:120]:
        print(
            f"{name}_LINE="
            + repr(row)
        )


# ============================================================
# 3. Print full source context around all records references
# ============================================================

centers = set()

for lo, hi, _ in record_nodes:
    centers.add(lo)
    centers.add(hi)

for row in hits["RECORDS"]:
    centers.add(
        row["line"]
    )


ranges = []

for center in sorted(
    centers
):

    ranges.append(
        [
            max(
                1,
                center - 15,
            ),
            min(
                len(lines),
                center + 20,
            ),
        ]
    )


merged = []

for lo, hi in ranges:

    if (
        not merged
        or lo > merged[-1][1] + 1
    ):
        merged.append(
            [lo, hi]
        )

    else:
        merged[-1][1] = max(
            merged[-1][1],
            hi,
        )


print()
print(
    "RECORDS_CONTEXT_RANGE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(merged)
    )
)

for lo, hi in merged:

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_BEGIN="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )

    for n in range(
        lo,
        hi + 1,
    ):
        print(
            f"SOURCE_{n}="
            + lines[n - 1]
        )

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_END="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Conservative resolution
# ============================================================

records_reference_present = (
    len(
        hits["RECORDS"]
    )
    >= 1
)

waypoint_reference_present = (
    len(
        hits["WAYPOINT"]
    )
    >= 1
)

loader_surface_present = (
    len(
        hits["JSON_LOAD"]
    )
    >= 1
    or len(
        hits["READ_TEXT"]
    )
    >= 1
    or len(
        hits["OPEN"]
    )
    >= 1
)


resolved = all(
    (
        records_reference_present,
        waypoint_reference_present,
        loader_surface_present,
    )
)


print()
print(
    "RECORDS_REFERENCE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        records_reference_present
    )
)

print(
    "WAYPOINT_REFERENCE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        waypoint_reference_present
    )
)

print(
    "RECORD_LOADER_SURFACE_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        loader_surface_present
    )
)

print(
    "ROUTE_RECORDS_SOURCE_RESOLUTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if resolved
        else "HOLD"
    )
)

    classification = (
        "C11_ROUTE_WAYPOINT_RECORD_SOURCE_IDENTIFIED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "COMPUTE_PREREGISTERED_60M_80M_100M_TARGET_POSES_READ_ONLY"
    )

    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_ROUTE_RECORD_LOADER_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_ROUTE_RECORDS_SOURCE_AUDIT_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_ROUTE_RECORD_SOURCE"
            if resolved
            else
            "HOLD_ROUTE_RECORD_SOURCE"
        ),

    "route_geometry_audit_sha256":
        sha(
            geom_p
        ),

    "records_ast_references":
        record_nodes,

    "records_hits":
        hits["RECORDS"],

    "json_loader_hits":
        hits["JSON_LOAD"],

    "read_text_hits":
        hits["READ_TEXT"],

    "open_hits":
        hits["OPEN"],

    "waypoint_hits":
        hits["WAYPOINT"],

    "important_boundary": {
        "pose_computed":
            False,

        "candidate_files_created":
            False,

        "scenario_modified":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "ROUTE_RECORDS_SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "ROUTE_RECORDS_SOURCE_AUDIT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "ROUTE_RECORDS_SOURCE_AUDIT_SHA256="
    + sha(
        out_p
    )
)

print("POSE_COMPUTED=False")
print("CANDIDATE_FILES_CREATED=False")
print("LOADER_EXECUTED=False")
print("NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False")

    "NEXT="
    + next_stage

这一步已经 PASS：records 的权威来源就是 frozen semantic map：

/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_vector_v2_targeted_runtime/maps/semantic_map/geojson_full_mine_vector_v2_dev_semantic_map.json
collector 读取 semantic["reference_path"] 建 records[token]，再从 records[token]["waypoints"] 拼成 A/B 两条 LineString。


所以现在可以直接做你刚才说的下一步：只读计算 60m / 80m / 100m 对应的 A/B 精确 x、y、yaw，并且先用 80m 反算当前 scenario，作为一致性 PASS gate。

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_RECORDS_AUDIT=3f42821ce00f4c0c1174c78dd86e665a0d2afbee41f7723646b032ec660e736f

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 60M/80M/100M TARGET POSES READ-ONLY ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$RECORDS_AUDIT" "$EXPECTED_RECORDS_AUDIT" RECORDS_AUDIT
check_sha "$SURFACE" "$EXPECTED_SURFACE" AB_SURFACE
check_sha "$PREREG" "$EXPECTED_PREREG" COVERAGE_PREREG
check_sha "$SCENARIO" "$EXPECTED_SCENARIO" C11_SCENARIO
check_sha "$MANIFEST" "$EXPECTED_MANIFEST" C11_MANIFEST

[ -f "$SEMANTIC" ] || {
    echo "FAIL SEMANTIC_MAP_MISSING=$SEMANTIC"
    false
}

    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"

"$OUT" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import math

from shapely.geometry import LineString, Point

(
    records_audit_p,
    surface_p,
    prereg_p,
    semantic_p,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


records_audit = json.loads(
    records_audit_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

surface = json.loads(
    surface_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

prereg = json.loads(
    prereg_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

semantic = json.loads(
    semantic_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

scenario = json.loads(
    scenario_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

manifest = json.loads(
    manifest_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)


assert (
    records_audit["status"]
    == "PASS_ROUTE_RECORD_SOURCE"
)

assert (
    surface["status"]
    == "PASS_AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_IDENTIFIED"
)

assert (
    prereg["status"]
    == "FROZEN_BEFORE_CANDIDATE_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTION"
)

print("PRIOR_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Exact semantic-map route construction
# ============================================================

records = {
    r["token"]: r
    for r in semantic[
        "reference_path"
    ]
}

route_tokens = {
    "A":
        tuple(
            manifest[
                "route_a"
            ]
        ),

    "B":
        tuple(
            manifest[
                "route_b"
            ]
        ),
}


def make_line(tokens):

    points = []

    for token in tokens:

        assert token in records

        segment = [
            (
                float(p[0]),
                float(p[1]),
            )
            for p in records[
                token
            ][
                "waypoints"
            ]
        ]

        assert len(segment) >= 2

        if (
            points
            and math.dist(
                points[-1],
                segment[0],
            )
            < 1e-7
        ):
            segment = segment[1:]

        points.extend(
            segment
        )

    return LineString(
        points
    )


ROUTES = {
    token:
        make_line(tokens)
    for token, tokens
    in route_tokens.items()
}


print(
    "ROUTE_A_LENGTH="
    + repr(
        float(
            ROUTES["A"].length
        )
    )
)

print(
    "ROUTE_B_LENGTH="
    + repr(
        float(
            ROUTES["B"].length
        )
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Exact frozen pose rule
#
# same as RouteAdapter.get_state_at_progress:
# eps=min(0.5,max(0.05,line.length/1000))
# ============================================================

def pose_at_s(
    line,
    s,
):

    s = max(
        0.0,
        min(
            float(
                line.length
            ),
            float(s),
        ),
    )

    p = line.interpolate(
        s
    )

    eps = min(
        0.5,
        max(
            0.05,
            float(
                line.length
            )
            / 1000.0,
        ),
    )

    p0 = line.interpolate(
        max(
            0.0,
            s - eps,
        )
    )

    p1 = line.interpolate(
        min(
            float(
                line.length
            ),
            s + eps,
        )
    )

    yaw = math.atan2(
        p1.y - p0.y,
        p1.x - p0.x,
    )

    return {
        "route_s":
            float(s),

        "x":
            float(p.x),

        "y":
            float(p.y),

        "yaw_rad":
            float(yaw),

        "eps":
            float(eps),
    }


# ============================================================
# 3. Target route_s for 60 / 80 / 100m
# ============================================================

conflict = {
    "A":
        float(
            manifest[
                "conflict_route_s"
            ]["A"]
        ),

    "B":
        float(
            manifest[
                "conflict_route_s"
            ]["B"]
        ),
}


levels = [
    60.0,
    80.0,
    100.0,
]

poses = {}


for d in levels:

    key = (
        f"{int(d)}m"
    )

    poses[
        key
    ] = {}

    for token in (
        "A",
        "B",
    ):

        target_s = (
            conflict[token]
            - d
        )

        assert target_s >= 0.0

        assert (
            target_s
            <= float(
                ROUTES[token].length
            )
        )

        pose = pose_at_s(
            ROUTES[token],
            target_s,
        )

        poses[
            key
        ][
            token
        ] = pose

        print(
            f"POSE_{key}_{token}="
            + repr(
                pose
            )
        )


# ============================================================
# 4. 80m consistency gate against frozen scenario
# ============================================================

ego_start = scenario[
    "ego_info"
][
    "start_states"
]

A_existing = {
    "x":
        float(
            ego_start["x"]
        ),

    "y":
        float(
            ego_start["y"]
        ),

    "yaw_rad":
        float(
            ego_start[
                "yaw_rad"
            ]
        ),
}


states = scenario[
    "TrajSegmentInfo"
][0][
    "states"
]


def scalar0(
    field,
):

    value = states[
        field
    ][0]

    if (
        isinstance(
            value,
            list,
        )
        and len(value) == 1
    ):
        return float(
            value[0]
        )

    return float(
        value
    )


B_existing = {
    "x":
        scalar0("x"),

    "y":
        scalar0("y"),

    "yaw_rad":
        scalar0(
            "yaw_rad"
        ),
}


def angle_diff(
    a,
    b,
):
    return abs(
        math.atan2(
            math.sin(
                a - b
            ),
            math.cos(
                a - b
            ),
        )
    )


def pose_errors(
    computed,
    existing,
):

    xy = math.hypot(
        computed["x"]
        - existing["x"],
        computed["y"]
        - existing["y"],
    )

    yaw = angle_diff(
        computed[
            "yaw_rad"
        ],
        existing[
            "yaw_rad"
        ],
    )

    return {
        "xy_error_m":
            float(xy),

        "yaw_error_rad":
            float(yaw),
    }


A_err = pose_errors(
    poses["80m"]["A"],
    A_existing,
)

B_err = pose_errors(
    poses["80m"]["B"],
    B_existing,
)


print()
print(
    "A_EXISTING_80M_POSE="
    + repr(
        A_existing
    )
)

print(
    "A_RECONSTRUCTED_80M_POSE="
    + repr(
        poses[
            "80m"
        ][
            "A"
        ]
    )
)

print(
    "A_80M_XY_ERROR_M="
    + repr(
        A_err[
            "xy_error_m"
        ]
    )
)

print(
    "A_80M_YAW_ERROR_RAD="
    + repr(
        A_err[
            "yaw_error_rad"
        ]
    )
)


print()
print(
    "B_EXISTING_80M_POSE="
    + repr(
        B_existing
    )
)

print(
    "B_RECONSTRUCTED_80M_POSE="
    + repr(
        poses[
            "80m"
        ][
            "B"
        ]
    )
)

print(
    "B_80M_XY_ERROR_M="
    + repr(
        B_err[
            "xy_error_m"
        ]
    )
)

print(
    "B_80M_YAW_ERROR_RAD="
    + repr(
        B_err[
            "yaw_error_rad"
        ]
    )
)


# Position should match exact frozen route geometry.
# Heading may differ slightly due scenario-export convention;
# keep a conservative static tolerance.
XY_TOL = 1e-3
YAW_TOL = 5e-2

A_pass = (
    A_err[
        "xy_error_m"
    ]
    <= XY_TOL
    and A_err[
        "yaw_error_rad"
    ]
    <= YAW_TOL
)

B_pass = (
    B_err[
        "xy_error_m"
    ]
    <= XY_TOL
    and B_err[
        "yaw_error_rad"
    ]
    <= YAW_TOL
)


print()
print(
    "A_80M_RECONSTRUCTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if A_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)

print(
    "B_80M_RECONSTRUCTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if B_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


all_pass = (
    A_pass
    and B_pass
)


print(
    "80M_ROUTE_GEOMETRY_RECONSTRUCTION_GATE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if all_pass
        else "HOLD"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 5. Verify route_s by projecting every computed pose back
# ============================================================

projection_checks = {}

for level in levels:

    key = (
        f"{int(level)}m"
    )

    projection_checks[
        key
    ] = {}

    for token in (
        "A",
        "B",
    ):

        p = poses[
            key
        ][
            token
        ]

        projected = float(
            ROUTES[
                token
            ].project(
                Point(
                    p["x"],
                    p["y"],
                )
            )
        )

        error = abs(
            projected
            - p["route_s"]
        )

        projection_checks[
            key
        ][
            token
        ] = {
            "projected_route_s":
                projected,

            "route_s_error":
                error,
        }

        print(
            f"{key.upper()}_{token}_PROJECTED_ROUTE_S="
            + repr(
                projected
            )
        )

        print(
            f"{key.upper()}_{token}_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
            + repr(
                error
            )
        )


projection_pass = all(
    projection_checks[
        f"{int(level)}m"
    ][token][
        "route_s_error"
    ]
    <= 1e-6

    for level in levels
    for token in (
        "A",
        "B",
    )
)


print(
    "ALL_TARGET_POSE_ROUTE_S_PROJECTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if projection_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


# ============================================================
# 6. Classification
# ============================================================

final_pass = (
    all_pass
    and projection_pass
)


if final_pass:

    classification = (
        "C11_60M_80M_100M_TARGET_POSES_RESOLVED_WITH_80M_RECONSTRUCTION_PASS"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "DEFINE_AND_FREEZE_EXACT_60M_100M_SCENARIO_TRANSFORMATION"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "C11_TARGET_POSE_RECONSTRUCTION_NOT_YET_PROVEN"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "DIAGNOSE_80M_POSE_RECONSTRUCTION_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_80M_100M_TARGET_POSES_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_TARGET_POSE_RESOLUTION"
            if final_pass
            else
            "HOLD_TARGET_POSE_RESOLUTION"
        ),

    "route_records_audit_sha256":
        sha(
            records_audit_p
        ),

    "AB_surface_audit_sha256":
        sha(
            surface_p
        ),

    "coverage_preregistration_sha256":
        sha(
            prereg_p
        ),

    "semantic_map_sha256":
        sha(
            semantic_p
        ),

    "scenario_sha256":
        sha(
            scenario_p
        ),

    "manifest_sha256":
        sha(
            manifest_p
        ),

    "routes": {
        "A_tokens":
            list(
                route_tokens["A"]
            ),

        "B_tokens":
            list(
                route_tokens["B"]
            ),

        "A_length":
            float(
                ROUTES["A"].length
            ),

        "B_length":
            float(
                ROUTES["B"].length
            ),
    },

    "conflict_route_s":
        conflict,

    "target_poses":
        poses,

    "80m_reconstruction": {
        "A_existing":
            A_existing,

        "A_error":
            A_err,

        "A_pass":
            A_pass,

        "B_existing":
            B_existing,

        "B_error":
            B_err,

        "B_pass":
            B_pass,

        "gate":
            all_pass,

        "xy_tolerance_m":
            XY_TOL,

        "yaw_tolerance_rad":
            YAW_TOL,
    },

    "route_s_projection_checks":
        projection_checks,

    "route_s_projection_gate":
        projection_pass,

    "important_boundary": {
        "read_only_pose_computation":
            True,

        "scenario_modified":
            False,

        "candidate_files_created":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)

    "TARGET_POSE_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

    "TARGET_POSE_ARTIFACT_WRITE=PASS"
)

    "TARGET_POSE_ARTIFACT_SHA256="
    + sha(
        out_p
    )
)

    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

粘贴的文本 (1)(20260824-071207).txt
文档

思考了 11s
当前资源：低资源即可。

这一步已经完全 PASS：

60/80/100m 的 A/B 精确 pose 已求出；

80m 反算位置误差 A/B 都是 0；

yaw 误差只有 0.00202 rad 和 0.000743 rad，在冻结容差内；

6 个目标 pose 反投影到 route_s 的误差都在约 10^-13 m；

因此这套 route_s → x/y/yaw 构造链可以正式使用。

 


但现在还差一个非常关键的小问题，先别生成 scenario：

A 很明确，只需要改 ego_info.start_states。
B 的初始状态来自 TrajSegmentInfo[0].states[...][0]，但这个对象有完整 900 帧轨迹。我们必须确认 collector 在接管 B 之后，第 1 帧以后的原始 B trajectory 是否还会参与 runtime/science calculation。

如果只改 B 第 0 帧，而后面 899 帧仍是旧 80m 轨迹，可能制造 20m 的瞬时跳变。即便运行时后来不使用，也必须先证明。

所以下一步只做 B trajectory post-initial-use audit。这是生成候选 scenario 前最后一个 transformation blocker。

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true

export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic

(
set -eu

ROOT=/root/autodl-tmp
V3="$ROOT/paper1_value_v3_development_only_v1"

POSES="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_80M_100M_TARGET_POSES_V1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_POSES=ab9eb1606675feb89f83cb4f49ab8fd15f7e006686209ec058e641b8af99e9ea
EXPECTED_SURFACE=f4a4e499de285b3bd30629d8ec5f6c3703411a296e8435d7b147d3d5f18d4c53
EXPECTED_ZIP=94ec9778a2899825125eaaed5c2ece50b8d0cd29390481c1e6f2ac568f052300

SOURCE_MEMBER="src/cross_scene_fleet_mcts_c11_seed0_paper1_neural_collector_v1.py"

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 B POST-INITIAL TRAJECTORY USE AUDIT ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import datetime
import re
import ast

poses_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
surface_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
zip_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[4])
source_member = sys.argv[5]


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


poses = json.loads(
    poses_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

surface = json.loads(
    surface_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    poses["status"]
    == "PASS_TARGET_POSE_RESOLUTION"
)

assert (
    surface["status"]
    == "PASS_AB_INITIAL_STATE_SURFACE_IDENTIFIED"
)

print("PRIOR_TRANSFORMATION_INPUT_GATE=PASS")


with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


lines = source.splitlines()
tree = ast.parse(source)


# ============================================================
# 1. Audit every scenario / tracked-object access
# ============================================================

patterns = {
    "SCENARIO_GET_TIME_POINT":
        r"scenario\.get_time_point",

    "SCENARIO_TRACKED_OBJECTS":
        r"tracked_objects|extract_tracked",

    "B_AGENT":
        r"\bb_agent\b",

    "B_INITIAL":
        r"\bb_initial\b",

    "RAW_STATES":
        r"\braw_states\b",

    "RAW_N1":
        r"raw_n1_scalar",

    "PERFECT_TRACKING":
        r"PerfectTrackingController",

    "B_CONTROLLER":
        r"\bb_controller\b",

    "MULTI_EGO_RUNTIME":
        r"MultiEgoRuntime",

    "STATE_FOR_B":
        r'state_for\(["\']B["\']\)',

    "RUNTIME_STATE_FOR":
        r"runtime\.state_for",

    "SIMULATION":
        r"\bsimulation\b",

    "OBJECT_1":
        r"object-1",
}


hits = {}

for name, pattern in patterns.items():

    rows = []

    for i, line in enumerate(
        lines,
        1,
    ):

        if re.search(
            pattern,
            line,
            flags=re.I,
        ):
            rows.append(
                {
                    "line": i,
                    "text": line.strip(),
                }
            )

    hits[name] = rows

    print()
    print(
        f"{name}_HIT_COUNT="
        + str(
            len(rows)
        )
    )

    for row in rows[:100]:

        print(
            f"{name}_LINE="
            + repr(row)
        )


# ============================================================
# 2. Specifically check any raw_states access other than [0]
# ============================================================

raw_state_accesses = []

for node in ast.walk(tree):

    try:
        text = ast.get_source_segment(
            source,
            node,
        )
    except Exception:
        text = None

    if not text:
        continue

    if "raw_states" not in text:
        continue

    if len(text) > 500:
        continue

    raw_state_accesses.append(
        {
            "node_type":
                type(node).__name__,

            "line":
                getattr(
                    node,
                    "lineno",
                    None,
                ),

            "text":
                text,
        }
    )


unique = []

seen = set()

for row in raw_state_accesses:

    key = (
        row["node_type"],
        row["line"],
        row["text"],
    )

    if key in seen:
        continue

    seen.add(key)

    unique.append(row)


unique.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x["line"]
        if x["line"]
        is not None
        else -1,
        x["node_type"],
    )
)


print()
print(
    "RAW_STATE_AST_ACCESS_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(unique)
    )
)

for row in unique:

    print(
        "RAW_STATE_AST_ACCESS="
        + repr(row)
    )


# ============================================================
# 3. Print source contexts around B initialization/controller
# ============================================================

centers = set()

for name in (
    "SCENARIO_GET_TIME_POINT",
    "SCENARIO_TRACKED_OBJECTS",
    "B_AGENT",
    "B_INITIAL",
    "RAW_STATES",
    "PERFECT_TRACKING",
    "B_CONTROLLER",
    "MULTI_EGO_RUNTIME",
):

    for row in hits[name]:
        centers.add(
            row["line"]
        )


ranges = []

for center in sorted(
    centers
):

    ranges.append(
        [
            max(
                1,
                center - 12,
            ),
            min(
                len(lines),
                center + 18,
            ),
        ]
    )


merged = []

for lo, hi in ranges:

    if (
        not merged
        or lo > merged[-1][1] + 1
    ):
        merged.append(
            [lo, hi]
        )

    else:
        merged[-1][1] = max(
            merged[-1][1],
            hi,
        )


print()
print(
    "B_USE_CONTEXT_RANGE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(merged)
    )
)

for lo, hi in merged:

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_BEGIN="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )

    for n in range(
        lo,
        hi + 1,
    ):
        print(
            f"SOURCE_{n}="
            + lines[
                n - 1
            ]
        )

    print(
        "SOURCE_CONTEXT_END="
        + f"{lo}:{hi}"
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Conservative decision
#
# PASS only if:
# - raw scenario B is explicitly used for initialization;
# - runtime afterwards has a controlled B state;
# - no evidence in collector source of reading raw_states[n>0].
#
# This does NOT yet authorize modifying only sample0.
# It only establishes collector-side usage.
# ============================================================

raw_initial_read = (
    len(
        hits[
            "RAW_STATES"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "RAW_N1"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

b_initial_constructed = (
    len(
        hits[
            "B_INITIAL"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

b_controller_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "B_CONTROLLER"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
    and len(
        hits[
            "PERFECT_TRACKING"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

multi_ego_runtime_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "MULTI_EGO_RUNTIME"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)

runtime_state_access_present = (
    len(
        hits[
            "RUNTIME_STATE_FOR"
        ]
    )
    >= 1
)


# Explicit source text already known:
# raw_n1_scalar -> raw_states[field][0].
# Reject PASS if any obvious raw_states[field][index != 0]
# occurrence exists.

nonzero_raw_index_lines = []

for i, line in enumerate(
    lines,
    1,
):

    if "raw_states" not in line:
        continue

    # allow declaration and field-[0] read;
    # flag anything else for manual review.
    if (
        "raw_states =" in line
        or "[0]" in line
    ):
        continue

    # raw_states[field] itself is part of
    # `value = raw_states[field][0]` and can be on one line.
    if "raw_states[field][0]" in line:
        continue

    nonzero_raw_index_lines.append(
        {
            "line": i,
            "text": line.strip(),
        }
    )


print()
print(
    "NONZERO_OR_UNRESOLVED_RAW_STATE_ACCESS_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            nonzero_raw_index_lines
        )
    )
)

for row in nonzero_raw_index_lines:
    print(
        "NONZERO_OR_UNRESOLVED_RAW_STATE_ACCESS="
        + repr(row)
    )


collector_postinitial_resolution = all(
    (
        raw_initial_read,
        b_initial_constructed,
        b_controller_present,
        multi_ego_runtime_present,
        runtime_state_access_present,
        len(
            nonzero_raw_index_lines
        )
        == 0,
    )
)


print()
print(
    "RAW_B_INITIAL_READ_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        raw_initial_read
    )
)

print(
    "B_INITIAL_CONSTRUCTED="
    + repr(
        b_initial_constructed
    )
)

print(
    "B_CONTROLLER_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        b_controller_present
    )
)

print(
    "MULTI_EGO_RUNTIME_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        multi_ego_runtime_present
    )
)

print(
    "RUNTIME_STATE_ACCESS_PRESENT="
    + repr(
        runtime_state_access_present
    )
)

print(
    "COLLECTOR_B_POSTINITIAL_USE_RESOLUTION="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if collector_postinitial_resolution
        else "HOLD"
    )
)


if collector_postinitial_resolution:

    classification = (
        "COLLECTOR_USES_RAW_B_TRAJECTORY_FOR_INITIALIZATION_ONLY_THEN_CONTROLLED_RUNTIME_STATE"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "FREEZE_EXACT_60M_100M_SCENARIO_TRANSFORMATION"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "RAW_B_POSTINITIAL_TRAJECTORY_USAGE_NOT_FULLY_RESOLVED"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_RAW_B_TRAJECTORY_RUNTIME_USAGE_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_B_POST_INITIAL_TRAJECTORY_USE_AUDIT_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_COLLECTOR_B_POSTINITIAL_USE"
            if collector_postinitial_resolution
            else
            "HOLD_COLLECTOR_B_POSTINITIAL_USE"
        ),

    "target_pose_artifact_sha256":
        sha(
            poses_p
        ),

    "AB_surface_audit_sha256":
        sha(
            surface_p
        ),

    "collector_contract": {
        "raw_B_initial_read_present":
            raw_initial_read,

        "B_initial_constructed":
            b_initial_constructed,

        "B_controller_present":
            b_controller_present,

        "MultiEgoRuntime_present":
            multi_ego_runtime_present,

        "runtime_state_access_present":
            runtime_state_access_present,

        "nonzero_or_unresolved_raw_state_accesses":
            nonzero_raw_index_lines,
    },

    "important_boundary": {
        "collector_side_usage_only":
            True,

        "candidate_transformation_not_yet_frozen":
            True,

        "candidate_files_created":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "B_POSTINITIAL_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "B_POSTINITIAL_AUDIT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "B_POSTINITIAL_AUDIT_SHA256="
    + sha(
        out_p
    )
)

print("CANDIDATE_FILES_CREATED=False")
print("LOADER_EXECUTED=False")
print("NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False")
print("TRAINING_EXECUTED=False")
print("MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False")

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

这一步也已经 PASS，而且结论很关键：

collector 对原始 B trajectory 的直接读取只发生在初始化阶段；

raw_states[field][0] 之后，没有读取 raw_states 的后续帧；

B 初始化后由 PerfectTrackingController + MultiEgoRuntime 接管；

后续科学计算读取的是 runtime.state_for("B")。

 


NONZERO_OR_UNRESOLVED_RAW_STATE_ACCESS_COUNT=0，所以可以确认不会因为保留旧的第1～899帧而影响 collector 运行。


因此现在可以正式冻结 transformation：

A：改 ego_info.start_states.{x,y,yaw_rad}；

B：只改 TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.{x,y,yaw_rad}[0]；

v_mps=4.5、acc_mpss=0、yawrate_radps 保持原值；

manifest 只改 start_route_s.A/B 和对应 approach distance；

route、conflict point、seed、MCTS、target speed 全不变。

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime

(
    prereg_p,
    poses_p,
    postb_p,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


prereg = json.loads(
    prereg_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

poses = json.loads(
    poses_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

postb = json.loads(
    postb_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

scenario = json.loads(
    scenario_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

manifest = json.loads(
    manifest_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert prereg["status"] == \
    "FROZEN_BEFORE_CANDIDATE_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTION"

assert poses["status"] == \
    "PASS_TARGET_POSE_RESOLUTION"

assert postb["status"] == \
    "PASS_COLLECTOR_B_POSTINITIAL_USE"

print("TRANSFORMATION_INPUT_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# Existing state contract
# ============================================================

ego_start = scenario[
    "ego_info"
][
    "start_states"
]

states = scenario[
    "TrajSegmentInfo"
][0][
    "states"
]

assert float(
    ego_start["v_mps"]
) == 4.5

assert float(
    ego_start["acc_mpss"]
) == 0.0

assert float(
    states["v_mps"][0][0]
) == 4.5

assert float(
    states["acc_mpss"][0][0]
) == 0.0

print("SOURCE_STATE_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# Exact frozen transformations
# ============================================================

targets = {}

for level in (
    "60m",
    "100m",
):

    d = float(
        level[:-1]
    )

    A = poses[
        "target_poses"
    ][level]["A"]

    B = poses[
        "target_poses"
    ][level]["B"]

    targets[level] = {
        "distance_to_conflict_m":
            d,

        "scenario_changes": {
            "ego_info.start_states.x":
                A["x"],

            "ego_info.start_states.y":
                A["y"],

            "ego_info.start_states.yaw_rad":
                A["yaw_rad"],

            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.x[0][0]":
                B["x"],

            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.y[0][0]":
                B["y"],

            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.yaw_rad[0][0]":
                B["yaw_rad"],
        },

        "scenario_preserve": {
            "ego_info.start_states.v_mps":
                4.5,

            "ego_info.start_states.acc_mpss":
                0.0,

            "ego_info.start_states.yawrate_radps":
                float(
                    ego_start[
                        "yawrate_radps"
                    ]
                ),

            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.v_mps[0][0]":
                4.5,

            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.acc_mpss[0][0]":
                0.0,

            "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.yawrate_radps[0][0]":
                float(
                    states[
                        "yawrate_radps"
                    ][0][0]
                ),
        },

            "start_route_s.A":
                A["route_s"],

                d,

                    manifest[

                    ]

                dict(
                    manifest[
                        "conflict_route_s"
                    ]
                ),

            "source_index":
                manifest[
                    "source_index"
                ],
        },
    }


for level, spec in targets.items():

    print()
    print(
        "TRANSFORMATION_LEVEL="
        + level
    )

    for k, v in spec[

invariants = {

Transformation 已经正式冻结，SHA：

a41e5897ed28fe3782879385535f7ed2d2d016760ce6d7043616e6dcbd1d2230

cd /root/MineSim-Dynamic
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null || true
conda activate minesim
export PYTHONPATH=/root/MineSim-Dynamic

(
set -eu

ROOT=/root/autodl-tmp
V3="$ROOT/paper1_value_v3_development_only_v1"

FREEZE="$V3/PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_EXACT_SCENARIO_TRANSFORMATION_FREEZE_V1.json"

SRC_SCENARIO="$ROOT/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/scenarios_v1/Scenario-fullmine-v4-cross-scene-c11-dual-aligned-v1.json"

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_FREEZE=a41e5897ed28fe3782879385535f7ed2d2d016760ce6d7043616e6dcbd1d2230
EXPECTED_SCENARIO=1127aeb78308b90a9d59127f9443fea4acd8b98e136cbf86d1201f7593978cc9
EXPECTED_MANIFEST=7bc4a6e327557bb038cd5da32c8b4af76976f470f2e8369201248a0cec7cfe12

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== GENERATE C11 60M/100M STATIC CANDIDATES ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$FREEZE" "$EXPECTED_FREEZE" TRANSFORMATION_FREEZE
check_sha "$SRC_SCENARIO" "$EXPECTED_SCENARIO" SOURCE_SCENARIO
check_sha "$SRC_MANIFEST" "$EXPECTED_MANIFEST" SOURCE_MANIFEST

[ ! -e "$OUTDIR" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_DIR_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUTDIR"
    false
}

mkdir -p "$OUTDIR"

python - \
"$FREEZE" \
"$SRC_SCENARIO" \
"$SRC_MANIFEST" \

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import copy

freeze_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
scenario_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
manifest_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
outdir = Path(sys.argv[4])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


freeze = json.loads(
    freeze_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

source_scenario = json.loads(
    scenario_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

source_manifest = json.loads(
    manifest_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    freeze["status"]
    == "FROZEN_EXACT_60M_100M_TRANSFORMATION"
)

print("FREEZE_CONTRACT=PASS")


def set_nested_scenario(
    obj,
    level,
):

    spec = freeze[
        "targets"
    ][level]

    changes = spec[
        "scenario_changes"
    ]

    obj[
        "ego_info"
    ][
        "start_states"
    ][
        "x"
    ] = changes[
        "ego_info.start_states.x"
    ]

    obj[
        "ego_info"
    ][
        "start_states"
    ][
        "y"
    ] = changes[
        "ego_info.start_states.y"
    ]

    obj[
        "ego_info"
    ][
        "start_states"
    ][
        "yaw_rad"
    ] = changes[
        "ego_info.start_states.yaw_rad"
    ]

    states = obj[
        "TrajSegmentInfo"
    ][0][
        "states"
    ]

    states[
        "x"
    ][0][0] = changes[
        "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.x[0][0]"
    ]

    states[
        "y"
    ][0][0] = changes[
        "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.y[0][0]"
    ]

    states[
        "yaw_rad"
    ][0][0] = changes[
        "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.yaw_rad[0][0]"
    ]


def set_nested_manifest(
    obj,
    level,
):

    spec = freeze[
        "targets"
    ][level]

    changes = spec[
        "manifest_changes"
    ]

    obj[
        "start_route_s"
    ][
        "A"
    ] = changes[
        "start_route_s.A"
    ]

    obj[
        "start_route_s"
    ][
        "B"
    ] = changes[
        "start_route_s.B"
    ]

    obj[
        "approach_distance_m"
    ] = changes[
        "approach_distance_m"
    ]


def diff_paths(
    a,
    b,
    prefix="",
):

    out = []

    if type(a) is not type(b):

        out.append(
            prefix
        )

        return out

    if isinstance(
        a,
        dict,
    ):

        keys = (
            set(a.keys())
            | set(b.keys())
        )

        for k in sorted(
            keys
        ):

            p = (
                f"{prefix}.{k}"
                if prefix
                else str(k)
            )

            if (
                k not in a
                or k not in b
            ):
                out.append(
                    p
                )

            else:
                out.extend(
                    diff_paths(
                        a[k],
                        b[k],
                        p,
                    )
                )

        return out

    if isinstance(
        a,
        list,
    ):

        if len(a) != len(b):

            out.append(
                prefix + ".length"
            )

            return out

        for i, (
            x,
            y,
        ) in enumerate(
            zip(
                a,
                b,
            )
        ):

            p = (
                f"{prefix}[{i}]"
            )

            out.extend(
                diff_paths(
                    x,
                    y,
                    p,
                )
            )

        return out

    if a != b:
        out.append(
            prefix
        )

    return out


expected_scenario_changes = {
    "ego_info.start_states.x",
    "ego_info.start_states.y",
    "ego_info.start_states.yaw_rad",
    "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.x[0][0]",
    "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.y[0][0]",
    "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.yaw_rad[0][0]",
}

expected_manifest_changes = {
    "start_route_s.A",
    "start_route_s.B",
    "approach_distance_m",
}


results = {}


for level in (
    "60m",
    "100m",
):

    scenario = copy.deepcopy(
        source_scenario
    )

    manifest = copy.deepcopy(
        source_manifest
    )

    set_nested_scenario(
        scenario,
        level,
    )

    set_nested_manifest(
        manifest,
        level,
    )

    scenario_name = (
        freeze[
            "candidate_file_naming"
        ][
            level + "_scenario"
        ]
    )

    manifest_name = (
        freeze[
            "candidate_file_naming"
        ][
            level + "_manifest"
        ]
    )

    scenario_out = (
        outdir
        / scenario_name
    )

    manifest_out = (
        outdir
        / manifest_name
    )

    scenario_out.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            scenario,
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            allow_nan=False,
        )
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    manifest_out.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            manifest,
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            allow_nan=False,
        )
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


    sdiff = set(
        diff_paths(
            source_scenario,
            scenario,
        )
    )

    mdiff = set(
        diff_paths(
            source_manifest,
            manifest,
        )
    )


    print()
    print(
        "CANDIDATE_LEVEL="
        + level
    )

    print(
        "SCENARIO_FILE="
        + str(
            scenario_out
        )
    )

    print(
        "SCENARIO_SHA256="
        + sha(
            scenario_out
        )
    )

    print(
        "MANIFEST_FILE="
        + str(
            manifest_out
        )
    )

    print(
        "MANIFEST_SHA256="
        + sha(
            manifest_out
        )
    )

    print(
        "SCENARIO_DIFF_PATHS="
        + repr(
            sorted(
                sdiff
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        "MANIFEST_DIFF_PATHS="
        + repr(
            sorted(
                mdiff
            )
        )
    )


    scenario_diff_pass = (
        sdiff
        == expected_scenario_changes
    )

    manifest_diff_pass = (
        mdiff
        == expected_manifest_changes
    )


    print(
        "SCENARIO_EXACT_DIFF_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if scenario_diff_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

    print(
        "MANIFEST_EXACT_DIFF_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if manifest_diff_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    # fixed state invariants
    assert (
        scenario[
            "ego_info"
        ][
            "start_states"
        ][
            "v_mps"
        ]
        ==
        source_scenario[
            "ego_info"
        ][
            "start_states"
        ][
            "v_mps"
        ]
    )

    assert (
        scenario[
            "ego_info"
        ][
            "start_states"
        ][
            "acc_mpss"
        ]
        ==
        source_scenario[
            "ego_info"
        ][
            "start_states"
        ][
            "acc_mpss"
        ]
    )

    for field in (
        "v_mps",
        "acc_mpss",
        "yawrate_radps",
    ):

        assert (
            scenario[
                "TrajSegmentInfo"
            ][0][
                "states"
            ][field][0][0]
            ==
            source_scenario[
                "TrajSegmentInfo"
            ][0][
                "states"
            ][field][0][0]
        )


    results[
        level
    ] = {
        "scenario_file":
            str(
                scenario_out
            ),

        "scenario_sha256":
            sha(
                scenario_out
            ),

        "manifest_file":
            str(
                manifest_out
            ),

        "manifest_sha256":
            sha(
                manifest_out
            ),

        "scenario_diff_paths":
            sorted(
                sdiff
            ),

        "manifest_diff_paths":
            sorted(
                mdiff
            ),

        "scenario_exact_diff_gate":
            scenario_diff_pass,

        "manifest_exact_diff_gate":
            manifest_diff_pass,
    }


all_pass = all(
    row[
        "scenario_exact_diff_gate"
    ]
    and row[
        "manifest_exact_diff_gate"
    ]

    for row in results.values()
)


print()
print(
    "ALL_CANDIDATE_EXACT_DIFF_GATE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if all_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


summary = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_STATIC_CANDIDATE_GENERATION_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_STATIC_CANDIDATE_GENERATION"
            if all_pass
            else
            "FAIL_STATIC_CANDIDATE_GENERATION"
        ),

    "transformation_freeze_sha256":
        sha(
            freeze_p
        ),

    "source_scenario_sha256":
        sha(
            scenario_p
        ),

    "source_manifest_sha256":
        sha(
            manifest_p
        ),

    "candidates":
        results,

    "important_boundary": {
        "candidate_files_created":
            True,

        "source_files_modified":
            False,

        "loader_executed":
            False,

        "runtime_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        (
            "STATIC_VALIDATE_60M_100M_CANDIDATE_CONTENT"
            if all_pass
            else
            "DIAGNOSE_CANDIDATE_DIFF_ONLY"
        ),
}


summary_p = (
    outdir
    / "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_STATIC_CANDIDATE_GENERATION_V1.json"
)

summary_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        summary,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print(
    "CANDIDATE_GENERATION_SUMMARY="
    + str(
        summary_p
    )
)

print(
    "CANDIDATE_GENERATION_SUMMARY_SHA256="
    + sha(
        summary_p
    )
)

    "SOURCE_FILES_MODIFIED=False"
)

    "LOADER_EXECUTED=False"

        "next_stage"

ROOT=/root/autodl-tmp
V3="$ROOT/paper1_value_v3_development_only_v1"

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import math

from shapely.geometry import LineString, Point

(
    freeze_p,
    poses_p,
    gen_p,
    src_scenario_p,
    src_manifest_p,
    semantic_p,
    s60_p,
    m60_p,
    s100_p,
    m100_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


freeze = load(freeze_p)
poses = load(poses_p)
generation = load(gen_p)

src_scenario = load(
    src_scenario_p
)

src_manifest = load(
    src_manifest_p
)

semantic = load(
    semantic_p
)


assert (
    freeze["status"]
    ==
    "FROZEN_EXACT_60M_100M_TRANSFORMATION"
)

assert (
    poses["status"]
    ==
    "PASS_TARGET_POSE_RESOLUTION"
)

assert (
    generation["status"]
    ==
    "PASS_STATIC_CANDIDATE_GENERATION"
)

print("PRIOR_ARTIFACT_CONTRACT=PASS")


records = {
    r["token"]: r
    for r in semantic[
        "reference_path"
    ]
}


def make_line(tokens):

    points = []

    for token in tokens:

        segment = [
            (
                float(p[0]),
                float(p[1]),
            )
            for p in records[
                token
            ][
                "waypoints"
            ]
        ]

        if (
            points
            and math.dist(
                points[-1],
                segment[0],
            )
            < 1e-7
        ):
            segment = segment[1:]

        points.extend(
            segment
        )

    return LineString(
        points
    )


routes = {
    "A":
        make_line(
            src_manifest[
                "route_a"
            ]
        ),

    "B":
        make_line(
            src_manifest[
                "route_b"
            ]
        ),
}


def recursive_diff(
    a,
    b,
    prefix="",
):

    out = []

    if type(a) is not type(b):
        return [
            prefix
        ]

    if isinstance(
        a,
        dict,
    ):

        keys = (
            set(a)
            | set(b)
        )

        for k in sorted(
            keys
        ):

            p = (
                f"{prefix}.{k}"
                if prefix
                else str(k)
            )

            if (
                k not in a
                or k not in b
            ):
                out.append(
                    p
                )

            else:
                out.extend(
                    recursive_diff(
                        a[k],
                        b[k],
                        p,
                    )
                )

        return out

    if isinstance(
        a,
        list,
    ):

        if len(a) != len(b):

            out.append(
                prefix + ".length"
            )

            return out

        for i, (
            x,
            y,
        ) in enumerate(
            zip(a, b)
        ):

            out.extend(
                recursive_diff(
                    x,
                    y,
                    f"{prefix}[{i}]",
                )
            )

        return out

    if a != b:
        out.append(
            prefix
        )

    return out


expected_sdiff = {
    "ego_info.start_states.x",
    "ego_info.start_states.y",
    "ego_info.start_states.yaw_rad",
    "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.x[0][0]",
    "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.y[0][0]",
    "TrajSegmentInfo[0].states.yaw_rad[0][0]",
}

expected_mdiff = {
    "approach_distance_m",
    "start_route_s.A",
    "start_route_s.B",
}


def close(
    a,
    b,
    tol=1e-12,
):
    return abs(
        float(a)
        - float(b)
    ) <= tol


def validate_candidate(
    level,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
):

    scenario = load(
        scenario_p
    )

    manifest = load(
        manifest_p
    )

    target = freeze[
        "targets"
    ][level]

    target_pose = poses[
        "target_poses"
    ][level]

    distance = float(
        level[:-1]
    )


    print()
    print(
        "VALIDATE_LEVEL="
        + level
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Exact diff
    # ========================================================

    sdiff = set(
        recursive_diff(
            src_scenario,
            scenario,
        )
    )

    mdiff = set(
        recursive_diff(
            src_manifest,
            manifest,
        )
    )


    print(
        "SCENARIO_DIFF_PATHS="
        + repr(
            sorted(sdiff)
        )
    )

    print(
        "MANIFEST_DIFF_PATHS="
        + repr(
            sorted(mdiff)
        )
    )


    exact_diff = (
        sdiff
        == expected_sdiff
        and mdiff
        == expected_mdiff
    )

    print(
        "EXACT_DIFF_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if exact_diff
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Exact A pose
    # ========================================================

    A = scenario[
        "ego_info"
    ][
        "start_states"
    ]

    A_pose_ok = all(
        (
            close(
                A["x"],
                target_pose["A"]["x"],
            ),
            close(
                A["y"],
                target_pose["A"]["y"],
            ),
            close(
                A["yaw_rad"],
                target_pose["A"]["yaw_rad"],
            ),
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Exact B sample0 pose
    # ========================================================

    states = scenario[
        "TrajSegmentInfo"
    ][0][
        "states"
    ]

    B_pose_ok = all(
        (
            close(
                states["x"][0][0],
                target_pose["B"]["x"],
            ),
            close(
                states["y"][0][0],
                target_pose["B"]["y"],
            ),
            close(
                states[
                    "yaw_rad"
                ][0][0],
                target_pose[
                    "B"
                ][
                    "yaw_rad"
                ],
            ),
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_TARGET_POSE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if A_pose_ok
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_TARGET_POSE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if B_pose_ok
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Manifest target contract
    # ========================================================

    manifest_ok = all(
        (
            close(
                manifest[
                    "approach_distance_m"
                ],
                distance,
            ),

            close(
                manifest[
                    "start_route_s"
                ]["A"],
                target_pose["A"]["route_s"],
            ),

            close(
                manifest[
                    "start_route_s"
                ]["B"],
                target_pose["B"]["route_s"],
            ),

            manifest[
                "route_a"
            ]
            ==
            src_manifest[
                "route_a"
            ],

            manifest[
                "route_b"
            ]
            ==
            src_manifest[
                "route_b"
            ],

            manifest[
                "conflict_route_s"
            ]
            ==
            src_manifest[
                "conflict_route_s"
            ],

            manifest[
                "source_index"
            ]
            ==
            src_manifest[
                "source_index"
            ],
        )
    )

    print(
        "MANIFEST_TARGET_CONTRACT="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if manifest_ok
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Preserve A dynamics
    # ========================================================

    A_dynamics_ok = all(
        scenario[
            "ego_info"
        ][
            "start_states"
        ][field]
        ==
        src_scenario[
            "ego_info"
        ][
            "start_states"
        ][field]

        for field in (
            "v_mps",
            "acc_mpss",
            "yawrate_radps",
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Preserve B sample0 dynamics
    # ========================================================

    B0_dynamics_ok = all(
        scenario[
            "TrajSegmentInfo"
        ][0][
            "states"
        ][field][0]
        ==
        src_scenario[
            "TrajSegmentInfo"
        ][0][
            "states"
        ][field][0]

        for field in (
            "v_mps",
            "acc_mpss",
            "yawrate_radps",
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_INITIAL_DYNAMICS_PRESERVED="
        + repr(
            A_dynamics_ok
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_INITIAL_DYNAMICS_PRESERVED="
        + repr(
            B0_dynamics_ok
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Critical: B samples 1..end unchanged
    # ========================================================

    B_postinitial_ok = True

    changed_postinitial = []

    for field in (
        "x",
        "y",
        "yaw_rad",
        "v_mps",
        "yawrate_radps",
        "acc_mpss",
    ):

        src_col = src_scenario[
            "TrajSegmentInfo"
        ][0][
            "states"
        ][field]

        cand_col = scenario[
            "TrajSegmentInfo"
        ][0][
            "states"
        ][field]

        if (
            src_col[1:]
            != cand_col[1:]
        ):

            B_postinitial_ok = False

            changed_postinitial.append(
                field
            )


    print(
        "B_POSTINITIAL_899_SAMPLES_UNCHANGED="
        + repr(
            B_postinitial_ok
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_POSTINITIAL_CHANGED_FIELDS="
        + repr(
            changed_postinitial
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Route-s projection
    # ========================================================

    A_projected = float(
        routes["A"].project(
            Point(
                float(A["x"]),
                float(A["y"]),
            )
        )
    )

    B_projected = float(
        routes["B"].project(
            Point(
                float(
                    states["x"][0][0]
                ),
                float(
                    states["y"][0][0]
                ),
            )
        )
    )


    A_route_error = abs(
        A_projected
        - float(
            manifest[
                "start_route_s"
            ]["A"]
        )
    )

    B_route_error = abs(
        B_projected
        - float(
            manifest[
                "start_route_s"
            ]["B"]
        )
    )


    route_projection_ok = (
        A_route_error <= 1e-6
        and B_route_error <= 1e-6
    )


    print(
        "A_PROJECTED_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(
            A_projected
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_PROJECTED_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(
            B_projected
        )
    )

    print(
        "A_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
        + repr(
            A_route_error
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
        + repr(
            B_route_error
        )
    )

    print(
        "ROUTE_S_PROJECTION_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if route_projection_ok
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Distance-to-conflict reconstruction
    # ========================================================

    conflict_A = float(
        manifest[
            "conflict_route_s"
        ]["A"]
    )

    conflict_B = float(
        manifest[
            "conflict_route_s"
        ]["B"]
    )

    dA = (
        conflict_A
        - A_projected
    )

    dB = (
        conflict_B
        - B_projected
    )


    distance_gate = (
        abs(
            dA - distance
        )
        <= 1e-6
        and abs(
            dB - distance
        )
        <= 1e-6
    )


    print(
        "A_DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_M="
        + repr(dA)
    )

    print(
        "B_DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_M="
        + repr(dB)
    )

    print(
        "DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if distance_gate
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    all_pass = all(
        (
            exact_diff,
            A_pose_ok,
            B_pose_ok,
            manifest_ok,
            A_dynamics_ok,
            B0_dynamics_ok,
            B_postinitial_ok,
            route_projection_ok,
            distance_gate,
        )
    )


    print(
        "STATIC_CONTENT_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if all_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    return {
        "scenario_sha256":
            sha(
                scenario_p
            ),

        "manifest_sha256":
            sha(
                manifest_p
            ),

        "exact_diff_gate":
            exact_diff,

        "A_target_pose_gate":
            A_pose_ok,

        "B_target_pose_gate":
            B_pose_ok,

        "manifest_target_contract":
            manifest_ok,

        "A_initial_dynamics_preserved":
            A_dynamics_ok,

        "B_initial_dynamics_preserved":
            B0_dynamics_ok,

        "B_postinitial_899_samples_unchanged":
            B_postinitial_ok,

        "B_postinitial_changed_fields":
            changed_postinitial,

        "A_projected_route_s":
            A_projected,

        "B_projected_route_s":
            B_projected,

        "A_route_s_error":
            A_route_error,

        "B_route_s_error":
            B_route_error,

        "route_s_projection_gate":
            route_projection_ok,

        "A_distance_to_conflict_m":
            dA,

        "B_distance_to_conflict_m":
            dB,

        "distance_to_conflict_gate":
            distance_gate,

        "static_content_gate":
            all_pass,
    }


results = {
    "60m":
        validate_candidate(
            "60m",
            s60_p,
            m60_p,
        ),

    "100m":
        validate_candidate(
            "100m",
            s100_p,
            m100_p,
        ),
}

    x[
        "static_content_gate"
    ]
    for x in results.values()
)

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 60M/100M CANDIDATE LOADER SMOKE ==="

    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}
