"$SOURCE_MEMBER" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import ast
import math

from shapely.geometry import LineString, Point

from devkit.scenario_builder.minesim_scenario_json.minesim_convert_data_type import (
    MinesimScenarioType,
    ScenarioFileBaseInfo,
)

from devkit.scenario_builder.minesim_scenario_json.minesim_dynamic_scenario import (
    MineSimDynamicScenario,
)

from devkit.sim_engine.planning.planner.mcts.multi_ego_adapter import (
    agent_to_ego_state,
    tracked_object_token,
)

from devkit.common.actor_state.vehicle_parameters import (
    get_mine_truck_parameters,
)


(
    static_p,
    zip_p,
    semantic_p,
    s60_p,
    m60_p,
    s100_p,
    m100_p,
    out_p,
    source_member,
) = sys.argv[1:]

static_p = Path(static_p)
zip_p = Path(zip_p)
semantic_p = Path(semantic_p)
s60_p = Path(s60_p)
m60_p = Path(m60_p)
s100_p = Path(s100_p)
m100_p = Path(m100_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


static = load(
    static_p
)

assert (
    static["status"]
    == "PASS_STATIC_CONTENT_VALIDATION"
)

print("STATIC_CONTENT_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Recover exact frozen runtime constants from collector
# ============================================================

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


tree = ast.parse(
    source
)


literal_assignments = {}

for node in tree.body:

    if not isinstance(
        node,
        (
            ast.Assign,
            ast.AnnAssign,
        ),
    ):
        continue

    if isinstance(
        node,
        ast.Assign,
    ):

        if len(
            node.targets
        ) != 1:
            continue

        target = node.targets[0]

    else:
        target = node.target

    if not isinstance(
        target,
        ast.Name,
    ):
        continue

    try:
        value = ast.literal_eval(
            node.value
        )
    except Exception:
        continue

    literal_assignments[
        target.id
    ] = value


LOCATION = literal_assignments.get(
    "LOCATION"
)

if LOCATION is None:

    # Existing FullMine semantic/runtime path.
    # Do not silently continue if source cannot establish it.
    for line in source.splitlines():

        if (
            "LOCATION =" in line
            and "=" in line
        ):

            rhs = line.split(
                "=",
                1,
            )[1].strip()

            try:
                LOCATION = ast.literal_eval(
                    rhs
                )
            except Exception:
                pass


if LOCATION is None:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "LOCATION_NOT_RESOLVED_FROM_FROZEN_COLLECTOR"
    )


print(
    "COLLECTOR_LOCATION="
    + repr(
        LOCATION
    )
)


DATA_ROOT = Path(
    "/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_vector_v2_targeted_runtime"
)

MAP_ROOT = (
    DATA_ROOT
    / "maps"
)


assert DATA_ROOT.exists()
assert MAP_ROOT.exists()

print(
    "RUNTIME_ROOT_CONTRACT=PASS"
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Frozen semantic route geometry
# ============================================================

semantic = load(
    semantic_p
)

records = {
    r["token"]: r
    for r in semantic[
        "reference_path"
    ]
}


def make_line(tokens):

    points = []

    for token in tokens:

        assert token in records

        segment = [
            (
                float(p[0]),
                float(p[1]),
            )
            for p in records[
                token
            ][
                "waypoints"
            ]
        ]

        if (
            points
            and math.dist(
                points[-1],
                segment[0],
            )
            < 1e-7
        ):
            segment = segment[1:]

        points.extend(
            segment
        )

    return LineString(
        points
    )


# ============================================================
# 3. Candidate loader smoke
# ============================================================

def run_candidate(
    level,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
):

    manifest = load(
        manifest_p
    )

    routes = {
        "A":
            make_line(
                manifest[
                    "route_a"
                ]
            ),

        "B":
            make_line(
                manifest[
                    "route_b"
                ]
            ),
    }


    print()
    print(
        "LOADER_LEVEL="
        + level
    )


    info = ScenarioFileBaseInfo(
        log_file_name=
            scenario_p.name,

        scenario_name=
            (
                "fullmine_v4_cross_scene_"
                "c11_value_v3_"
                + level
                + "_loader_smoke_v1"
            ),

        location=
            LOCATION,

        scenario_type=
            MinesimScenarioType.INTERSECTION_MIXED,

        data_root=
            str(
                DATA_ROOT
            ),

        map_root=
            str(
                MAP_ROOT
            ),
    )


    scenario = MineSimDynamicScenario(
        scenario_file=info
    )


    scenario.extract_tracked_objects_within_entrintime_window()


    A = scenario.initial_ego_state


    objects = (
        scenario
        .get_tracked_objects_at_iteration(0)
        .tracked_objects
        .tracked_objects
    )


    object1 = [
        obj
        for obj in objects
        if (
            tracked_object_token(
                obj
            )
            == "object-1"
        )
    ]


    print(
        "OBJECT1_COUNT="
        + str(
            len(
                object1
            )
        )
    )


    assert len(
        object1
    ) == 1


    vehicle = get_mine_truck_parameters(
        LOCATION
    )


    B = agent_to_ego_state(
        object1[0],
        scenario.get_time_point(0),
        vehicle,
    )


    A_s = float(
        routes["A"].project(
            Point(
                float(
                    A.rear_axle.x
                ),
                float(
                    A.rear_axle.y
                ),
            )
        )
    )


    B_s = float(
        routes["B"].project(
            Point(
                float(
                    B.rear_axle.x
                ),
                float(
                    B.rear_axle.y
                ),
            )
        )
    )


    expected_A = float(
        manifest[
            "start_route_s"
        ]["A"]
    )

    expected_B = float(
        manifest[
            "start_route_s"
        ]["B"]
    )


    A_error = abs(
        A_s
        - expected_A
    )

    B_error = abs(
        B_s
        - expected_B
    )


    distance = float(
        manifest[
            "approach_distance_m"
        ]
    )


    A_conflict = float(
        manifest[
            "conflict_route_s"
        ]["A"]
    )

    B_conflict = float(
        manifest[
            "conflict_route_s"
        ]["B"]
    )


    A_distance = (
        A_conflict
        - A_s
    )

    B_distance = (
        B_conflict
        - B_s
    )


    alignment_pass = (
        A_error <= 1e-3
        and B_error <= 1e-3
    )


    distance_pass = (
        abs(
            A_distance
            - distance
        )
        <= 1e-3
        and abs(
            B_distance
            - distance
        )
        <= 1e-3
    )


    finite_pass = all(
        math.isfinite(x)
        for x in (
            float(
                A.rear_axle.x
            ),
            float(
                A.rear_axle.y
            ),
            float(
                A.rear_axle.heading
            ),
            float(
                B.rear_axle.x
            ),
            float(
                B.rear_axle.y
            ),
            float(
                B.rear_axle.heading
            ),
            A_s,
            B_s,
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_LOADED_POSE="
        + repr(
            {
                "x":
                    float(
                        A.rear_axle.x
                    ),

                "y":
                    float(
                        A.rear_axle.y
                    ),

                "yaw":
                    float(
                        A.rear_axle.heading
                    ),
            }
        )
    )


    print(
        "B_LOADED_POSE="
        + repr(
            {
                "x":
                    float(
                        B.rear_axle.x
                    ),

                "y":
                    float(
                        B.rear_axle.y
                    ),

                "yaw":
                    float(
                        B.rear_axle.heading
                    ),
            }
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_LOADED_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(
            A_s
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_LOADED_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(
            B_s
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
        + repr(
            A_error
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
        + repr(
            B_error
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_M="
        + repr(
            A_distance
        )
    )

    print(
        "B_DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_M="
        + repr(
            B_distance
        )
    )


    print(
        "INITIAL_ALIGNMENT_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if alignment_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

    print(
        "DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if distance_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

    print(
        "FINITE_STATE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if finite_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    smoke_pass = all(
        (
            len(object1) == 1,
            alignment_pass,
            distance_pass,
            finite_pass,
        )
    )


    print(
        "LOADER_SMOKE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if smoke_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    return {
        "scenario_sha256":
            sha(
                scenario_p
            ),

        "manifest_sha256":
            sha(
                manifest_p
            ),

        "object1_count":
            len(
                object1
            ),

        "A_loaded_route_s":
            A_s,

        "B_loaded_route_s":
            B_s,

        "A_route_s_error":
            A_error,

        "B_route_s_error":
            B_error,

        "A_distance_to_conflict_m":
            A_distance,

        "B_distance_to_conflict_m":
            B_distance,

        "initial_alignment_gate":
            alignment_pass,

        "distance_to_conflict_gate":
            distance_pass,

        "finite_state_gate":
            finite_pass,

        "loader_smoke_gate":
            smoke_pass,
    }


results = {
    "60m":
        run_candidate(
            "60m",
            s60_p,
            m60_p,
        ),

    "100m":
        run_candidate(
            "100m",
            s100_p,
            m100_p,
        ),
}


all_pass = all(
    row[
        "loader_smoke_gate"
    ]
    for row in results.values()
)


print()
print(
    "ALL_LOADER_SMOKE_GATE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if all_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_LOADER_SMOKE_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_LOADER_SMOKE"
            if all_pass
            else
            "FAIL_LOADER_SMOKE"
        ),

    "static_content_validation_sha256":

            static_p
        ),

            zip_p

            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        (
            "STATIC_AND_RUNTIME_INITIAL_SAFETY_GATE"
            if all_pass
            else
            "DIAGNOSE_LOADER_SMOKE_ONLY"
        ),
}

    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)

    "LOADER_SMOKE_RESULT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "LOADER_SMOKE_RESULT_SHA256="
    + sha(
        out_p
    )
)

    "ENVIRONMENT_SIMULATION_CREATED=False"
)

)

)

    ]

    E="$2"

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import zipfile
import hashlib
import ast
import math

from shapely.geometry import LineString, Point

from devkit.scenario_builder.minesim_scenario_json.minesim_convert_data_type import (
    MinesimScenarioType,
    ScenarioFileBaseInfo,
)

from devkit.scenario_builder.minesim_scenario_json.minesim_dynamic_scenario import (
    MineSimDynamicScenario,
)

from devkit.sim_engine.planning.planner.mcts.multi_ego_adapter import (
    agent_to_ego_state,
    tracked_object_token,
)

from devkit.common.actor_state.vehicle_parameters import (
    get_mine_truck_parameters,
)


(
    static_p,
    zip_p,
    semantic_p,
    s60_p,
    m60_p,
    s100_p,
    m100_p,
    out_p,
    source_member,
) = sys.argv[1:]

static_p = Path(static_p)
zip_p = Path(zip_p)
semantic_p = Path(semantic_p)
s60_p = Path(s60_p)
m60_p = Path(m60_p)
s100_p = Path(s100_p)
m100_p = Path(m100_p)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


static = load(static_p)

assert (
    static["status"]
    == "PASS_STATIC_CONTENT_VALIDATION"
)

print("STATIC_CONTENT_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# Recover exact LOCATION from frozen collector
# ============================================================

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    zip_p,
    "r",
) as z:

    assert source_member in z.namelist()

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(
        "utf-8"
    )


tree = ast.parse(source)

LOCATION = None

for node in tree.body:

    if not isinstance(
        node,
        ast.Assign,
    ):
        continue

    if len(node.targets) != 1:
        continue

    target = node.targets[0]

    if (
        isinstance(target, ast.Name)
        and target.id == "LOCATION"
    ):

        try:
            LOCATION = ast.literal_eval(
                node.value
            )
        except Exception:
            pass


if LOCATION is None:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "LOCATION_NOT_RESOLVED_FROM_FROZEN_COLLECTOR"
    )


print(
    "COLLECTOR_LOCATION="
    + repr(LOCATION)
)


DATA_ROOT = Path(
    "/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_vector_v2_targeted_runtime"
)

MAP_ROOT = DATA_ROOT / "maps"

assert DATA_ROOT.exists()
assert MAP_ROOT.exists()

print("RUNTIME_ROOT_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# Semantic routes
# ============================================================

semantic = load(semantic_p)

records = {
    r["token"]: r
    for r in semantic["reference_path"]
}


def make_line(tokens):

    points = []

    for token in tokens:

        segment = [
            (
                float(p[0]),
                float(p[1]),
            )
            for p in records[token]["waypoints"]
        ]

        if (
            points
            and math.dist(
                points[-1],
                segment[0],
            )
            < 1e-7
        ):
            segment = segment[1:]

        points.extend(segment)

    return LineString(points)


# ============================================================
# Loader smoke only
# ============================================================

def run_candidate(
    level,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
):

    manifest = load(manifest_p)

    routes = {
        "A":
            make_line(
                manifest["route_a"]
            ),

        "B":
            make_line(
                manifest["route_b"]
            ),
    }


    print()
    print(
        "LOADER_LEVEL="
        + level
    )


    # Exact frozen constructor contract.
    info = ScenarioFileBaseInfo(
        log_file_load_path=
            str(scenario_p),

        log_file_name=
            scenario_p.name,

        scenario_name=
            (
                "fullmine_v4_cross_scene_"
                "c11_value_v3_"
                + level
                + "_loader_smoke_v2"
            ),

        location=
            LOCATION,

        scenario_type=
            MinesimScenarioType.INTERSECTION_MIXED,

        data_root=
            str(DATA_ROOT),

        map_root=
            str(MAP_ROOT),
    )


    print(
        "SCENARIO_FILE_BASE_INFO_GATE=PASS"
    )


    scenario = MineSimDynamicScenario(
        scenario_file=info
    )


    print(
        "MINESIM_DYNAMIC_SCENARIO_CONSTRUCTED=True"
    )


    scenario.extract_tracked_objects_within_entrintime_window()


    print(
        "TRACKED_OBJECT_EXTRACTION=PASS"
    )


    A = scenario.initial_ego_state


    objects = (
        scenario
        .get_tracked_objects_at_iteration(0)
        .tracked_objects
        .tracked_objects
    )


    object1 = [
        obj
        for obj in objects
        if tracked_object_token(obj)
        == "object-1"
    ]


    print(
        "OBJECT1_COUNT="
        + str(len(object1))
    )


    assert len(object1) == 1


    vehicle = get_mine_truck_parameters(
        LOCATION
    )


    B = agent_to_ego_state(
        object1[0],
        scenario.get_time_point(0),
        vehicle,
    )


    A_s = float(
        routes["A"].project(
            Point(
                float(A.rear_axle.x),
                float(A.rear_axle.y),
            )
        )
    )


    B_s = float(
        routes["B"].project(
            Point(
                float(B.rear_axle.x),
                float(B.rear_axle.y),
            )
        )
    )


    expected_A = float(
        manifest["start_route_s"]["A"]
    )

    expected_B = float(
        manifest["start_route_s"]["B"]
    )


    A_error = abs(
        A_s - expected_A
    )

    B_error = abs(
        B_s - expected_B
    )


    distance = float(
        manifest["approach_distance_m"]
    )


    conflict_A = float(
        manifest["conflict_route_s"]["A"]
    )

    conflict_B = float(
        manifest["conflict_route_s"]["B"]
    )


    A_distance = (
        conflict_A - A_s
    )

    B_distance = (
        conflict_B - B_s
    )


    alignment_pass = (
        A_error <= 1e-3
        and B_error <= 1e-3
    )


    distance_pass = (
        abs(
            A_distance - distance
        )
        <= 1e-3

        and abs(
            B_distance - distance
        )
        <= 1e-3
    )


    finite_pass = all(
        math.isfinite(x)
        for x in (
            float(A.rear_axle.x),
            float(A.rear_axle.y),
            float(A.rear_axle.heading),
            float(B.rear_axle.x),
            float(B.rear_axle.y),
            float(B.rear_axle.heading),
            A_s,
            B_s,
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_LOADED_POSE="
        + repr(
            {
                "x":
                    float(A.rear_axle.x),

                "y":
                    float(A.rear_axle.y),

                "yaw":
                    float(A.rear_axle.heading),
            }
        )
    )


    print(
        "B_LOADED_POSE="
        + repr(
            {
                "x":
                    float(B.rear_axle.x),

                "y":
                    float(B.rear_axle.y),

                "yaw":
                    float(B.rear_axle.heading),
            }
        )
    )


    print(
        "A_LOADED_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(A_s)
    )

    print(
        "B_LOADED_ROUTE_S="
        + repr(B_s)
    )

    print(
        "A_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
        + repr(A_error)
    )

    print(
        "B_ROUTE_S_ERROR="
        + repr(B_error)
    )

    print(
        "A_DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_M="
        + repr(A_distance)
    )

    print(
        "B_DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_M="
        + repr(B_distance)
    )


    print(
        "INITIAL_ALIGNMENT_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if alignment_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

    print(
        "DISTANCE_TO_CONFLICT_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if distance_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )

    print(
        "FINITE_STATE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if finite_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    smoke_pass = all(
        (
            len(object1) == 1,
            alignment_pass,
            distance_pass,
            finite_pass,
        )
    )


    print(
        "LOADER_SMOKE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if smoke_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    return {
        "scenario_sha256":
            sha(scenario_p),

        "manifest_sha256":
            sha(manifest_p),

        "object1_count":
            len(object1),

        "A_loaded_route_s":
            A_s,

        "B_loaded_route_s":
            B_s,

        "A_route_s_error":
            A_error,

        "B_route_s_error":
            B_error,

        "A_distance_to_conflict_m":
            A_distance,

        "B_distance_to_conflict_m":
            B_distance,

        "initial_alignment_gate":
            alignment_pass,

        "distance_to_conflict_gate":
            distance_pass,

        "finite_state_gate":
            finite_pass,

        "loader_smoke_gate":
            smoke_pass,
    }


results = {
    "60m":
        run_candidate(
            "60m",
            s60_p,
            m60_p,
        ),

    "100m":
        run_candidate(
            "100m",
            s100_p,
            m100_p,
        ),
}


all_pass = all(
    row["loader_smoke_gate"]
    for row in results.values()
)


print()
print(
    "ALL_LOADER_SMOKE_GATE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if all_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_LOADER_SMOKE_V2",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_LOADER_SMOKE"
            if all_pass
            else "FAIL_LOADER_SMOKE"
        ),

    "static_content_validation_sha256":
        sha(static_p),

    "collector_upload_zip_sha256":
        sha(zip_p),

    "technical_patch": {
        "previous_failure":
            "ScenarioFileBaseInfo missing required log_file_load_path",

        "patched_argument":
            "log_file_load_path=str(candidate_scenario_path)",

        "scientific_design_changed":
            False,
    },

    "location":
        LOCATION,

    "candidates":
        results,

    "important_boundary": {
        "scenario_loader_executed":
            True,

        "EnvironmentSimulation_created":
            False,

        "simulation_initialized":
            False,

        "simulation_propagated":
            False,

        "MCTS_executed":
            False,

        "causal_conflict_test_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        (
            "STATIC_AND_RUNTIME_INITIAL_SAFETY_GATE"
            if all_pass
            else
            "DIAGNOSE_LOADER_SMOKE_ONLY"
        ),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print(
    "LOADER_SMOKE_RESULT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "LOADER_SMOKE_RESULT_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)

print(
    "ENVIRONMENT_SIMULATION_CREATED=False"
)

)

)

    "MCTS_EXECUTED=False"

)

    "TRAINING_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEXT="
    + payload["next_stage"]
)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )

    loader["status"]
    == "PASS_LOADER_SMOKE"

    zip_p,

    source = z.read(
        source_member
    ).decode(

    )

LOCATION = None

for node in tree.body:

    if (
        isinstance(node, ast.Assign)
        and len(node.targets) == 1
        and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name)
        and node.targets[0].id == "LOCATION"
    ):
        try:
            LOCATION = ast.literal_eval(
                node.value
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    LOCATION

        },

                "devkit.sim_engine.simulation_time_controller."
                "step_simulation_time_controller."
                "StepSimulationTimeController"
        },

                "agent_update_policy.box_tracks_observation."
                "BoxTracksObservation"

    }
)


def footprint_polygon(
    state,
):

    fp = state.car_footprint

    corners = fp.oriented_box.all_corners()

    pts = [
        (
            float(p.x),
            float(p.y),
        )
        for p in corners
    ]

    return Polygon(
        pts
    )


def run_candidate(
    level,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
):

    print()
    print(
        "SAFETY_LEVEL="
        + level
    )

    info = ScenarioFileBaseInfo(
        log_file_load_path=
            str(scenario_p),

        log_file_name=
            scenario_p.name,

        scenario_name=
            (
                "fullmine_v4_c11_"
                + level
                + "_initial_safety_v1"
            ),

        location=
            LOCATION,

        scenario_type=
            MinesimScenarioType.INTERSECTION_MIXED,

        data_root=
            str(DATA_ROOT),

        map_root=
            str(MAP_ROOT),
    )


    scenario = MineSimDynamicScenario(
        scenario_file=info
    )

    scenario.extract_tracked_objects_within_entrintime_window()


    setup = SimulationSetup(
        scenario=scenario,
        cfg=cfg,
    )


    objects = (
        scenario
        .get_tracked_objects_at_iteration(0)
        .tracked_objects
        .tracked_objects
    )


    B_obj = next(
        obj
        for obj in objects
        if tracked_object_token(obj)
        == "object-1"
    )


    B_initial = agent_to_ego_state(
        B_obj,
        scenario.get_time_point(0),
        vehicle,
    )


    b_controller = PerfectTrackingController(
        scenario
    )


    runtime = MultiEgoRuntime(
        [
            ControlledVehicleRuntime(
                "A",
                setup.ego_controller,
                scenario.initial_ego_state,
            ),
            ControlledVehicleRuntime(
                "B",
                b_controller,
                B_initial,
            ),
        ]
    )


    setup.set_multi_ego_runtime(
        runtime
    )


    A_poly = footprint_polygon(
        runtime.state_for("A")
    )

    B_poly = footprint_polygon(
        runtime.state_for("B")
    )


    overlap_area = float(
        A_poly.intersection(
            B_poly
        ).area
    )

    clearance = float(
        A_poly.distance(
            B_poly
        )
    )


    initial_overlap = (
        overlap_area > 1e-9
    )


    print(
        "INITIAL_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP="
        + repr(
            initial_overlap
        )
    )

    print(
        "INITIAL_OVERLAP_AREA_M2="
        + repr(
            overlap_area
        )
    )

    print(
        "INITIAL_FOOTPRINT_CLEARANCE_M="
        + repr(
            clearance
        )
    )


    no_overlap_gate = (
        not initial_overlap
    )


    print(
        "INITIAL_NO_OVERLAP_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if no_overlap_gate
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    simulation = EnvironmentSimulation(
        setup,
        simulation_history_buffer_duration=2.0,
    )


    print(
        "ENVIRONMENT_SIMULATION_CREATED=True"
    )


    simulation.initialize()


    print(
        "SIMULATION_INITIALIZED=True"
    )


    A_after = runtime.state_for("A")
    B_after = runtime.state_for("B")


    finite_after = all(
        math.isfinite(x)
        for x in (
            float(
                A_after.rear_axle.x
            ),
            float(
                A_after.rear_axle.y
            ),
            float(
                A_after.rear_axle.heading
            ),
            float(
                B_after.rear_axle.x
            ),
            float(
                B_after.rear_axle.y
            ),
            float(
                B_after.rear_axle.heading
            ),
        )
    )


    print(
        "POST_INITIALIZE_FINITE_STATE_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if finite_after
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    A_after_poly = footprint_polygon(
        A_after
    )

    B_after_poly = footprint_polygon(
        B_after
    )


    post_overlap_area = float(
        A_after_poly.intersection(
            B_after_poly
        ).area
    )

    post_clearance = float(
        A_after_poly.distance(
            B_after_poly
        )
    )


    post_overlap = (
        post_overlap_area > 1e-9
    )


    print(
        "POST_INITIALIZE_OVERLAP="
        + repr(
            post_overlap
        )
    )

    print(
        "POST_INITIALIZE_CLEARANCE_M="
        + repr(
            post_clearance
        )
    )


    post_no_overlap = (
        not post_overlap
    )


    print(
        "POST_INITIALIZE_NO_OVERLAP_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if post_no_overlap
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    # No propagate here.
    gate = all(
        (
            no_overlap_gate,
            finite_after,
            post_no_overlap,
        )
    )


    print(
        "INITIAL_SAFETY_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if gate
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    return {
        "scenario_sha256":
            sha(scenario_p),

        "manifest_sha256":
            sha(manifest_p),

        "initial_overlap":
            initial_overlap,

        "initial_overlap_area_m2":
            overlap_area,

        "initial_clearance_m":
            clearance,

        "post_initialize_finite_state_gate":
            finite_after,

        "post_initialize_overlap":
            post_overlap,

        "post_initialize_overlap_area_m2":
            post_overlap_area,

        "post_initialize_clearance_m":
            post_clearance,

        "initial_safety_gate":
            gate,
    }


results = {
    "60m":
        run_candidate(
            "60m",
            s60_p,
            m60_p,
        ),

    "100m":
        run_candidate(
            "100m",
            s100_p,
            m100_p,
        ),
}


all_pass = all(
    row[
        "initial_safety_gate"
    ]
    for row in results.values()
)


print()
print(
    "ALL_INITIAL_SAFETY_GATE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if all_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_60M_100M_INITIAL_SAFETY_GATE_V1",

    "status":
        (
            "PASS_INITIAL_SAFETY"
            if all_pass
            else "FAIL_INITIAL_SAFETY"
        ),

    "loader_smoke_sha256":
        sha(loader_p),

    "candidates":
        results,

    "important_boundary": {
        "EnvironmentSimulation_created":
            True,

        "simulation_initialized":
            True,

        "simulation_propagated":
            False,

        "MCTS_executed":
            False,

        "causal_conflict_test_executed":
            False,

        "collection_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,

        "MCTS_value_integration":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        (
            "PREREGISTER_60M_100M_CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE"
            if all_pass
            else
            "DIAGNOSE_INITIAL_SAFETY_ONLY"
        ),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print(
    "INITIAL_SAFETY_RESULT_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "INITIAL_SAFETY_RESULT_SHA256="
    + sha(out_p)
)

print(
    "SIMULATION_PROPAGATED=False"
)

print(
    "MCTS_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False"
)

print(
    "TRAINING_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "MODEL_FORWARD_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEXT="
    + payload[
        "next_stage"
    ]
)

    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import zipfile
import datetime

root = Path(sys.argv[1])
prereg_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
expected_sha = sys.argv[4]


def sha_bytes(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        data
    ).hexdigest()


def sha_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()

    with p.open("rb") as f:
        for block in iter(
            lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024),
            b"",
        ):
            h.update(block)

    return h.hexdigest()


prereg = json.loads(
    prereg_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    prereg["status"]
    == "FROZEN_BEFORE_CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTION"
)

assert (
    prereg["lineage"][
        "historical_C11_nomcts_baseline_source_sha256"
    ]
    == expected_sha
)

print("PREREG_SOURCE_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# Search only historically relevant MineSim areas.
# First regular source files, then ZIP members.
# ============================================================

search_roots = [
    root / "fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1",
    root / "90_MineSim_ARCHIVE",
    root / "paper1_neural_c11_c06_current_semantic_collector_v1",
]

regular_matches = []

checked_regular = 0

for base in search_roots:

    if not base.exists():
        continue

    for p in base.rglob("*"):

        if not p.is_file():
            continue

        if p.suffix.lower() not in {
            ".py",
            ".sh",
        }:
            continue

        checked_regular += 1

        try:
            digest = sha_file(p)
        except Exception:
            continue

        if digest == expected_sha:
            regular_matches.append(
                {
                    "path":
                        str(p),

                    "sha256":
                        digest,

                    "size_bytes":
                        p.stat().st_size,

                    "source_kind":
                        "REGULAR_FILE",
                }
            )


print(
    "REGULAR_SOURCE_FILES_CHECKED="
    + str(checked_regular)
)

print(
    "REGULAR_EXACT_MATCH_COUNT="
    + str(len(regular_matches))
)

for row in regular_matches:

    print(
        "REGULAR_EXACT_SOURCE="
        + row["path"]
    )

    print(
        "REGULAR_EXACT_SOURCE_SHA256="
        + row["sha256"]
    )


# ============================================================
# Search ZIP members only if useful.
# Keep scan to historically relevant archives.
# ============================================================

zip_matches = []

checked_zips = 0
checked_members = 0

for base in search_roots:

    if not base.exists():
        continue

    for zp in base.rglob("*.zip"):

        checked_zips += 1

        try:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(
                zp,
                "r",
            ) as z:

                for info in z.infolist():

                    name = info.filename

                    if not name.lower().endswith(
                        (
                            ".py",
                            ".sh",
                        )
                    ):
                        continue

                    # Avoid giant irrelevant members.
                    if info.file_size > 10_000_000:
                        continue

                    checked_members += 1

                    try:
                        raw = z.read(name)
                    except Exception:
                        continue

                    digest = sha_bytes(raw)

                    if digest == expected_sha:

                        zip_matches.append(
                            {
                                "zip_path":
                                    str(zp),

                                "member":
                                    name,

                                "member_sha256":
                                    digest,

                                "member_size_bytes":
                                    info.file_size,

                                "source_kind":
                                    "ZIP_MEMBER",
                            }
                        )

        except zipfile.BadZipFile:
            continue


print()
print(
    "ZIP_FILES_CHECKED="
    + str(checked_zips)
)

print(
    "ZIP_SOURCE_MEMBERS_CHECKED="
    + str(checked_members)
)

print(
    "ZIP_EXACT_MATCH_COUNT="
    + str(len(zip_matches))
)

for row in zip_matches:

    print(
        "ZIP_EXACT_SOURCE="
        + row["zip_path"]
        + "::"
        + row["member"]
    )

    print(
        "ZIP_EXACT_SOURCE_SHA256="
        + row["member_sha256"]
    )


all_matches = (
    regular_matches
    + zip_matches
)


# ============================================================
# Conservative binding decision
# Multiple copies with identical SHA are allowed:
# they represent byte-identical copies of one frozen source.
# ============================================================

if len(all_matches) >= 1:

    status = (
        "PASS_FROZEN_NOMCTS_SOURCE_BOUND"
    )

    classification = (
        "EXACT_SHA_MATCH_FOUND_FOR_HISTORICAL_C11_NOMCTS_BASELINE_SOURCE"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "PREPARE_CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTION_SOURCE"
    )

else:

    status = (
        "HOLD_FROZEN_NOMCTS_SOURCE_NOT_FOUND"
    )

    classification = (
        "EXPECTED_HISTORICAL_C11_NOMCTS_SOURCE_SHA_NOT_FOUND_IN_TARGETED_STORAGE"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_ARCHIVED_NOMCTS_SOURCE_LOCATION_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_NOMCTS_BASELINE_SOURCE_BINDING_V1",

    "status":
        status,

    "causal_conflict_preregistration_sha256":
        sha_file(prereg_p),

    "expected_source_sha256":
        expected_sha,

    "regular_exact_matches":
        regular_matches,

    "zip_exact_matches":
        zip_matches,

    "exact_match_count":
        len(all_matches),

    "binding_rule": {
        "byte_identical_duplicate_copies_allowed":
            True,

        "source_identity_defined_by_sha256":
            True,

        "source_modification_authorized":
            False,

        "candidate_specific_science_change_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "causal_conflict_smoke_executed":
            False,

        "simulation_propagated":
            False,

        "MCTS_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "NOMCTS_SOURCE_BINDING_STATUS="
    + status
)

print(
    "NOMCTS_SOURCE_BINDING_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "NOMCTS_EXACT_MATCH_COUNT="
    + str(len(all_matches))
)

print(
    "NOMCTS_SOURCE_BINDING_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "NOMCTS_SOURCE_BINDING_SHA256="
    + sha_file(out_p)
)

print(
    "CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "SIMULATION_PROPAGATED=False"
)

print(
    "MCTS_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False"
)

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

当前资源：低资源即可。

现在不是 NO-MCTS 证据失效，而是一个 provenance HOLD：我们拿 060880... 当作“源代码文件 SHA”去找，但 targeted storage 里 313 个 .py/.sh 和 280 个 ZIP source member 都没有这个字节级 SHA。说明下一步不能继续扩大盲搜范围，应该先确认：

060880... 在历史证据链里到底代表“脚本 SHA”、还是某个 baseline artifact / provenance SHA。

当前 smoke 绝对不要执行。

下一步只做 SHA 语义解析：搜索 060880... 这个字面值出现在哪里，并读取历史 C11 NO-MCTS result 中所有带 source / sha / provenance / script 的字段。

EXPECTED_HEAD=112d2bd0f3412fc83b13d5587d2b41402d2d0f5e
EXPECTED_BIND=e12ea283fb50735c9df2cad8f3b2cf59dd0c733817c4bbfdec8d7aefd52c944a

TARGET_SHA=060880c8edc851e05606871cb647058d091ce8737f952b9864cc94e9d6fcfd13

    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

echo "=== C11 NO-MCTS BASELINE SHA SEMANTICS RESOLUTION ==="

    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

    echo "FAIL BINDING_LOCK_MISSING"
    false
}

    echo "FAIL BINDING_LOCK_SHA"
    false
}

    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"
    false

echo "IMMUTABLE_INPUT_GATE=PASS"

python - \
"$ROOT" \
"$BIND" \
"$OUT" \
"$TARGET_SHA" <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import zipfile
import datetime

root = Path(sys.argv[1])
bind_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[3])
target = sys.argv[4].lower()


def sha_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()

    with p.open("rb") as f:
        for block in iter(
            lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024),
            b"",
        ):
            h.update(block)

    return h.hexdigest()


binding = json.loads(
    bind_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    binding["status"]
    == "HOLD_FROZEN_NOMCTS_SOURCE_NOT_FOUND"
)

print("PRIOR_BINDING_HOLD_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Literal TARGET_SHA references in historically relevant
#    textual artifacts.
#
# We are resolving what the SHA MEANS, not hashing more files.
# ============================================================

search_roots = [
    root / "fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1",
    root / "paper1_neural_c11_c06_current_semantic_collector_v1",
    root / "90_MineSim_ARCHIVE",
    root,
]

allowed_suffixes = {
    ".py",
    ".sh",
    ".json",
    ".jsonl",
    ".txt",
    ".log",
    ".md",
}

seen_files = set()
literal_hits = []

for base in search_roots:

    if not base.exists():
        continue

    for p in base.rglob("*"):

        if not p.is_file():
            continue

        try:
            rp = p.resolve()
        except Exception:
            rp = p

        if rp in seen_files:
            continue

        seen_files.add(rp)

        if p.suffix.lower() not in allowed_suffixes:
            continue

        try:
            size = p.stat().st_size
        except Exception:
            continue

        if size > 25_000_000:
            continue

        try:
            text = p.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8",
                errors="ignore",
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        if target not in text.lower():
            continue

        lines = text.splitlines()

        matches = []

        for i, line in enumerate(
            lines,
            1,
        ):
            if target in line.lower():
                matches.append(
                    {
                        "line":
                            i,

                        "text":
                            line.strip(),
                    }
                )

        literal_hits.append(
            {
                "path":
                    str(p),

                "file_sha256":
                    sha_file(p),

                "matches":
                    matches,
            }
        )


print(
    "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_FILE_HIT_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(literal_hits)
    )
)

for row in literal_hits:

    print()
    print(
        "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_FILE="
        + row["path"]
    )

    print(
        "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_FILE_SHA256="
        + row["file_sha256"]
    )

    for m in row["matches"]:
        print(
            "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_LINE="
            + repr(m)
        )


# ============================================================
# 2. Search ZIP text members for literal target SHA
# ============================================================

zip_hits = []

seen_zips = set()

for base in search_roots:

    if not base.exists():
        continue

    for zp in base.rglob("*.zip"):

        try:
            rz = zp.resolve()
        except Exception:
            rz = zp

        if rz in seen_zips:
            continue

        seen_zips.add(rz)

        try:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(
                zp,
                "r",
            ) as z:

                for info in z.infolist():

                    if not info.filename.lower().endswith(
                        (
                            ".py",
                            ".sh",
                            ".json",
                            ".txt",
                            ".log",
                            ".md",
                        )
                    ):
                        continue

                    if info.file_size > 10_000_000:
                        continue

                    try:
                        raw = z.read(
                            info.filename
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        continue

                    text = raw.decode(
                        "utf-8",
                        errors="ignore",
                    )

                    if target not in text.lower():
                        continue

                    matches = []

                    for i, line in enumerate(
                        text.splitlines(),
                        1,
                    ):
                        if target in line.lower():
                            matches.append(
                                {
                                    "line": i,
                                    "text":
                                        line.strip(),
                                }
                            )

                    zip_hits.append(
                        {
                            "zip_path":
                                str(zp),

                            "zip_sha256":
                                sha_file(zp),

                            "member":
                                info.filename,

                            "member_sha256":
                                hashlib.sha256(
                                    raw
                                ).hexdigest(),

                            "matches":
                                matches,
                        }
                    )

        except zipfile.BadZipFile:
            continue


print()
print(
    "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_ZIP_HIT_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(zip_hits)
    )
)

for row in zip_hits:

    print()
    print(
        "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_ZIP="
        + row["zip_path"]
        + "::"
        + row["member"]
    )

    print(
        "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_MEMBER_SHA256="
        + row["member_sha256"]
    )

    for m in row["matches"]:
        print(
            "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_ZIP_LINE="
            + repr(m)
        )


# ============================================================
# 3. Locate C11 NO-MCTS result JSONs by name/content
# ============================================================

nomcts_jsons = []

for p in root.rglob("*.json"):

    text_path = str(p).lower()

    if (
        "c11" not in text_path
        or "nomcts" not in text_path
    ):
        continue

    try:
        obj = json.loads(
            p.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8",
                errors="ignore",
            )
        )
    except Exception:
        continue

    nomcts_jsons.append(
        {
            "path":
                str(p),

            "sha256":
                sha_file(p),

            "object":
                obj,
        }
    )


print()
print(
    "C11_NOMCTS_JSON_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(nomcts_jsons)
    )
)


def flatten(
    obj,
    prefix="",
):

    rows = []

    if isinstance(
        obj,
        dict,
    ):

        for k, v in obj.items():

            path = (
                f"{prefix}.{k}"
                if prefix
                else str(k)
            )

            rows.extend(
                flatten(
                    v,
                    path,
                )
            )

    elif isinstance(
        obj,
        list,
    ):

        for i, v in enumerate(
            obj[:100]
        ):

            rows.extend(
                flatten(
                    v,
                    f"{prefix}[{i}]",
                )
            )

    else:
        rows.append(
            (
                prefix,
                obj,
            )
        )

    return rows


provenance_rows = []

keywords = (
    "sha",
    "source",
    "script",
    "runner",
    "path",
    "provenance",
    "baseline",
    "commit",
    "head",
    "version",
)


for rec in nomcts_jsons:

    obj = rec["object"]

    interesting = []

    for key, value in flatten(
        obj
    ):

        lk = key.lower()

        if not any(
            token in lk
            for token in keywords
        ):
            continue

        interesting.append(
            {
                "key":
                    key,

                "value":
                    value,
            }
        )

    provenance_rows.append(
        {
            "path":
                rec["path"],

            "sha256":
                rec["sha256"],

            "fields":
                interesting,
        }
    )

    print()
    print(
        "C11_NOMCTS_JSON="
        + rec["path"]
    )

    print(
        "C11_NOMCTS_JSON_SHA256="
        + rec["sha256"]
    )

    for row in interesting:

        print(
            "C11_NOMCTS_PROVENANCE_FIELD="
            + repr(row)
        )


# ============================================================
# 4. Classify semantics conservatively
# ============================================================

references = []

for row in literal_hits:

    for m in row["matches"]:

        references.append(
            {
                "container":
                    row["path"],

                "line":
                    m["line"],

                "text":
                    m["text"],
            }
        )


for row in zip_hits:

    for m in row["matches"]:

        references.append(
            {
                "container":
                    row["zip_path"]
                    + "::"
                    + row["member"],

                "line":
                    m["line"],

                "text":
                    m["text"],
            }
        )


semantic_labels = []

for r in references:

    text = r["text"].lower()

    if "source" in text:
        semantic_labels.append(
            "SOURCE"
        )

    if "baseline" in text:
        semantic_labels.append(
            "BASELINE"
        )

    if "result" in text:
        semantic_labels.append(
            "RESULT"
        )

    if "nomcts" in text:
        semantic_labels.append(
            "NOMCTS"
        )


if references:

    classification = (
        "TARGET_SHA_SEMANTICS_REFERENCES_FOUND"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "BIND_NOMCTS_EXECUTION_PROVENANCE_FROM_DISCOVERED_REFERENCE"
    )

else:

    classification = (
        "TARGET_SHA_LITERAL_REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "INSPECT_FROZEN_C11_NOMCTS_RESULT_PROVENANCE_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_NOMCTS_BASELINE_SHA_SEMANTICS_V1",

    "status":
        "PASS_READ_ONLY_SHA_SEMANTICS_RESOLUTION",

    "prior_binding_sha256":
        sha_file(
            bind_p
        ),

    "target_sha256":
        target,

    "literal_file_hits":
        literal_hits,

    "literal_zip_hits":
        zip_hits,

    "C11_nomcts_json_provenance":
        provenance_rows,

    "semantic_labels":
        sorted(
            set(
                semantic_labels
            )
        ),

    "classification":
        classification,

    "science_boundary": {
        "causal_conflict_smoke_executed":
            False,

        "simulation_propagated":
            False,

        "MCTS_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,
    },

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)


print()
print(
    "NOMCTS_SHA_SEMANTICS_CLASSIFICATION="
    + classification
)

print(
    "NOMCTS_SHA_SEMANTICS_WRITE=PASS"
)

print(
    "NOMCTS_SHA_SEMANTICS_SHA256="
    + sha_file(
        out_p
    )
)

print(
    "CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "SIMULATION_PROPAGATED=False"
)

print(
    "MCTS_EXECUTED=False"
)

print(
    "NEW_COLLECTION_AUTHORIZED=False"
)

print(
    "NEXT="
    + next_stage
)

文档

思考了 5s
当前资源：低资源即可。

现在已经把 060880... 的语义纠正清楚了：它不是 NO-MCTS runner 的源码 SHA，而是 Polygon21 冻结 NO-MCTS baseline result artifact 的 SHA。最直接的证据是 release 的 SHA256SUMS.txt 明确把它对应到 source_evidence/polygon21_rank1_nomcts_conflict_baseline_result_v1.json。


另外，C11 自己的历史 NO-MCTS result 是另一套 artifact：有两份候选结果 SHA 19082f... 与 c5bd45...，都标记 baseline_conflict_confirmed=True，且绑定同一个 C11 scenario/manifest。


所以接下来不能再拿 060880... 去约束 C11 runner。正确做法是：

保留 060880... 作为“历史 NO-MCTS 方法/基线 lineage”证据，同时从 C11 历史 result + 现有可恢复 NO-MCTS source 中绑定真正的 C11 execution provenance。

现在只做这个绑定，不执行 smoke。

# Current semantic historical NO-MCTS runner candidates.
# Resolve exact C11-capable source by content, not by guessed SHA.
SEARCH_ROOT="$ROOT/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1"

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

echo "=== C11 NO-MCTS EXECUTION PROVENANCE BINDING ==="

[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_HEAD" ] || {
    echo "FAIL HEAD"
    false
}

TRACKED="$(git status --porcelain=v1 | grep -v '^?? ' || true)"

[ -z "$TRACKED" ] || {
    echo "FAIL REPO_DIRTY"
    printf '%s\n' "$TRACKED"
    false
}

echo "REPO_PREFLIGHT=PASS"

check_sha "$SEMANTICS" "$EXPECTED_SEMANTICS" SHA_SEMANTICS
check_sha "$PREREG" "$EXPECTED_PREREG" CAUSAL_PREREG

check_sha "$C11_RESULT_V1" "$EXPECTED_C11_RESULT_V1_SHA" C11_RESULT_V1
check_sha "$C11_RESULT_V2" "$EXPECTED_C11_RESULT_V2_SHA" C11_RESULT_V2

[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || {
    echo "FAIL OUTPUT_ALREADY_EXISTS=$OUT"

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import re

(
    semantics_p,
    prereg_p,
    result_v1_p,
    result_v2_p,
    search_root,
    out_p,
    polygon21_baseline_sha,
) = sys.argv[1:]

semantics_p = Path(semantics_p)
prereg_p = Path(prereg_p)
result_v1_p = Path(result_v1_p)
result_v2_p = Path(result_v2_p)
search_root = Path(search_root)
out_p = Path(out_p)


def sha_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()

    with p.open("rb") as f:
        for block in iter(
            lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024),
            b"",
        ):
            h.update(block)

    return h.hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


semantics = load(
    semantics_p
)

prereg = load(
    prereg_p
)

r1 = load(
    result_v1_p
)

r2 = load(
    result_v2_p
)


assert (
    semantics["status"]
    == "PASS_READ_ONLY_SHA_SEMANTICS_RESOLUTION"
)

assert (
    prereg["status"]
    == "FROZEN_BEFORE_CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTION"
)

print("INPUT_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Correct semantics of 060880...
# ============================================================

polygon21_result_semantics = (
    "FROZEN_POLYGON21_NOMCTS_BASELINE_RESULT_ARTIFACT"
)

print(
    "POLYGON21_BASELINE_SHA_SEMANTICS="
    + polygon21_result_semantics
)

    "POLYGON21_BASELINE_RESULT_SHA256="
    + polygon21_baseline_sha
)


# ============================================================
# 2. Freeze historical C11 result evidence
# ============================================================

for label, obj, path in (
    (
        "V1",
        r1,
        result_v1_p,
    ),
    (
        "V2",
        r2,
        result_v2_p,
    ),
):

    print()
    print(
        f"C11_RESULT_{label}_PATH="
        + str(path)
    )

    print(
        f"C11_RESULT_{label}_SHA256="
        + sha_file(path)
    )

    print(
        f"C11_RESULT_{label}_BASELINE_CONFLICT_CONFIRMED="
        + repr(
            obj.get(
                "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        f"C11_RESULT_{label}_SCENARIO_SHA256="
        + repr(
            obj.get(
                "scenario_sha256"
            )
        )
    )

    print(
        f"C11_RESULT_{label}_MANIFEST_SHA256="
        + repr(
            obj.get(
                "manifest_sha256"
            )
        )
    )


assert (
    r1.get(
        "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
    )
    is True
)

assert (
    r2.get(
        "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
    )
    is True
)


# ============================================================
# 3. Find actual C11 NO-MCTS source by exact execution contract
#
# Required signatures:
# - NO MCTS constant speed baseline
# - TARGET_SPEED = 4.5
# - DT = 0.1
# - constant_speed_trajectory
# - cross-scene-c%02d-nomcts-causal-conflict-v2.json
# - C11 source_index support
# ============================================================

candidates = []

for p in search_root.rglob("*.py"):

    if not p.is_file():
        continue

    try:
        text = p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="ignore",
        )
    except Exception:
        continue

    signatures = {
        "constant_speed_trajectory":
            "constant_speed_trajectory" in text,

        "nomcts_output_name":
            "nomcts-causal-conflict-v2.json"
            in text,

        "target_speed_4_5":
            bool(
                re.search(
                    r"TARGET_SPEED\s*=\s*4\.5\b",
                    text,
                )
            ),

        "dt_0_1":
            bool(
                re.search(
                    r"\bDT\s*=\s*0\.1\b",
                    text,
                )
            ),

        "source_index_11":
            (
                "11:" in text
                or '"source_index": 11'
                in text
            ),

        "no_mcts_phrase":
            (
                "No MCTS" in text
                or "NO_MCTS" in text
                or "nomcts" in text.lower()
            ),
    }

    score = sum(
        bool(v)
        for v in signatures.values()
    )

    if score < 5:
        continue

    candidates.append(
        {
            "path":
                str(p),

            "sha256":
                sha_file(p),

            "signatures":
                signatures,

            "score":
                score,
        }
    )


candidates.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x["score"],
        x["path"],
    ),
    reverse=True,
)


print()
print(
    "C11_NOMCTS_SOURCE_CANDIDATE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(candidates)
    )
)

for i, row in enumerate(
    candidates[:20],
    1,
):

    print(
        "C11_NOMCTS_SOURCE_CANDIDATE="
        + repr(
            {
                "rank":
                    i,

                "path":
                    row[
                        "path"
                    ],

                "sha256":
                    row[
                        "sha256"
                    ],

                "score":
                    row[
                        "score"
                    ],

                "signatures":
                    row[
                        "signatures"
                    ],
            }
        )
    )


exact_sources = [
    row
    for row in candidates
    if all(
        row[
            "signatures"
        ].values()
    )
]


print()
print(
    "C11_NOMCTS_EXACT_SOURCE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(
            exact_sources
        )
    )
)

for row in exact_sources:

    print(
        "C11_NOMCTS_EXACT_SOURCE="
        + row["path"]
    )

    print(
        "C11_NOMCTS_EXACT_SOURCE_SHA256="
        + row["sha256"]
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Conservative binding
# ============================================================

if len(
    exact_sources
) >= 1:

    status = (
        "PASS_C11_NOMCTS_EXECUTION_PROVENANCE_BOUND"
    )

    classification = (
        "POLYGON21_BASELINE_RESULT_LINEAGE_PLUS_C11_EXECUTABLE_NOMCTS_SOURCE_BOUND"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "PREPARE_60M_100M_CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTION_SOURCE"
    )

else:

    status = (
        "HOLD_C11_NOMCTS_EXECUTABLE_SOURCE_NOT_FOUND"
    )

    classification = (
        "C11_HISTORICAL_RESULT_VALID_BUT_EXECUTION_SOURCE_NOT_YET_BOUND"
    )

    next_stage = (
        "RESOLVE_C11_NOMCTS_RUNNER_LOCATION_ONLY"
    )


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_NOMCTS_EXECUTION_PROVENANCE_BINDING_V1",

    "status":
        status,

    "sha_semantics_sha256":
        sha_file(
            semantics_p
        ),

    "causal_conflict_preregistration_sha256":
        sha_file(
            prereg_p
        ),

    "polygon21_baseline_lineage": {
        "sha256":
            polygon21_baseline_sha,

        "semantics":
            polygon21_result_semantics,

        "treated_as_source_code_sha":
            False,
    },

    "historical_C11_results": [
        {
            "path":
                str(
                    result_v1_p
                ),

            "sha256":
                sha_file(
                    result_v1_p
                ),

            "baseline_conflict_confirmed":
                r1.get(
                    "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
                ),

            "scenario_sha256":
                r1.get(
                    "scenario_sha256"
                ),

            "manifest_sha256":
                r1.get(
                    "manifest_sha256"
                ),
        },
        {
            "path":
                str(
                    result_v2_p
                ),

            "sha256":
                sha_file(
                    result_v2_p
                ),

            "baseline_conflict_confirmed":
                r2.get(
                    "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
                ),

            "scenario_sha256":
                r2.get(
                    "scenario_sha256"
                ),

            "manifest_sha256":
                r2.get(
                    "manifest_sha256"
                ),
        },
    ],

    "execution_source_candidates":
        candidates,

    "exact_execution_sources":
        exact_sources,

    "binding_rule": {
        "Polygon21_result_SHA_is_lineage_not_runner_identity":
            True,

        "C11_execution_source_identified_by_code_contract":
            True,

        "source_modification_authorized":
            False,

        "candidate_specific_tuning_authorized":
            False,
    },

    "science_boundary": {
        "causal_conflict_smoke_executed":
            False,

        "simulation_propagated":
            False,

        "MCTS_executed":
            False,

        "new_collection_executed":
            False,

        "training_executed":
            False,

        "model_forward_executed":
            False,

        "V3_design_changed":
            False,

        "C03_used":
            False,

        "C13_used":
            False,
    },

    "classification":
        classification,

    "next_stage":
        next_stage,

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,

    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",

print()
print(
    "C11_NOMCTS_PROVENANCE_BINDING_STATUS="
    + status
)

print(

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import datetime
import re
import ast

prov_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
runner_p = Path(sys.argv[2])
result_dir = Path(sys.argv[3])
result_p = Path(sys.argv[4])
out_p = Path(sys.argv[5])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


prov = json.loads(
    prov_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

result = json.loads(
    result_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )
)

assert (
    prov["status"]
    == "PASS_C11_NOMCTS_EXECUTION_PROVENANCE_BOUND"
)

assert result.get(
    "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
) is True

print("PRIOR_PROVENANCE_GATE=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 1. Exact runner contract
# ============================================================

source = runner_p.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8"
)

required_literals = {
    "TARGET_SPEED_4_5":
        r"TARGET_SPEED\s*=\s*4\.5\b",

    "DT_0_1":
        r"\bDT\s*=\s*0\.1\b",

    "CONSTANT_SPEED_TRAJECTORY":
        r"def\s+constant_speed_trajectory",

    "NO_MCTS_COMMENT":
        r"No MCTS|NO_MCTS|nomcts",

    "RESULT_FILENAME":
        r"nomcts-causal-conflict-v2\.json",

    "SOURCE_INDEX_11":
        r'"source_index"\s*:\s*11|11\s*:\s*\{',

    "BASELINE_CONFLICT_CONFIRMED":
        r"baseline_conflict_confirmed",

    "PHYSICAL_OVERLAP":
        r"physical_overlap",

    "TIMELINE_SYNC":
        r"timeline_sync",
}


contract = {}

for name, pattern in required_literals.items():

    ok = bool(
        re.search(
            pattern,
            source,
            flags=re.I,
        )
    )

    contract[name] = ok

    print(
        f"{name}="
        + repr(ok)
    )


assert all(
    contract.values()
)

print("C11_HORIZON_FIX_RUNNER_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 2. Result contract
# ============================================================

result_checks = {
    "baseline_conflict_confirmed":
        result.get(
            "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
        )
        is True,

    "physical_overlap":
        result.get(
            "physical_overlap"
        )
        is True,

    "completed_post_conflict":
        result.get(
            "completed_post_conflict"
        )
        is True,

    "timeline_sync":
        result.get(
            "timeline_sync"
        )
        is True,

    "nan_or_invalid":
        result.get(
            "nan_or_invalid"
        )
        is False,

    "scenario_sha":
        result.get(
            "scenario_sha256"
        )
        ==
        "1127aeb78308b90a9d59127f9443fea4acd8b98e136cbf86d1201f7593978cc9",

    "manifest_sha":
        result.get(
            "manifest_sha256"
        )
        ==
        "7bc4a6e327557bb038cd5da32c8b4af76976f470f2e8369201248a0cec7cfe12",
}


for k, v in result_checks.items():
    print(
        "RESULT_CONTRACT_"
        + k.upper()
        + "="
        + repr(v)
    )


assert all(
    result_checks.values()
)

print("FINAL_C11_RESULT_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# 3. Directory-local execution evidence
# ============================================================

local_files = sorted(
    p
    for p in result_dir.rglob("*")
    if p.is_file()
)


print(
    "RESULT_DIR_FILE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(local_files)
    )
)


local_evidence = []

runner_name = runner_p.name

for p in local_files:

    if p == result_p:
        continue

    if p.stat().st_size > 20_000_000:
        continue

    try:
        text = p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="ignore",
        )
    except Exception:
        continue

    signals = []

    if runner_name in text:
        signals.append(
            "RUNNER_FILENAME"
        )

    if sha(runner_p) in text:
        signals.append(
            "RUNNER_SHA"
        )

    if "c11_nomcts_horizon_fix" in text.lower():
        signals.append(
            "HORIZON_FIX_NAME"
        )

    if "cross-scene-c11-nomcts-causal-conflict-v2" in text:
        signals.append(
            "RESULT_FILENAME"
        )

    if signals:
        local_evidence.append(
            {
                "path":
                    str(p),

                "sha256":
                    sha(p),

                "signals":
                    signals,
            }
        )


print(
    "DIRECT_LOCAL_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_COUNT="
    + str(
        len(local_evidence)
    )
)

for row in local_evidence:
    print(
        "DIRECT_LOCAL_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE="
        + repr(row)
    )


# ============================================================
# 4. Compare generic v2/v3 vs horizon-fix source
#    only to establish why horizon-fix is chosen.
# ============================================================

generic_paths = [
    Path(
        "/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/"
        "cross_scene_nomcts_causal_conflict_v2.py"
    ),
    Path(
        "/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/"
        "cross_scene_nomcts_causal_conflict_v3.py"
    ),
]


generic = []

for p in generic_paths:

    if p.is_file():
        generic.append(
            {
                "path":
                    str(p),

                "sha256":
                    sha(p),

                "same_bytes_as_horizon_fix":
                    p.read_bytes()
                    == runner_p.read_bytes(),
            }
        )


for row in generic:
    print(
        "GENERIC_SOURCE_COMPARISON="
        + repr(row)
    )


# ============================================================
# 5. Binding decision
#
# Even if no shell log directly names the runner, the
# result directory is horizon-fix-specific and the runner
# is the only C11-specific horizon-fix source adjacent to it.
# We distinguish DIRECT vs DIRECTORY_AND_CONTRACT lineage.
# ============================================================

if local_evidence:

    binding_strength = (
        "DIRECT_LOCAL_EXECUTION_LINEAGE"
    )

else:

    binding_strength = (
        "C11_HORIZON_FIX_DIRECTORY_AND_EXECUTION_CONTRACT_LINEAGE"
    )


status = (
    "PASS_FINAL_C11_NOMCTS_PARENT_SOURCE_BOUND"
)

classification = (
    "C11_HORIZON_FIX_RUNNER_SELECTED_AS_PARENT_FOR_CANDIDATE_SMOKE"
)


payload = {
    "type":
        "PAPER1_VALUE_V3_C11_FINAL_NOMCTS_PARENT_SOURCE_BINDING_V1",

    "status":
        status,

    "prior_provenance_binding_sha256":
        sha(
            prov_p
        ),

    "parent_source": {
        "path":
            str(
                runner_p
            ),

        "sha256":
            sha(
                runner_p
            ),

        "selection_basis": [
            "C11_specific",

                result_p

            True,

            True,

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}
