from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import re
import ast
import datetime


(
    parent_bind_p,
    parent_p,
    s60_p,
    m60_p,
    s100_p,
    m100_p,
    outdir,
    summary_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


binding = load(
    parent_bind_p
)

assert (
    binding["status"]
    == "PASS_FINAL_C11_NOMCTS_PARENT_SOURCE_BOUND"
)

assert (
    binding["parent_source"]["sha256"]
    == sha(parent_p)
)

print("FINAL_PARENT_BINDING_GATE=PASS")


parent = parent_p.read_text(
    encoding="utf-8"
)


# ============================================================
# Parent science contract
# ============================================================

required_parent_contract = {
    "DT_0_1":
        bool(
            re.search(
                r"\bDT\s*=\s*0\.1\b",
                parent,
            )
        ),

    "TARGET_SPEED_4_5":
        bool(
            re.search(
                r"\bTARGET_SPEED\s*=\s*4\.5\b",
                parent,
            )
        ),

    "CONSTANT_SPEED_TRAJECTORY":
        "def constant_speed_trajectory" in parent,

    "NO_MCTS":
        (
            "No MCTS" in parent
            or "NO_MCTS" in parent
            or "nomcts" in parent.lower()
        ),

    "BASELINE_CONFLICT_CONFIRMED":
        "baseline_conflict_confirmed" in parent,

    "PHYSICAL_OVERLAP":
        "physical_overlap" in parent,

    "TIMELINE_SYNC":
        "timeline_sync" in parent,

    "COMPLETED_POST_CONFLICT":
        "completed_post_conflict" in parent,
}


for k, v in required_parent_contract.items():

    print(
        "PARENT_CONTRACT_"
        + k
        + "="
        + repr(v)
    )


assert all(
    required_parent_contract.values()
)

print("PARENT_SCIENCE_CONTRACT=PASS")


# ============================================================
# Frozen source C11 literals
# ============================================================

OLD_SCENARIO = (
    "/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/"
    "scenarios_v1/"
    "Scenario-fullmine-v4-cross-scene-c11-dual-aligned-v1.json"
)

OLD_SCENARIO_SHA = (
    "1127aeb78308b90a9d59127f9443fea4"
    "acd8b98e136cbf86d1201f7593978cc9"
)

OLD_MANIFEST = (
    "/root/autodl-tmp/fullmine_v4_fleet_cross_scene_v1/"
    "scenarios_v1/"
    "cross_scene_c11_scenario_manifest_v1.json"
)

OLD_MANIFEST_SHA = (
    "7bc4a6e327557bb038cd5da32c8b4af"
    "76976f470f2e8369201248a0cec7cfe12"
)


for name, literal in (
    (
        "OLD_SCENARIO",
        OLD_SCENARIO,
    ),
    (
        "OLD_SCENARIO_SHA",
        OLD_SCENARIO_SHA,
    ),
    (
        "OLD_MANIFEST",
        OLD_MANIFEST,
    ),
    (
        "OLD_MANIFEST_SHA",
        OLD_MANIFEST_SHA,
    ),
):

    count = parent.count(
        literal
    )

    print(
        f"PARENT_{name}_COUNT="
        + str(count)
    )

    assert count >= 1


# ============================================================
# Identify C11 approach literal structurally
# ============================================================

tree = ast.parse(
    parent
)


def find_c11_approach():

    for node in ast.walk(
        tree
    ):

        if not isinstance(
            node,
            ast.Assign,
        ):
            continue

        for target in node.targets:

            if not (
                isinstance(
                    target,
                    ast.Name,
                )
                and target.id
                == "EXPECTED_GEOMETRY"
            ):
                continue

            obj = ast.literal_eval(
                node.value
            )

            assert 11 in obj

            return float(
                obj[11][
                    "approach_m"
                ]
            )

    raise RuntimeError(
        "EXPECTED_GEOMETRY_C11_NOT_FOUND"
    )


parent_approach = (
    find_c11_approach()
)

print(
    "PARENT_C11_APPROACH_M="
    + repr(
        parent_approach
    )
)

assert abs(
    parent_approach - 80.0
) <= 1e-12


# ============================================================
# Output root statement
# ============================================================

output_match = re.search(
    r'OUTPUT_ROOT\s*=\s*Path\(\s*os\.environ\.get\(\s*'
    r'"OUTPUT_ROOT"\s*,\s*"([^"]+)"\s*\)\s*\)',
    parent,
    flags=re.S,
)

assert output_match is not None

OLD_OUTPUT_ROOT = (
    output_match.group(1)
)

print(
    "PARENT_DEFAULT_OUTPUT_ROOT="
    + OLD_OUTPUT_ROOT
)


# ============================================================
# Exact C11 EXPECTED_GEOMETRY block edit
#
# Only approach_m for key 11 is changed.
# ============================================================

def replace_c11_approach(
    text,
    distance,
):

    pattern = (
        r'(11\s*:\s*\{'
        r'.*?'
        r'["\']approach_m["\']\s*:\s*)'
        r'80\.0'
    )

    updated, n = re.subn(
        pattern,
        lambda m:
            m.group(1)
            + f"{distance:.1f}",
        text,
        count=1,
        flags=re.S,
    )

    assert n == 1

    return updated


def replace_exact(
    text,
    old,
    new,
    label,
):

    count = text.count(
        old
    )

    print(
        f"{label}_REPLACEMENT_COUNT="
        + str(count)
    )

    assert count >= 1

    return text.replace(
        old,
        new
    )


def reverse_candidate(
    text,
    *,
    new_scenario,
    new_scenario_sha,
    new_manifest,
    new_manifest_sha,
    distance,
    new_output_root,
):

    x = text

    x = x.replace(
        new_scenario,
        OLD_SCENARIO,
    )

    x = x.replace(
        new_scenario_sha,
        OLD_SCENARIO_SHA,
    )

    x = x.replace(
        new_manifest,
        OLD_MANIFEST,
    )

    x = x.replace(
        new_manifest_sha,
        OLD_MANIFEST_SHA,
    )

    x = x.replace(
        new_output_root,
        OLD_OUTPUT_ROOT,
    )

    pattern = (
        r'(11\s*:\s*\{'
        r'.*?'
        r'["\']approach_m["\']\s*:\s*)'
        + re.escape(
            f"{distance:.1f}"
        )
    )

    x, n = re.subn(
        pattern,
        lambda m:
            m.group(1)
            + "80.0",
        x,
        count=1,
        flags=re.S,
    )

    assert n == 1

    return x


def generate(
    level,
    distance,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
):

    scenario_sha = sha(
        scenario_p
    )

    manifest_sha = sha(
        manifest_p
    )

    new_output_root = str(
        outdir
        / (
            "run_"
            + level
        )
    )

    runner_name = (
        "cross_scene_nomcts_"
        "causal_conflict_c11_"
        + level
        + "_value_v3_smoke_v1.py"
    )

    runner_p = (
        outdir
        / runner_name
    )


    text = parent


    text = replace_exact(
        text,
        OLD_SCENARIO,
        str(
            scenario_p
        ),
        level
        + "_SCENARIO",
    )

    text = replace_exact(
        text,
        OLD_SCENARIO_SHA,
        scenario_sha,
        level
        + "_SCENARIO_SHA",
    )

    text = replace_exact(
        text,
        OLD_MANIFEST,
        str(
            manifest_p
        ),
        level
        + "_MANIFEST",
    )

    text = replace_exact(
        text,
        OLD_MANIFEST_SHA,
        manifest_sha,
        level
        + "_MANIFEST_SHA",
    )


    text = replace_c11_approach(
        text,
        distance,
    )


    # Only change the default OUTPUT_ROOT literal.
    old_output_statement = (
        output_match.group(0)
    )

    new_output_statement = (
        old_output_statement.replace(
            OLD_OUTPUT_ROOT,
            new_output_root,
        )
    )

    count_output_stmt = text.count(
        old_output_statement
    )

    print(
        level
        + "_OUTPUT_ROOT_REPLACEMENT_COUNT="
        + str(
            count_output_stmt
        )
    )

    assert count_output_stmt == 1

    text = text.replace(
        old_output_statement,
        new_output_statement,
        1,
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Static science invariants
    # ========================================================

    assert re.search(
        r"\bDT\s*=\s*0\.1\b",
        text,
    )

    assert re.search(
        r"\bTARGET_SPEED\s*=\s*4\.5\b",
        text,
    )

    assert (
        "def constant_speed_trajectory"
        in text
    )

    assert (
        "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
        in text
    )

    assert (
        "physical_overlap"
        in text
    )

    assert (
        "timeline_sync"
        in text
    )

    assert (
        "completed_post_conflict"
        in text
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Strongest static equivalence gate:
    # reverse all explicitly authorized candidate substitutions.
    # Result MUST be byte-for-byte identical to parent text.
    # ========================================================

    reversed_text = reverse_candidate(
        text,
        new_scenario=
            str(
                scenario_p
            ),

        new_scenario_sha=
            scenario_sha,

        new_manifest=
            str(
                manifest_p
            ),

        new_manifest_sha=
            manifest_sha,

        distance=
            distance,

        new_output_root=
            new_output_root,
    )


    reverse_exact = (
        reversed_text
        == parent
    )


    print()
    print(
        "RUNNER_LEVEL="
        + level
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_REVERSE_TO_PARENT_EXACT="
        + repr(
            reverse_exact
        )
    )

    assert reverse_exact


    # ========================================================
    # Parse generated source
    # ========================================================

    ast.parse(
        text
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_AST_PARSE_GATE=PASS"
    )


    runner_p.write_text(
        text,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


    runner_sha = sha(
        runner_p
    )


    print(
        "RUNNER_FILE="
        + str(
            runner_p
        )
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_SHA256="
        + runner_sha
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_SCENARIO="
        + str(
            scenario_p
        )
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_SCENARIO_SHA256="
        + scenario_sha
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_MANIFEST="
        + str(
            manifest_p
        )
    )

    print(
        "RUNNER_MANIFEST_SHA256="

sha() {
    sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
}

check_sha() {
    P="$1"
    E="$2"
    N="$3"

    [ -f "$P" ] || {
        echo "FAIL MISSING_$N=$P"
        false
    }

    A="$(sha "$P")"
    echo "${N}_SHA=$A"

    [ "$A" = "$E" ] || {
        echo "FAIL ${N}_SHA"
        false
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import sys
import json
import hashlib
import ast
import re
import datetime

(
    prereg_p,
    parent_bind_p,
    gen_p,
    r60_p,
    r100_p,
    s60_p,
    m60_p,
    s100_p,
    m100_p,
    out_p,
) = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])


def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(
        p.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()


def load(p):
    return json.loads(
        p.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )


prereg = load(prereg_p)
parent_bind = load(parent_bind_p)
generation = load(gen_p)

assert (
    prereg["status"]
    == "FROZEN_BEFORE_CAUSAL_CONFLICT_SMOKE_EXECUTION"
)

assert (
    parent_bind["status"]
    == "PASS_FINAL_C11_NOMCTS_PARENT_SOURCE_BOUND"
)

assert (
    generation["status"]
    == "PASS_STATIC_RUNNER_GENERATION"
)

print("UPSTREAM_ARTIFACT_GATE=PASS")


def validate_runner(
    level,
    runner_p,
    scenario_p,
    manifest_p,
):

    text = runner_p.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    ast.parse(text)

    manifest = load(
        manifest_p
    )

    distance = float(
        manifest[
            "approach_distance_m"
        ]
    )

    expected_distance = (
        60.0
        if level == "60m"
        else 100.0
    )

    checks = {
        "ast_parse":
            True,

        "scenario_path":
            str(scenario_p) in text,

        "scenario_sha":
            sha(scenario_p) in text,

        "manifest_path":
            str(manifest_p) in text,

        "manifest_sha":
            sha(manifest_p) in text,

        "approach_distance":
            abs(
                distance
                - expected_distance
            ) <= 1e-12,

        "DT_0_1":
            bool(
                re.search(
                    r"\bDT\s*=\s*0\.1\b",
                    text,
                )
            ),

        "TARGET_SPEED_4_5":
            bool(
                re.search(
                    r"\bTARGET_SPEED\s*=\s*4\.5\b",
                    text,
                )
            ),

        "constant_speed":
            "def constant_speed_trajectory"
            in text,

        "baseline_conflict_confirmed":
            "baseline_conflict_confirmed"
            in text,

        "physical_overlap":
            "physical_overlap"
            in text,

        "timeline_sync":
            "timeline_sync"
            in text,

        "completed_post_conflict":
            "completed_post_conflict"
            in text,

        "no_MCTS_execution":
            (
                "FleetMCTSSearch("
                not in text
            ),
    }


    # Important nuance:
    # imports from shared modules are allowed;
    # actual FleetMCTSSearch invocation is not.
    all_pass = all(
        checks.values()
    )


    print()
    print(
        "AUTHORIZE_LEVEL="
        + level
    )

    for k, v in checks.items():
        print(
            "CHECK_"
            + k.upper()
            + "="
            + repr(v)
        )

    print(
        "RUNNER_AUTHORIZATION_GATE="
        + (
            "PASS"
            if all_pass
            else "FAIL"
        )
    )


    return {
        "runner":
            str(runner_p),

        "runner_sha256":
            sha(runner_p),

        "scenario":
            str(scenario_p),

        "scenario_sha256":
            sha(scenario_p),

        "manifest":
            str(manifest_p),

        "manifest_sha256":
            sha(manifest_p),

        "distance_m":
            expected_distance,

        "checks":
            checks,

        "runner_authorization_gate":
            all_pass,
    }


results = {
    "60m":
        validate_runner(
            "60m",
            r60_p,
            s60_p,
            m60_p,
        ),

    "100m":
        validate_runner(
            "100m",
            r100_p,
            s100_p,
            m100_p,
        ),
}


all_pass = all(
    row[
        "runner_authorization_gate"
    ]
    for row in results.values()
)


print()
print(
    "ALL_EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION_GATE="
    + (
        "PASS"
        if all_pass
        else "FAIL"
    )
)

            False,

        "rerun_on_technical_failure":
            (
                "ONLY_AFTER_EXPLICIT_DIAGNOSIS_AND_NEW_TECHNICAL_AUTHORIZATION"
            ),
    },

    "qualification_rule": {
        "physical_overlap_or_collision_required":
            True,

        "baseline_conflict_confirmed_required":
            True,

        "timeline_sync_required":
            True,

        "completed_post_conflict_required":
            True,

            False,

        "60m_pass":
            "QUALIFIED_FOR_DEVELOPMENT_COLLECTION",

        "100m_pass":
            "QUALIFIED_FOR_DEVELOPMENT_COLLECTION",

        "candidate_failure":
            "REJECT_THAT_CANDIDATE_WITHOUT_TUNING",
    },

    "important_boundary": {
        "authorization_created":

            False,

            else
            "DIAGNOSE_EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION_ONLY"
        ),

    "created_at_utc":
        datetime.datetime.now(
            datetime.timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
}


out_p.write_text(
    json.dumps(
        payload,
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
        allow_nan=False,
    )
    + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",

print(
    "EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION_WRITE=PASS"

        all_pass
    )
